### [1] [Quasi-hostless LRDs with intrinsically red, faint UV continua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28209)
*Callum Witten,Andrea Weibel,Pascal A. Oesch,Rohan P. Naidu,Jorryt Matthee,Alba Covelo-Paz,Romain A. Meyer,Albert Sneppen,Parker Fairfield,Anna de Graaff,Christina C. Williams,Zhiyuan Ji,Pratika Dayal,Yoshinobu Fudamoto,Garth Illingworth,Cristian Carvajal-Bohorquez*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文从132个高红移小红点（LRDs）样本中，筛选出3个具有极弱紫外（FUV）与光学连续谱比（L1500/L5100 < 0.03）的新型类黑洞星（BH*-like）天体，其中两个位于z > 6，一个在z ∼ 3；发现其紫外谱斜率β_UV随FUV/Optical比下降而显著变红（从−1.8升至−0.5），表明中心引擎主导、气体包络遮蔽、准无宿主星系特征，并提出中性氢（n=2）高柱密度屏+黑体+星云辐射模型可解释该红化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对黑洞星（BH*）的识别仅基于光学波段模板主导，忽略紫外波段信息；本文旨在结合紫外与光学连续谱比，系统识别真正由中心引擎主导、紫外极度微弱的纯BH*候选体，拓展高红移类星体演化认知边界。

Method: 基于132个红移2≲z≲9的光谱小红点（LRDs）样本，设定FUV（1500Å）与光学（5100Å）连续谱光度比L1500/L5100 < 0.03为选择标准，筛选出类MoM-BH*和The Cliff特征的源；结合紫外谱斜率β_UV分析、Balmer线强比、禁线强度及辐射转移建模（黑体+氢星云+中性氢n=2屏），探讨红化物理机制。

Result: 新确认3个BH*-like LRDs（2个z>6，1个z∼3），使此类样本数量翻倍以上；发现β_UV随L1500/L5100降低而显著变红（达−0.5），远超同红移典型值；观测到极端巴耳末减幅、弱禁线、准无宿主特征；提出N_H,n=2≈10^17 cm⁻²中性氢屏模型可复现红化，并预测宿主星系FUV贡献增强将导致可检验的紫外形态演化。

Conclusion: 本文确立了一类具有共同本征红紫外连续谱的准无宿主小红点（quasi-hostless LRDs），其红化起源于中心引擎（而非尘埃或恒星），支持高红移黑洞早期‘裸露’吸积相的存在；所提物理模型为区分中心引擎主导与宿主星系主导紫外辐射提供了新诊断工具。

Abstract: High-redshift objects characterised by very weak UV relative to their strong, red rest-optical continua, with broad Balmer lines, appear to represent little red dots (LRDs) in their purest form: sources dominated by emission from their central engine (CE), sometimes dubbed black hole stars (BH*s). Previous work has selected BH*-dominated objects, where a BH* template dominates the rest-optical emission; this remains agnostic to the rest-UV. We therefore select low FUV-to-optical continuum ratio ($L_{\rm 1500}/L_{5100}<0.03$) objects resembling MoM-BH* and The Cliff, from a spectroscopic sample of 132 LRDs at $2\lesssim z\lesssim9$. This identifies three LRDs that we newly classify as BH*-like objects, more than doubling the existing sample: two at $z>6$ and one at $z\sim3$. The UV slope reddens as the NUV- ($L_{\rm 3500}$) and FUV-to-optical ratios fall, with $β_{\rm UV}$ rising from $-1.8$ at $L_{\rm 1500}/L_{5100}\sim 0.5$ to $-0.5$ at $L_{\rm 1500}/L_{5100}<0.03$, beyond values typically seen at these redshifts. As the UV-to-optical ratio weakens, gas-enshrouded BH emission appears to dominate, leading to the quasi-hostless regime: a weak, red UV continuum emerges alongside extreme Balmer decrements and weak forbidden line emission. This reddening could be reproduced by a simple model of a blackbody and hydrogen nebular emission leaking through a high column density screen of neutral hydrogen in the $n=2$ level ($N_{\rm H,\,n=2}\approx10^{17}\,{\rm cm^{-2}}$), combined with a relatively weak host galaxy, although alternative CE scenarios may also reproduce this reddening. The host galaxy's growing FUV contribution in this model results in a testable UV morphological evolution with wavelength. This work identifies a family of quasi-hostless LRDs that share a common, intrinsically red, rest-UV continuum originating from their central engine.

</details>


### [2] [Resolved internal dynamics of a globular cluster candidate in a cosmological simulation at cosmic dawn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28212)
*Raffaele Pascale,Francesco Calura,Enrico Vesperini,Alessandro della Croce,Joakim Rosdahl,Alessandro Lupi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨率宇宙学模拟，首次在亚秒差距尺度上追踪了球状星团（GC）前身系统的引力坍缩与组装过程，揭示其在红移z≈10.5时3 Myr内快速形成、迅速转为恒星主导并获得有序内禀转动，其性质与JWST观测到的高红移致密恒星系统一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球状星团是宇宙中最古老、最致密的恒星系统，其快速形成机制和早期内部动力学结构仍不清楚，现有宇宙学模拟难以分辨其诞生细节。

Method: 采用一种独特的宇宙学模拟方法，兼具亚秒差距空间分辨率与单颗恒星粒子形成能力，直接解析GC前身系统的引力坍缩、质量组装、内部动力学演化及角动量积累过程。

Result: 该GC前身系统在z≈10.5、约3 Myr内完成大部分恒星质量组装，迅速变为气体贫乏、恒星主导系统，并通过多个子恒星系统的等级式并合获得有序内禀转动；其寄主暗物质晕质量约为1.73×10⁸ M⊙，系统密度、尺寸和质量与JWST在高红移发现的致密恒星系统重叠。

Conclusion: GC前身系统在形成之初即可具备明确的内部运动学结构（如有序转动），表明其动力学特征可能在极早期即已建立，挑战了传统认为GC初始为动力学弛豫系统的观点。

Abstract: Globular clusters (GCs) are among the oldest and densest stellar systems in the Universe. Their compact sizes and old ages imply they must have assembled rapidly in the early Universe, yet the detailed physical mechanisms responsible for their formation remain uncertain. Although several scenarios have been proposed, cosmological simulations have so far been unable to resolve their detailed formation and internal dynamical structure at birth. Using a cosmological simulation that uniquely combines sub-parsec resolution with the formation of individual star particles, we follow the gravitational collapse and assembly of a compact stellar system with GC-progenitor mass and size, while directly resolving its internal dynamics and tracing the build-up of its angular momentum. The system assembles most of its stellar mass within 3 Myr, at $z\simeq10.5$, it rapidly becomes gas-poor, star-dominated, and develops ordered internal rotation acquired largely through the hierarchical assembly of distinct stellar subsystems. It forms as a satellite of a larger stellar complex within the virial radius of the $M_{\rm vir}\simeq1.73\times10^8\,M_\odot$ dark-matter halo hosting it. Its density, size and mass overlap with those of compact stellar systems recently observed by JWST at high redshift. These results indicate that GC progenitors can have well-defined internal kinematic structures already after their formation.

</details>


### [3] [Modeling extraordinary relativistic jets on the scale of the cosmic web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28376)
*Gourab Giri,Marco Tavani,Dario Borgogno,Andrea Botteon,Rossella Cassano,Valerio Vittorini,Claudio Gheller,Gianfranco Brunetti,Paul J. Wiita,Tiziana Venturi,Bernie Fanaroff,Andrea Mignone,D J Saikia,Somak Raychaudhury*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过三维相对论磁流体动力学数值模拟，成功解释了超大尺度喷流Porphyrion（约7 Mpc）的观测特性，指出其可在低密度星系群环境中以近似相对论速度传播达9 Mpc；相比之下，高密度环境中同类功率喷流仅能传播数Mpc，这解释了巨射电星系的普遍尺度，并预测未来巡天将在宇宙网低密度区发现更大尺度喷流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有磁流体动力学模型难以解释延伸至宇宙网纤维尺度（如Porphyrion达7 Mpc）的超大质量黑洞喷流，亟需在真实环境条件下验证其可行性。

Method: 采用先进的三维相对论磁流体动力学（MHD）数值模拟，构建Porphyrion喷流在低密度星系群环境中的演化模型，并对比不同外部介质密度下的喷流传播行为。

Result: 模拟表明Porphyrion喷流在低密度环境中可扩展至约9 Mpc、历时约20 Myr，且大部分时间以相对论速度传播；而在高密度结构中，同等功率喷流仅能传播几Mpc，与多数巨射电星系观测一致。

Conclusion: 喷流尺度强烈依赖于宿主环境密度，低密度区域是产生超大尺度喷流的关键条件；该结果为理解喷流反馈及宇宙网中稀有强喷流种群提供了新视角，并提出可观测预言。

Abstract: Recent observations of supermassive black hole jets extending up to the typical sizes of cosmic web filaments challenge our current understanding. In particular, the overall jet size of J152932.16+601534.4 ("Porphyrion") reaches $\sim 7$ Mpc at redshift 0.896. Magneto-hydrodynamic (MHD) models of relativistic jets have never been successfully applied to such extraordinary distances, requiring demanding jet-environment and flow stability conditions. By using state-of-the-art numerical simulations of 3D relativistic-MHD jets, we show that Porphyrion's observational properties can be successfully modelled in a realistic scenario, involving a jet kinetic power of $3.5 \times 10^{46}\,\mathrm{erg\,s^{-1}}$ and an external medium of a low-density galaxy group. We find that the pair of jets expands to $\sim 9$ Mpc in about $20$ Myr, implying that the jet grows with relativistic speeds for most of its evolution. Comparing this case with other jets of similar power propagating in substantially denser cosmic structures, we find much slower outflows that can only reach a few Mpc, consistent with the bulk of the observed population of giant radio galaxies. Our results make a testable prediction, i.e. future detections of even larger-scale jets in upcoming surveys and database searches will be invaluable in studying the demographics and feedback of the poorly known population of powerful jets, particularly in under-dense regions of the cosmic web.

</details>

*Jack T. Warfield,Nitya Kallivayalil,Sangmo Tony Sohn,Christopher T. Garling,Andrew B. Pace,Roeland P. van der Marel,Daniel R. Weisz,Mark A. Fardal,Niusha Ahvazi,Jay Anderson,Kaia R. Atzberger,Paul Bennet,Martha L. Boyer,Roger E. Cohen,Andrew A. Cole,Matteo Correnti,Andrew E. Dolphin,Marla C. Geha,Mario Gennaro,Kevin A. McKinnon,Kristen B. W. McQuinn,Max J. B. Newman,Ekta Patel,Karin M. Sandstrom,Alessandro Savino,Evan D. Skillman,Eduardo Vitral,Laura L. Watkins,Benjamin F. Williams*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文首次通过结合哈勃太空望远镜（HST）和詹姆斯·韦布太空望远镜（JWST）的成像数据，测得了孤立矮星系WLM的绝对系统自行（PM），并评估了跨望远镜测量的精度与系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上星系自行测量受限于参考系（如Gaia仅适用于银河系内恒星），而对河外星系需依赖背景星系作为静止参考；本文旨在突破观测平台限制，实现HST与JWST跨代望远镜联合自行测量，并评估其可行性与精度。

Method: 利用HST/ACS（F814W，2015 & 2024）和JWST/NIRCam（多滤光片，2022 & 2024）对WLM星系进行多历元成像；通过匹配点扩散函数、注入模拟星系校正偏差、分别拟合各NIRCam探测器，测量相对于背景星系的系统自行；比较不同滤光片（F090W/F150W/F200W/F250M/F430M）及HST–HST结果的一致性。

Result: 获得WLM最精确的HST–HST自行：(μα*, μδ) = (+0.087±0.019, −0.079±0.017) mas yr⁻¹（8.93年基线，145个背景星系）；HST–JWST自行在F090W/F150W下与HST–HST在1σ内一致，F200W偏差约2σ；长波段F250M/F430M结果一致但精度较低；发现约0.05 mas yr⁻¹的跨台站系统误差无法排除（7年基线）。

Conclusion: HST与JWST联合自行测量可行且精度接近HST自身重复测量，推荐选用与首期滤光片波长最接近的NIRCam滤光片（如F090W匹配F814W）；长波滤光片在更近目标或更长时基下具潜力；该方法为未来河外星系动力学研究开辟新路径。

Abstract: We present the first absolute systemic proper motion (PM) of a galaxy measured by combining Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and JWST imaging. The PM of WLM, an isolated dwarf galaxy at $\sim$1 Mpc, is measured against background galaxies with Advanced Camera for Surveys (ACS) F814W imaging from 2015 and 2024 and NIRCam imaging from 2022 and 2024. The two ACS epochs give a systemic PM of $(μ_α^*, μ_δ) = (+0.087 \pm 0.019, -0.079 \pm 0.017)$ mas yr$^{-1}$ from 145 galaxies over 8.93 years, which agrees with previous Gaia-based PMs to within 0.4$σ$ and is the most precise PM of WLM. For the HST-to-JWST PMs, we fit each NIRCam detector separately, match the point-spread functions with empirically derived convolution kernels, and correct biases with injected galaxies. With an F090W second epoch, the PM is $(+0.081 \pm 0.045, -0.113 \pm 0.046)$ mas yr$^{-1}$ from 43 galaxies over 7.02 years, which, like the F150W PM, agrees with HST-to-HST over the same area to within 1$σ$, while F200W differs by $\sim$2$σ$. The long-wavelength F250M and F430M PMs are consistent to within 1$σ$, though less precise. Fewer galaxies can be used at wavelengths farther from F814W. A cross-observatory systematic of $\sim$0.05 mas yr$^{-1}$ cannot be ruled out over 7 years. At equal baselines, an F090W second epoch is at least as precise as our second ACS epoch over the same area. We recommend the NIRCam filter closest in wavelength to the first-epoch filter, though the long-wavelength filters show promise for closer targets or longer baselines.

</details>


### [5] [Age dependence of the radial metallicity gradient in the Galactic disc with LAMOST and Gaia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28456)
*Yi-De Jing,Duojie Jia,Bin Wang,Yulong Gao,Liyuan Lu,Zheng-Jun Du,Zenghui Zou*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用LAMOST DR7、Gaia DR3和修正后的StarHorse年龄星表，分析银河系盘恒星的径向金属丰度梯度与年龄关系（MGAR），发现其呈非单调变化：中等年龄（~6–8 Gyr）梯度最陡负，随后随年龄增大显著变平；该 flattening 特征在多种稳健性检验下均成立，但年轻端形态和极小值位置较不稳定；结果表明恒星参数选择可能是以往研究中MGAR形态差异的重要原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解银河系盘化学演化历史，特别是金属丰度梯度如何随恒星年龄变化，以约束星系形成与演化模型。

Method: 基于580,037颗太阳邻域FGK型矮星和亚巨星样本，结合LAMOST DR7光谱、Gaia DR3天体测量及StarHorse年龄数据，构建径向[Fe/H]梯度-年龄关系（MGAR）；进行多组稳健性检验（如不同α元素分群、引导半径定义、径向拟合范围、垂直轨道选择、年龄误差传播、替代年龄标度等），并控制有效温度与表面重力分布以评估系统偏差。

Result: 观测到非单调MGAR：中等年龄（6–8 Gyr）梯度最负，之后显著变平；该 flattening 在低α星族中依然存在，且在多种方法和假设下稳健；而年轻端梯度幅度和形态对恒星参数选择敏感；APOGEE DR17交叉验证支持该中间至老年段变平特征。

Conclusion: MGAR中中间至老年段的梯度变平是最稳健的观测特征，反映了银河系盘化学演化的重要阶段；而年轻端细节易受恒星参数系统偏差影响，提示此前文献中MGAR形态差异可能源于样本选择与参数控制策略不同。

Abstract: We investigate the stellar radial metallicity ([Fe/H]) gradient--age relation (MGAR) of the Galactic disc using a solar-neighbourhood sample of 580\,037 F-, G-, and K-type (FGK) dwarf and turn-off stars from LAMOST DR7, Gaia Data Release3, and the corrected StarHorse age catalogue. The fiducial MGAR is non-monotonic, with relatively strong negative gradients at intermediate ages followed by substantial flattening towards older populations. The most negative gradient occurs at $6$--$8$ Gyr in the adopted 2-Gyr bins, although the $4$--$6$ Gyr slope is statistically similar, indicating a broad intermediate-age turnover rather than a sharply defined transition. The flattening persists within the low-$α$ population, indicating that chemical-population mixing alone cannot explain the full-sample relation, and is also recovered under alternative guiding-radius definitions, radial fitting ranges, vertical-orbit selections, propagation of the reported random age uncertainties, and an alternative stellar-age scale. In contrast, controlling the age-dependent $T_{\rm eff}$--$\log g$ distribution substantially reduces the absolute gradient amplitudes and modifies the younger-age behaviour. A same-star comparison with APOGEE DR17 further shows that the broad age dependence of the controlled MGAR is recovered using an external [Fe/H] scale. We therefore identify the intermediate-to-old-age flattening as the most robust feature of the observed MGAR, whereas the precise location and depth of the intermediate-age minimum and the detailed younger-age morphology are less stable. These results indicate that stellar-parameter selection can contribute to the diversity of MGAR morphologies reported in previous studies.

</details>


### [6] [Chemical structure of the solar neighbourhood with DESI DR1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28475)
*Yi-De Jing,Duojie Jia,Yulong Gao,Zheng-jun Du,Zenghui Zou,Liyuan Lu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用DESI DR1和Gaia数据，对太阳邻域恒星的化学、空间与运动学性质进行分析，通过[α/Fe]–[Fe/H]平面高斯混合模型将FGK矮星分为低α和高α两类，发现二者具有明显不同的化学与动力学特征，但速度空间存在显著重叠，表明化学分类不能唯一对应当前运动学成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究银河系盘恒星的化学与动力学关联，理解其形成与演化历史。

Method: 结合DESI Data Release 1与Gaia天体测量数据，构建高质量FGK矮星样本，并在[α/Fe]–[Fe/H]平面上使用高斯混合模型划分低α与高α恒星种群，进而分析其金属丰度梯度、空间分布及运动学特性。

Result: 低α种群更富金属、集中在银道面附近、具有负的径向与垂直金属丰度梯度；高α种群更贫金属、垂直梯度更陡、径向梯度近似平坦、动力学更热；但两者在速度空间显著重叠。

Conclusion: 低α与高α恒星具有不同的化学增丰历史，但化学分类不唯一对应当前运动学成分，观测到的化学-动力学结构可由化学增丰、径向迁移与长期动力学演化共同解释。

Abstract: We analyse the chemical, spatial, and kinematic properties of stars in the solar neighbourhood using DESI Data Release 1 combined with Gaia astrometry. A high-quality FGK dwarf sample is separated into low-$α$ and high-$α$ populations using Gaussian mixture modelling in the [$α$/Fe]--[Fe/H] plane. The low-$α$ population is more metal rich, dominates near the Galactic mid-plane, and shows negative radial and vertical metallicity gradients. The high-$α$ population is more metal poor, has a steeper vertical metallicity gradient, an approximately flat radial metallicity profile, and dynamically hotter kinematics. Despite these differences, the two chemically defined populations overlap substantially in velocity space. These results indicate distinct chemical enrichment histories for the low-$α$ and high-$α$ populations, while showing that chemical classification does not map uniquely onto present-day kinematic components. The observed chemo-dynamical structure is consistent with the combined effects of chemical enrichment, radial redistribution, and secular dynamical evolution in shaping the Galactic disc.

</details>


### [7] [Identifying Distant and Highly Reddened Star Clusters: 486 Open Clusters and Candidates in the Dusty Galactic Disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29181)
*Zhihong He,Xiaoping Wang,Qian Cui,Yangping Luo,Kun Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用Gaia DR3和2MASS数据，结合贝叶斯成员概率与DBSCAN算法，在银河系盘中发现了486个星团（含候选体），其中450个为全新发现，显著填补了高距离、高消光区域的观测空白，并揭示了银河系翘曲结构特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

The high-mass regime is statistically indistinguishable across all groups, while four groups also exhibit consistent IMFs over the full mass range. The non-clustered population exhibits a broader and more uncertain IMF, although residual field contamination cannot be ruled out. Conclusions: Our results support previous evidence for the complex internal structure of OB1b. Abstract: Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will observe millions of galactic binaries (GBs) at once, thousands of them resolvable, with signals that overlap in frequency. Separating them requires a global fit, a joint inference of all sources, which today takes Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) samplers hours to days on a cluster and is repeated for each new stretch of data. We adapt recent advances in large-scale diffusion models, flow matching with a multimodal transformer, to learn the joint posterior once and then draw from it in a fraction of a second. We evaluate our approach on a realistic simulator, with full instrument response and noise, priors fitted to a simulated Galactic catalogue, and sources sampled anywhere in the entire 0.1 to 16 mHz frequency band. We demonstrate that the approach is scalable to up to 15 653 sources fitted simultaneously, evaluating calibration and coverage w.r.t. the MCMC baseline. The results show that this approach could be realistically scaled to tackle the full global fit problem.

</details>

Abstract: We use the POSSUM catalog with its high density of background RM sources (30-50 per square degree) to extend previous LOFAR work and investigate the sign of galactic halo magnetic fields. Our analysis considers 10 edge-on galaxies in the field of view of the POSSUM survey with angular sizes between 60 and 100. For each such galaxy we collect all available background RM sources in a circular region with a radius of 2? around it. We rescale all galaxy sizes and their associated background RM source distances to a common galaxy size of 100 and stack all these regions together. We divide the stacked configuration of RM sources into the four quadrants on either side of the stacked galaxy axis. The mean RM of background sources is computed in each quadrant, and we obtain a pattern of positive and negative mean RM values around the stacked galaxy. A consistent, systematic RM-gradient pattern emerges inside about 6 stacked galactic radii, as expected in Magnetic Disk-Winds with the axial magnetic field configuration predicted by the Cosmic Battery. The inferred large-scale axial electric current in the halo flows from the center outwards. Beyond 6 galactic radii we observe the reverse mean RM pattern, as expected in the axial Magnetic Black-Hole Jet configuration predicted by the Cosmic Battery. Our work confirms the prediction of the Cosmic Battery, and presents one of the very few known signatures of the magnetic field in the halo and the circumgalactic medium (CGM).

Abstract: JWST observations have revealed a higher-than-expected abundance of bright galaxies at $z \gtrsim 10$. In this work, we construct spectral energy distribution (SED) templates based on a redshift- and metallicity-dependent IMF ($z$-$Z$ dependent IMF) motivated by the star-cluster simulations of Chon et al. (2022) and the redshift-dependent IMF model of Steinhardt et al. (2020). The adopted $z$-$Z$ dependent IMF enhances the UV luminosity of young stellar populations, particularly at low metallicity, and increases the scatter in the UV luminosity-to-star formation rate conversion factor relative to the Kroupa IMF. Without dust attenuation, both top-heavy IMF models predict a substantially enhanced bright end of the UVLF. However, when dust attenuation is included and calibrated at $z=7$, IMF-dependent metal enrichment and the resulting dust attenuation partly offset the intrinsic UV enhancement. The self-consistent model broadly reproduces the observed UVLF at $z\sim10$, although discrepancies remain at $z\gtrsim12$. For the selected high-redshift UVLF constraints, the best-fitting dust optical depths are 51% and 21% of the fiducial values at $z=9.7$ and $13.04$, respectively, indicating a preference for weaker effective dust attenuation. The highest-redshift comparison does not provide a robust constraint. Our results highlight the importance of accounting for both enhanced stellar UV emission and IMF-dependent metal enrichment and dust attenuation when assessing the impact of a top-heavy IMF on high-redshift galaxies.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同神经网络在强透镜搜寻中的选择偏差，以评估ECLIPSE星表的完备性，并指导未来多网络融合策略。

Method: 对比分析五个独立构建的神经网络（含CNN与Vision Transformer）在相同数据但不同图像预处理与训练策略下的候选体识别结果；在50 deg²标定集和完整DR1样本上评估其得分差异、回收率及系统性偏好（如Einstein半径、构型、光度红移、星等、质量分布等）。

Result: 各网络打分差异显著；融合可使2000次人工检验内回收超500个透镜候选体；单个网络存在明显偏差——部分偏好大Einstein半径系统，部分更敏感于紧凑构型；并在光度红移、星等、光晕形状等方面呈现不同倾向。

Conclusion: 神经网络的选择偏差深刻影响ECLIPSE星表构成；需采用互补性网络组合策略，以在后续Euclid数据释放及LSST/Roman等宽视场巡天中兼顾透镜样本的完备性与多样性。

Abstract: The Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1) strong lens search presents ECLIPSE, the largest catalogue of strong gravitational lens candidates to date, with >15 000 systems identified across 1900 deg2 of sky. It was constructed using five neural networks applied to approximately 68 million galaxies, followed by citizen science and expert visual inspection. In this work, we compare how the independently developed networks use image information to identify their selection of strong lens candidates. The search combines convolutional neural networks and a vision transformer, using the same pool of data but with different image processing and training choices. We investigate why these networks find different systems and what that means for the completeness of our catalogue. We evaluate all networks on a 50 deg2 calibration set and their application to the full DR1 sample. We find that the networks score candidates very differently, and that combining them recovers over 500 lens candidates within our calibration set at an inspection budget of 2000 objects. On the full DR1 sample, we show that individual networks have distinct biases: some networks favour systems with a larger Einstein radius while others are more sensitive to compact configurations. We also investigate network preferences in terms of lens light profile, mass distribution geometry, photometric redshift, and magnitudes. These findings provide a detailed analysis of how neural network selection biases have shaped the Euclid DR1 ECLIPSE catalogue. The results inform how networks should be combined in future Euclid data releases and upcoming wide-field surveys such as LSST or Roman to maximise both completeness and diversity of the recovered lens population.

</details>

Abstract: This work presents a Galilean covariant extension of the MIT bag model to describe heavy quark-antiquark systems within a non-relativistic framework. Using a 5-dimensional formulation of Galilean covariance, a Dirac-like equation governing quark dynamics inside a confining bag is constructed, and the corresponding boundary conditions are derived. Analytical solutions for the spinor wavefunctions and eigenfrequencies are obtained. Numerical results show some agreement with experimental masses and hyperfine splittings for heavy mesons, particularly charmonium and bottomonium states. However, the approach is less reliable for light-quark systems, indicating the limitations of the non-relativistic approximation.

</details>

Abstract: There is high interest in improving experimental sensitivity to sub-GeV dark matter (DM) that scatters with electrons, as compelling theoretical models seem within reach. At the lowest DM masses, most of the expected signal lies in the single-electron regime, but so do large backgrounds. Xenon experiments therefore either do not yet search there at all or, as in XENONnT, apply cuts so severe that only $\sim$$10^{-4}$ of the exposure is retained, with sensitivity set by the criterion $S \lesssim B$, where those are the signal and background counts. We advocate that xenon detectors instead utilize most of their dark-electron rate (i.e., the total raw electron hit rate) and perform an annual-modulation search, for which the sensitivity scales as $S \lesssim \sqrt{B}$. Modern DAQs already record this rate, continuously in triggerless systems such as the XENONnT and PandaX-4T DAQs, and within trigger windows in LZ, so no new hardware is required. Using measured dark-electron rates, we estimate the sensitivity of XENONnT, LZ, PandaX-4T, PandaX-xT, and XLZD. We find that dramatic gains in sensitivity are possible, motivating new analyses of present and future xenon data.

</details>

Abstract: Vector boson scattering is a powerful tool to test the mechanism of electroweak symmetry breaking. We focus on Composite Higgs models with an underlying fermionic description and assume that only one pseudo Nambu-Goldstone boson contributes to electroweak symmetry breaking, which we identify as the Higgs state of the Standard Model. In these scenarios, LHC data imply lower bounds on the predicted resonances below 2 TeV depending on the scenario. Using $WZjj$ production as a benchmark, we demonstrate that the tails of various distributions nevertheless show significant deviations from Standard Model predictions. This holds in particular for the case of longitudinally polarised vector bosons.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 量化了SMEFT算符对PDFs的影响：PDF不确定性仅适度增加，且拟合结果对SMEFT效应具有鲁棒性；首次在全局拟合中纳入带电流传Drell-Yan数据。

Conclusion: SMEFT与PDFs必须联合拟合以获得可靠的新物理约束；当前框架显著提升了低能与高能探针的一致性，为未来精度实验提供了基准方法。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive global analysis of Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) operators utilising a diverse set of high- and low-energy experimental probes. By systematically matching the SMEFT to the Low-Energy Effective Field Theory (LEFT), we incorporate parity-violating observables into a global fit of 15 independent Wilson coefficients. This low-energy dataset, comprising parity-violation observables, neutron and nuclear $β$-decays, as well as leptonic and semileptonic kaon and pion decays, is combined with high-energy measurements including high-invariant-mass Drell-Yan data in both neutral- and charged-current channels, with the latter included for the first time, Higgs boson measurements, Electroweak Precision Observables (EWPOs) from LEP, and diboson production at LEP and the LHC. Crucially, this framework features a simultaneous fit of both the proton Parton Distribution Functions (PDFs) and the SMEFT Wilson coefficients using the public {\tt SIMUnet} framework. We quantify the direct interplay between SMEFT operators and hadron structure, demonstrating the robustness of the SMEFT bounds and the relative stability of the resulting PDFs with respect to the inclusion of SMEFT effects, with only a moderate increase in their uncertainties.

</details>

Abstract: Weakly Interacting Massive Particles (WIMPs) are conventionally assumed to acquire their dark matter relic abundance through thermal freeze-out. However, this association relies on assumptions concerning the thermal history of the early Universe. We revisit the Inert Doublet Model (IDM), a representative WIMP framework in which dark matter interacts with the Standard Model through electroweak gauge interactions, even when its Higgs-portal coupling vanishes. We demonstrate that sufficiently low reheating temperatures can prevent chemical equilibrium, allowing a genuine WIMP to acquire its relic abundance through freeze-in rather than freeze-out. Considering both instantaneous and finite-duration reheating, we identify different production histories, including freeze-out during radiation domination, freeze-out during reheating, and freeze-in with potentially significant annihilation backreactions. These scenarios open regions of the IDM parameter space that would otherwise be excluded by dark matter overabundance. Our results demonstrate that WIMPs can behave as FIMPs, depending on the thermal history of the Universe.

</details>

The resulting light active neutrinos are of Majorana nature and the lightest electrically neutral state mediating the scotogenic loop is stabilized by $M_P$ and can be identified as a weakly interacting massive particle dark matter candidate. We analyze the phenomenology of a predominantly singlet-like CP-even scalar dark matter candidate and the representative regions of parameter space compatible with the observed thermal relic abundance. The $Z'$ boson is compatible with a broad range of parameters relevant for dark matter. In particular, electroweak precision constraints do not lead to significant tensions for kinetic mixing values up to $ε\sim 10^{-2}$. We discuss the implications of existing searches for leptophilic gauge bosons and the conditions under which visible and invisible collider signatures become relevant.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: SU(3)对称性约束的NPHF框架可有效统一描述N*(1535)和N*(1650)，揭示裸态质量序的反常现象，表明耦合道效应不可忽略，为理解低能强子谱提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigate two of the lowest-lying odd-parity nucleon resonances, $N^*(1535)$ and $N^*(1650)$, within a nonperturbative Hamiltonian framework (NPHF). The coupled-channel model contains two three-quark bare states with total quark spins $1/2$ and $3/2$, together with four meson-baryon channels: $πN$, $ηN$, $KΛ$, and $KΣ$. Constraints from SU(3) flavor symmetry and the bare-state spin wave functions are imposed on the bare-state-channel vertices and the channel-channel Weinberg-Tomozawa interactions. This substantially reduces the number of free parameters. We combine fits to infinite-volume $πN$ $S_{11}$ scattering data with finite-volume lattice QCD spectra. We use Hamiltonian eigenvectors to analyze the model-basis compositions. A contamination function then compares the resulting operator-mixing pattern with lattice correlation-matrix eigenvectors. Within the scenarios considered, both bare states and coupled-channel interactions play important roles in describing the two resonances. From our combined infinite and finite volume analysis, in contrast to conventional quark-model expectations, the spin-$1/2$ bare state is heavier than the spin-$3/2$ bare state. The contamination analysis is consistent with the associated operator assignment and with a recent analysis of the relativistic wavefunctions associated with these states. The symmetry-constrained NPHF provides a unified description of the two $S_{11}$ resonances. The combined analysis also constrains their model interpretation.

</details>

Abstract: In this work, we investigate helicity-dependent fermion production driven by a pseudoscalar Yukawa interaction within the one-loop effective action framework. The resulting non-commuting transfer matrices produce a persistent helical spectrum, as demonstrated by the probabilities as a function of momentum obtained after successive scattering events. A comparison with scalar Yukawa preheating further confirms the role of helicity mixing in generating the helical asymmetry induced by pseudoscalar mechanism. These results establish an effective action framework for studying helicity asymmetry during fermionic preheating and provide insight into the influence of the inflaton's dynamical evolution on particle production.

A key requirement of this mechanism is that the force carrier be lighter than the strong-coupling scale. A natural application would be to embed classicalization in the electroweak Higgs sector to address the little hierarchy problem. This breaking yields a Majoron $J$ and makes the lightest $\textit{dark}$ particle stable. The charged $\textit{dark}$ fermion acquires its mass solely from U(1)$_L$ breaking and thus induces an anomalous Majoron coupling to photons. Current bounds on this coupling exclude a massless Majoron for TeV-scale U(1)$_L$ breaking. We add a linear term that breaks lepton number softly and gives the Majoron a GeV-scale mass. We analyse how the framework can be tested through charged-lepton flavour-violating decays such as $μ\to eγ$, which receive contributions from both the $\textit{dark}$ and the seesaw sectors. Moreover, both sectors enhance the Majoron couplings to charged leptons, so that the Majoron-emitting decays $τ\to \ell J$ ($\ell = e,μ$) typically exceed $τ\to \ellγ$ by orders of magnitude and can probe parameter space beyond the reach of $μ\to eγ$.

Abstract: Using the Polyakov-loop extended linear-sigma model (PLSM), we investigate strongly interacting matter at finite temperature and baryon density by comparing the three-flavor (u,d,s) and four-flavor (u,d,s,c) formulations on equal footing, with the aim of isolating the thermal contribution of the charm quark. Abstract: We investigate the compatibility of existing limits on trilepton nucleon decay by the Super-Kamiokande (Super-K) experiment with Baryon Number Violation (BNV) at the TeV scale and motivate a dedicated search using existing data at the LHC. Working within an effective field theory framework, we derive model-independent constraints on higher-dimensional operators associated with $B - L/3$ conserving texture of BNV. These constraints translate into bounds of $O(1-30)$ TeV on the BNV scale, leaving open the possibility of an LHC search for $B - L/3$ type BNV. Motivated by this complementarity, we investigate the collider phenomenology of a representative UV completion featuring an $s$-channel diquark resonance that decays into a leptoquark--dilepton pair, producing a distinctive final state containing an anti-top quark, two charged anti-leptons, and missing transverse energy. Our results indicate that, although same-sign dilepton searches by ATLAS and CMS already place indirect constraints on this model, a dedicated analysis based on the selection cuts we develop significantly extends the reach for our chosen benchmarks. Abstract: The soft anomalous dimension governs the universal long-distance singularities of gauge theory amplitudes. It exhibits a highly constrained analytic structure and has been shown to be amenable to bootstrap methods. In this paper we revisit the relation between unitarity cuts and sequential discontinuities of Feynman integrals and use it to derive new constraints on the soft anomalous dimension. At three loops, we show that certain sequential discontinuities in non-overlapping two-particle channels vanish for every web contributing to the quadrupole function that correlates four coloured partons. These constraints hold for arbitrary parton masses, and we explicitly verify them for the known massless and one-mass cases. We then demonstrate their constraining power by substantially strengthening the massless bootstrap and carrying out the first bootstrap construction of the one-mass quadrupole function. The new constraints are largely complementary to those arising from the absence of unphysical branch points, symmetries, and collinear and Regge limits. With their help, we determine each quadrupole function within the assumed function space up to a single coefficient, which we fix by matching to existing diagrammatic results. Abstract: Non-Abelian lattice gauge theory with dynamical fermions is a central target for tensor-network and quantum-simulation methods, but controlled reliability studies on small systems are scarce. We benchmark two complementary Hilbert-space approaches for a 2+1D SU(2) quantum link model with one staggered fermion flavour on small two-dimensional lattices: sparse exact diagonalization (ED) and matrix-product-state density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG). The link variables use the five-state $\{5\}$ truncation, and fermions are represented by two-colour four-state sites. We implement ED with explicit cross-site Jordan--Wigner strings and DMRG with total-fermion-number conservation and a matrix product operator (MPO) built from local terms. We validate the link algebra, Gauss-law commutation, Hermiticity, the pure-gauge limit, and the MPO against ED component by component. The $2\times2$ methods agree to $10^{-13}$ and the $2\times3$--$2\times5$ ladders are controlled at or below the $10^{-5}$ level. The $3\times3$ geometry illustrates the role of colour neutrality: at half filling the odd fermion number makes $G^2=3/4$ the exact lower bound, while one hole or one doublon restores neutrality and lowers the residual to $\sim2\times10^{-5}$ at $χ=2048$. An independent exact-Gauss fPEPS provides a reference for the even-filling $3\times2$ sector. The results define a reproducible reliability envelope for non-Abelian gauge-fermion benchmarks and show where larger-scale simulations require larger bond dimensions or different tensor-network geometries.

Abstract: QED corrections to observables in QCD are by now well-understood at zero temperature, where QED effects are perturbative and required for precision physics observables, such as the neutron-proton mass difference or the muon anomalous magnetic moment, among many others. However, there are indications in the literature that at finite temperature the introduction of QED can lead to non-perturbative effects, affecting the structure of the phase diagram, since the combined theory is exactly center-symmetric. In this proceedings article, we analyze the structure of the combined theory and the different strategies to simulate it on the lattice, as well as presenting novel results of lattice QCD+QED simulations at finite temperature, focusing on understanding the role of the electromagnetic coupling in the thermodynamics of this system. We also discuss new features that arise in the combined theory and the challenges that these simulations entail, which require the development of new algorithms in order to understand the phase diagram of QCD+QED from first principles.

They differ, however, in whether a thermal transition persists in the continuum chiral limit. Our results indicate that the thermal chiral transition persists in the continuum chiral limit for $N_f=7$, whereas the massless continuum theory with $N_f=8$ remains chirally symmetric in the vacuum. Abstract: We present a first lattice calculation of the leading-order effect of dynamical photons on the thermodynamics of QCD at nonzero isospin chemical potential $μ_I$, across the onset of charged-pion condensation. However, a direct simulation is not possible since unequal quark charges render the QCD+QED action complex at nonzero $μ_I$. Abstract: Spaceborne remote sensing of the cryosphere is fundamental to map ice structure and dynamics. Accurate mass balance estimation requires a better understanding of melt/refreeze processes and firn hydrology, whereas spaceborne optical and Radar sensors are mostly limited to near-surface observations. The BIOMASS P-band Synthetic Aperture Radar, launched by European Space Agency in 2025, provides an unprecedented opportunity to gain a more direct access to englacial processes, thanks to the 70 cm wavelength paired with polarimetry, interferometry and tomography. This paper investigates subsurface beneath a region of historical supraglacial lake activity with BIOMASS tomography, at the Riiser-Larsen Ice Shelf in East Antarctica. The main result is that subsurface scattering appears to be related to historical supraglacial lake recurrence, with structural and depth variations over time. This suggests that recurrent supraglacial lake activity may leave persistent hydrological signatures within the firn. Overall, this study highlights the potential of gaining valuable insights into ice sheet hydrology with P-band.

Abstract: Designing low-thrust multi-asteroid rendezvous missions requires the coupled selection of asteroid sequences, encounter epochs, and low-thrust trajectories. Lambert transfers support rapid beam search but do not directly describe thrust-limited transfer capacity or propellant consumption. This paper develops two complementary low-thrust neural approximators. The first directly predicts the continuous maximum admissible initial mass from numerically optimized low-thrust transfers, providing a transfer-capacity estimate for comparison with the current spacecraft mass. The second predicts mass-dependent propellant consumption and propagates spacecraft mass to subsequent legs. With the carefully-designed input features and network structure, the approximators reduce mean absolute errors by 72.2% and 75.5%, respectively, relative to conventional deep neural network baselines. These predictions are incorporated into a Lambert-ranked beam search through resource filtering and recursive mass propagation before final beam selection. The method is evaluated on fixed-visit, maximum-visit, and deployment-collection missions. Across the tested cases, the proposed resource filtering increases the fraction of candidate sequences that yield accepted low-thrust solutions, raises the maximum validated asteroid-visit count by up to 15.4%, and improves the best mission objective by up to 17.8%. These results demonstrate that continuous transfer-capacity and propellant predictions can improve finite-width sequence search.

Abstract: Cosmic voids are vast underdense regions of the Universe that encode valuable information about its large-scale structure and evolution. By extending spherical voids into overlapping sphere structures, the Popcorn Void Finder demonstrated an improved statistical characterization of cosmic void populations. However, its origin as a research prototype progressively hindered its maintainability and extensibility. Moreover, post-publication algorithmic modifications have not been systematically evaluated, leaving their statistical and computational consequences insufficiently characterized. In this work, we present a comprehensive reimplementation of the Popcorn Void Finder, prioritizing algorithmic transparency, maintainability, and reproducibility. We document the complete identification procedure, revisit the original algorithmic choices, and evaluate their statistical and computational consequences. Comparisons between implementations revealed a significant change in the behavior of the Void Size Function, which is now characterized by a power-law regime transitioning to a Schechter-like distribution. These changes not only provide a better characterization of void sizes, but enable a new and more robust method to the determination of the shot-noise dominated regime, without implicit dependencies on internal finder parameters present in previous works. Performance benchmarks demonstrate a 1.62x speedup in spherical void identification and a substantial improvement in overlap-removal throughput. We determined the Popcorn algorithm to be negatively affected by load imbalance, and found a speedup of 63x in a tested configuration with a newly introduced early growth termination criterion. These results establish a more maintainable and reproducible foundation for popcorn void identification, while clarifying the statistical and computational trade-offs that govern its performance.

Flow matching has emerged as a promising alternative to diffusion models, with easier training, efficient sampling, and better-quality data generation. Our method shows promising capabilities to accurately process strong lensing data from modern sky surveys at scale. Our code and data are available on our project page.

The null hypothesis of natural evolution, tested with manoeuvre-free propagations, is rejected for all 18 parameter combinations: the encounter required manoeuvres by Cosmos 2612, whereas those of ICEYE-X36 are not needed to reproduce it. Closest approach occurred on 18 October at 06:33 UTC, below 0.79 km (95th percentile), with a local relative velocity of about 0.08 m/s and 41.4 h continuously within 5 km. It was the final stage of a sequence that began in May with a plane match, followed by a phasing drift, the approach and a manoeuvred retreat, with a minimum cumulative $Δv$ of 174-180 m/s. Conclusion: 定制ViT为高阶波前传感和下一代仪器（如ULTIMATE-Subaru）的遥测辅助数据压缩提供了可行且优越的深度学习方案，是注意力机制应用于SHWFS的重要进展。

Abstract: A systematic performance evaluation of attention-based deep-learning models for wavefront reconstruction in Shack-Hartmann wavefront sensor (SHWFS) systems is presented. A simulation dataset consisting of 3.5 million open-loop uncorrelated turbulent phase and image pairs modeled after the Subaru Telescope pupil was generated. The dataset spans four pupil-sampling cases (8$\times$8, 16$\times$16, 20$\times$20, and 32$\times$32 subapertures) under realistic conditions. A custom Vision Transformer (ViT) which incorporates features such as rotary positional embeddings, register tokens, and a cross-attention decoder, is benchmarked against a fully convolutional U-Net and a classical Maximum A Posteriori (MAP) linear reconstructor. The results show that the ViT architecture yields the lowest overall residual phase variance across all tested subaperture configurations, demonstrating a robust capability to model global spatial correlations and spot-to-spot cross-coupling across the pupil. While the U-Net performs competitively by capturing localized features, it exhibits $\sim1.5$ times more residual variance per mode than the ViT, which is relatively significant in high-order modes. This study represents a key step toward implementing attention-based models for high-order wavefront sensing and telemetry-assisted data reduction in next-generation instrument suites like ULTIMATE-Subaru.

Abstract: Spatial intensity interferometry has seen a major resurgence in astronomy due to its immunity to atmospheric phase turbulence, but its temporal counterpart - a cornerstone of quantum optics - has remained largely unexplored for astronomical sources. Here, we demonstrate that high-visibility temporal photon bunching signatures from stellar sources are now accessible by combining ultra-narrowband spectral filtering with high-speed single-photon counting detectors. Leveraging the adaptive optics architecture at the Palomar Observatory, we report the first observation of resolved stellar temporal photon bunching, with a peak of $g^{(2)}(τ=0) = 1.3 \pm 0.1$, from the red supergiant $α$ Her. This advance establishes stellar temporal intensity interferometry as a viable observational modality, bridging quantum optics with high-resolution stellar astrophysics.

Abstract: The Radio Neutrino Observatory Greenland (RNO-G) aims to detect ultra-high-energy neutrinos (E $\geq 100\,$PeV) through the nanosecond-long coherent radio pulses produced by the Askaryan effect when neutrino-induced particle cascades develop in the Greenlandic ice sheet. Precise calibration of the analog signal chain of each antenna channel is essential for accurate reconstruction of neutrino-induced events and their physical properties. In this work, we present a data-driven calibration method based on measurement of the ambient radio background. By combining first-principles simulations of the physical noise contributions with a parametric model of the detector response, we determine the absolute gain of each antenna channel. The resulting calibration provides a season-averaged gain for every channel with an accuracy of approximately 0.5\,dB (better than 5\%), while the investigated systematic uncertainties associated with the noise signal-model were found to be negligible. This work represents the first application of an ambient-noise-based calibration that utilizes a multi-component model for the ambient noise, originally developed for radio air-shower experiments, to an in-ice radio neutrino detector.

Abstract: Modern astronomical workflows require the coordinated use of specialised software, scientific archives, heterogeneous data, and domain-specific knowledge. Advances in large language models and multimodal foundation models enable natural-language interaction with scientific data and tools. Realising this potential requires integrating these models with established scientific capabilities.
  We present MAASAI (Multi-agent Astronomy Assistant for Scientific Analysis and Inference), a modular multi-agent system combining foundation-model reasoning, retrieval-augmented knowledge, and specialised astronomical tools within a graph-based execution framework. MAASAI coordinates request preparation, planning, execution, and response synthesis via specialised workers. Its architecture supports native and external applications, artefact and provenance management, optional human approval, and configurable inference backends, including fully local deployment with open-weight models. Motivated by SKA-era data-analysis challenges, MAASAI is also designed to support other astronomical domains.
  We evaluate MAASAI through radio-image benchmarks, single- and multi-step workflow demonstrations, and literature-grounded reasoning. Compared with a commercial-model baseline, MAASAI improves macro-averaged F1 scores from 0.53 to 0.72 for extended/diffuse emission classification and from 0.37 to 0.88 for source morphology classification. Workflow demonstrations show the coordination of measurements, source extraction, archive queries, and catalogue processing. A 94-question literature benchmark yielded generally favourable automated assessments, with comparable ratings from a pilot expert evaluation of 59 answers. These results highlight how tool-augmented multi-agent systems extend foundation-model reasoning to specialised astronomical analyses through established scientific software.

</details>


### [84] [Shaderous: A Browser-based Hydrodynamics Code for Physics Research and Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31162)
*James Guillochon*

TL;DR: Shaderous 是一个基于 WebGPU 的三维可压缩气体动力学和磁流体动力学模拟代码，专为浏览器内运行设计，支持多种物理过程和自适应网格细化，并在单精度下实现高精度模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个可在任意厂商 GPU 上通过 Web 浏览器运行的高性能、教学友好的天体物理模拟代码，兼顾易用性、可扩展性和精度。

Method: 采用无分裂 MUSCL-Hancock 格式与 HLLC 通量、多网格泊松求解器、块结构自适应网格细化（含每块独立时间步）、约束传输（HLLD 通量）、灰度辐射 M1 模型、低马赫数隐式压力、查表物态方程、冷却/粘滞/热导/电阻率/多组分气体/神经网络核燃烧等；创新地取消面通量存储、全步骤 GPU 执行（含时间步计算与 ghost cell 即时生成）、单精度浮点下保障守恒与精度。

Result: 成功实现 175 百万单元自适应网格（NVIDIA H200），在 206 个验证测试中准确再现恒星、大气平衡、激波、引力坍缩和磁化流等天体物理系统；质量、动量、能量守恒达舍入误差水平，磁场散度保持在舍入误差量级。

Conclusion: Shaderous 展示了现代 WebGPU 硬件上高效、高保真、全 GPU 执行的天体物理模拟可行性，其设计创新（无面通量、全 GPU 流程、单精度高精度）为教学、参数扫描和中小规模研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: Shaderous is a three-dimensional code for compressible gas dynamics and magnetohydrodynamics that runs in WebGPU compute shaders in a web browser on any vendor's GPU. It combines an unsplit MUSCL-Hancock scheme and HLLC fluxes with a multigrid Poisson solver, block-structured adaptive mesh refinement with per-block timesteps, constrained transport with HLLD fluxes, point masses with sinks, gray radiation transport with the M1 closure, implicit pressure for low Mach numbers, tabulated equations of state from degenerate electrons to rock and iron, cooling, viscosity, conduction, resistivity, mixtures of gases, and nuclear burning by a neural network trained on a 13-isotope reaction network. A setup is shared as a link and runs on a student's own laptop or phone, so the code suits teaching at every level. A run is limited to one GPU: the largest adaptive mesh so far held about 175 million cells on an NVIDIA H200. It therefore suits parameter studies, run as sweeps from the command line, rather than the largest single simulations. Three aspects of the design are, as far as we can find, new. First, the hydrodynamic solver stores no face flux. The two cells that share a face compute bit-identical fluxes, so cells at boundaries between levels and between timesteps recompute their neighbors' fluxes instead of keeping a flux register. Mass, momentum and energy are conserved to round-off on the adaptive mesh, and compensated sums keep the divergence of the magnetic field at round-off. Second, the whole step runs on the GPU: the timestep and the assignment of blocks to time bins are reduced there, and ghost cells are computed as they are loaded rather than exchanged. Third, the code computes in single precision yet reproduces a range of astrophysical systems accurately, from stars and atmospheres in hydrostatic balance to shocks, gravitational collapse and magnetized flows, in 206 validation tests.

</details>

Method: 提出无监督基础模型JAISP，利用首批公开发布的ECDFS深场约0.2平方度数据，通过九波段预测第十波段的方式学习十波段（Rubin ugrizy + Euclid VIS,Y,J,H）统一表征；冻结编码器后接轻量任务专用网络，分别用于源检测与天体测量。

Conclusion: JAISP证明单一像素级联合表征可同时支撑多任务、多区域、多巡天的高精度测量，为弱引力透镜、测光红移、暗源自行等联合巡天科学提供关键数据基础。

Abstract: Joint pixel-level processing of Rubin and Euclid imaging promises deeper source detection and the cross-survey consistency that precision cosmology requires. We introduce JAISP (Joint AI Survey Processing), a foundation model that learns a single representation of ten-band imaging: Rubin ugrizy and Euclid VIS, Y, J, and H, from the first public releases over $\approx 0.2$ deg$^2$ of the ECDFS deep field. Trained without labels by predicting each band from the other nine, its frozen encoder drives small task-specific networks for source detection and astrometry. The detection network recovers nearly all VIS-selected Euclid Q1 catalog sources on held-out sky, and source injection shows that combining all ten bands extends the point-source detection limit by $\approx 0.3$ mag beyond VIS alone. The astrometry network transfers the sharp VIS localization to the other nine bands, reducing median cross-band positional offsets from $\approx 50$ to $6$--$10$ mas while retaining the full precision of its VIS centroid anchor even for faint sources. Separating this per-source scatter exposes the cross-survey concordance field: a coherent $9$--$10$ mas offset between the two independently Gaia-registered astrometric solutions, dominated by a component common to all bands. Applied without retraining to a second Euclid Deep Field, both networks perform without measurable loss. These gains serve the joint-survey science case: a deeper census recovers faint neighbors whose blending biases weak-lensing shapes and photometric redshifts, and VIS-grade positions in every band supply the common astrometric reference that joint photometry and faint-source proper motions require. A single representation learned once from survey pixels thus serves several measurements across the sky.

</details>

### [86] [ACT Excited: Reconciling Starobinsky and Quadratic Chaotic Inflationary Models with ACT Results via Excited States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28144)
*Amjad Ashoorioon,Abdolreza Yousefi-Sostani*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了通过放松Bunch-Davies初始态假设，利用标量扰动的激发态（由激发参数χ_S和相位φ_S表征）来调和ACT DR6等最新宇宙学数据与Starobinsky及二次混沌暴胀模型之间的张力，尤其改善ns、αs拟合并压制r，同时满足新物理能标Λ > H等约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最新宇宙微波背景（ACT DR6+Planck+CMB lensing+DESI BAO）数据显示ns偏高、αs轻微正，使Starobinsky模型受压，二次混沌模型因r过大被排除；需不修改势函数而恢复理论与观测一致性。

Method: 引入非Bunch-Davies初始态，以尺度依赖的Bogoliubov系数（参数化为χ_S(k)和φ_S(k)）修正标量功率谱，导出其对ns、αs的修正；联合r < 0.038、Λ > H等约束，在(φ_S, χ_S)平面上搜寻兼容区域；对QC模型额外要求r压制，并解析推导χ_S导数的上下界。

Result: Starobinsky模型在(φ_S, χ_S)平面绝大部分区域兼容数据，仅小部分（如φ_S=0,π且χ_S≳1.22）被排除；QC模型需χ_S≳0.63且Λ≲6H，且χ_S必须具有特定幅度的尺度依赖性（一阶导≈0.0021–0.0058，二阶导≈0.0008–0.0062）；若张量保持BD态，则预言一致性关系r = −8n_T被破坏。

Conclusion: 非BD初始态（尤其是尺度依赖的激发参数）是缓解当前暴胀模型与高精度CMB/BAO数据张力的有效机制，无需修改暴胀势，且具备可检验的预言（如r–n_T偏离）。

Abstract: Data Release 6 of the Atacama Cosmology Telescope, combined with Planck, CMB lensing and DESI BAO data, favours a higher scalar spectral index $n_{s}$ than Planck and a mildly positive running $α_{s}$, putting the Starobinsky model in tension with the data, while the quadratic chaotic model is already excluded by its large tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$. We show that relaxing the Bunch--Davies assumption for the initial state of the scalar perturbations can restore agreement without modifying the inflaton potential. Parametrising the Bogoliubov coefficients by an excitation parameter $χ_{S}$ and a relative phase $2\varphi_{S}$, we derive the shifts of $n_{s}$ and $α_{s}$ induced by their scale dependence and map the regions of the $(\varphi_{S},χ_{S})$ plane compatible with the $1σ$ ACT DR6 ranges, the bound $r<0.038$, and the requirement that the scale of new physics $Λ$ exceed the Hubble scale $H$. For the Starobinsky model, a scale-dependent $χ_{S}$ or $\varphi_{S}$ works almost everywhere in this plane: only small neighbourhoods of $\varphi_{S}=0,π$ with $χ_{S}\gtrsim1.22$ are excluded, and a scale-dependent phase fails at $\varphi_{S}=0,π/2,π$ and at $χ_{S}=0$, where the required ${\rm d}\varphi_{S}/{\rm d}\ln k$ and ${\rm d}^2\varphi_{S}/{\rm d}(\ln k)^2$ diverge. For the QC model, the excited state must also suppress $r$, which requires $χ_{S}\gtrsim0.63$ and $Λ\lesssim6H$. A scale-dependent $χ_{S}$ must satisfy $|{\rm d}χ_{S}/{\rm d}\ln k|\gtrsim0.0021$ in both models, and in the QC model we show analytically that $0.0021\lesssim{\rm d}χ_{S}/{\rm d}\ln k\lesssim0.0058$ and $0.0008\lesssim{\rm d}^2χ_{S}/{\rm d}(\ln k)^2\lesssim0.0062$. If the tensor modes remain in the BD state, the scenario also predicts a violation of the consistency relation $r=-8n_{T}$, testable once the tensor power spectrum and its tilt are measured.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据分析中，尤其是宇宙学交叉相关测量中，需要了解两个独立高斯变量交叉相关估计量的分布，但该分布尚未被广泛理解与应用。

Method: 提供了两种教学性质的推导方法，用于获得交叉相关估计量的分布函数，并探讨其数学性质及与宇宙学可观测量的联系。

Abstract: The ubiquitous Gaussian distribution, $e^{-x^2/2}/\sqrt{2π}$, has mean of zero and variance of 1. It is well known that the estimator $\widehat{x^2} = N^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^N x_i^2$ for the variance obtained from $N$ samples $x_i$ is drawn from a $χ_n^2$ distribution, a result used often to assess the implications of data for a model. One often also needs to measure the covariance, or cross-correlation, of two different variables $x$ and $y$. If the distributions for $x$ and $y$ are both gaussian but independent of each other, the estimator $N^{-1}\sum_i x_i y_i$ for the cross-correlation is distributed about its expectation value (zero) with variance unity. The distribution function for this estimator may also be required to interpret data, for example in the measurement of cosmological cross-correlations. This distribution---the cross-correlation analog of the $χ_n^2$ distribution---appears in the literature, but it has not been widely internalized by many in the field. We therefore here present two pedagogical derivations of this distribution and discuss some of its properties. We discuss in particular the connection to cosmological observables, but note several other areas of physics where these results may apply.

</details>

Abstract: We study scalar perturbations in a quadratic subclass Ia degenerate higher-order scalar-tensor (DHOST) realization of a nonsingular bouncing cosmology previously analyzed in higher-derivative mimetic gravity. After a constant rescaling of the scalar potential, the subclass Ia model reproduces the same scale-factor history as the original model, although the associated mimetic Lagrange multiplier generally differs. For the comparison with the original mimetic model, we focus on the branch $0<γ<2/3$, for which the scalar sound speed of the original model is positive. We perform an ADM and Hamiltonian reduction of the scalar sector and show that, imposing the mimetic and momentum constraints, the reduced canonical scalar mode contains no spatial-gradient contribution. The physical scalar sound speed therefore vanishes, $c_s^2=0$, in agreement with the known vacuum behavior of the \(α_L=0\) mimetic-DHOST sector. On the matched bouncing background, the resulting effective frequency squared is independent of the comoving wavenumber and approaches zero from below in the remote contracting phase. Consequently, the asymptotic oscillatory regime used in the previous mimetic model to impose positive-frequency initial conditions is absent. Although canonical normalization can still be imposed through the Wronskian condition, it does not select the initial state uniquely. Without an additional state prescription, the asymptotic dynamics therefore does not uniquely determine the primordial scalar spectrum.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 为实现毫米波天空中主要天体物理成分（除射电源外）的高保真、联合、可微分建模，以支持场级宇宙学分析。

Method: 将多尺度小波分解与条件流匹配（CFM）结合，对约9×9平方度、1角分分辨率的四类场（κ、kSZ、tSZ、CIB）进行联合训练，显式建模其交叉耦合，并保持可解析的似然函数。

Abstract: We develop a Wavelet-factorized Conditional Flow Matching (Wavelet-CFM) framework to generate fast, accurate simulations of correlated astrophysical fields including gravitational lensing convergence ($κ$), the kinetic and thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich (kSZ and tSZ) effects, and cosmic infrared background (CIB) emission. Together these make up every bright extragalactic component of the millimeter-wave (mm-wave) sky apart from radio sources. Our approach combines multi-scale wavelet decomposition with conditional flow matching models, trained on $\sim$9$\times$9 deg$^2$ patches at 1-arcminute resolution. The four fields are generated jointly, including their cross couplings, and the model has a tractable likelihood, which to our knowledge has not previously been achieved for this set of components. The power spectra, one-point distributions and Minkowski functionals of the generated maps match a validation set to a few percent. These results show promise for enabling field-level cosmological analyses of mm-wave data.

</details>

Method: 基于理论论证，将半径依赖的熵函数K(r)推广为尺度χ依赖的普适熵函数K(χ)=⟨σ²_DM⟩，构建跨尺度通用状态方程；结合宇宙学演化分析，检验其对背景膨胀与结构形成的贡献，并引入多组分暗物质框架以协调星系与宇宙学尺度的不同行为。

Result: 尺度依赖状态方程暗物质无法单独作为全宇宙暗物质成分；K₀的星系间差异源于各星系暗物质速度弥散σ_DM的不同；提出双组分暗物质模型：一组主导星系尺度动力学，另一组主导宇宙学尺度演化。

Conclusion: 尺度依赖状态方程暗物质是有效的星系尺度模型，但需与其他暗物质组分共存以满足宇宙学约束；该工作为统一星系与宇宙学暗物质建模提供了新思路和必要框架。

Abstract: In this work we generalize the concept of scale-dependent equation of state dark matter from galactic to cosmological scales. At galactic scales, the scale-dependent equation of state which successfully describes dark matter dominated galaxies has a nearly isothermal equation of state of the form $P(r)=K(r)ρ(r)$, where the entropy function $K(r)$ is radius dependent and slowly varying with the galactic radius. Using appropriate theoretical arguments, the successful galactic scale dark matter model is generalized to have a universal equation of state of the form $\langle P(χ)\rangle =\langle ρ(χ)\rangle \langle σ_{DM}^2\rangle$, where $χ$ is the scale of the system under study and it can describe cosmological or galactic scales and $σ_{DM}$ is the characteristic velocity dispersion of dark matter at the relevant scales. This results to the entropy function behaving universally as $K(χ)=\langle σ_{DM}^2\rangle$. We investigate the cosmological implications of scale-dependent equation of state dark matter, and we conclude that this scale-dependent equation of state dark matter cannot be the sole component of dark matter in the Universe. Also the second aim of this work is to provide a sufficient explanation for the question why $K_0$ changes for each galaxy, where $K_0$ is the value of $K(r)$ at intermediate and large radii from the core of the galaxy. Furthermore, we introduce the concept of having distinct dark matter components which cluster differently, and thus controlling differently the dynamics of the Universe at various scales. Scale-dependent equation of state dark matter can control the galactic scales, while another component can dominate at cosmological scales and thus control the Universe at cosmological scales.

</details>

Abstract: The cosmological principle assumes homogeneity and isotropy on the largest scales for which the cosmic microwave background (CMB) provides the best testing grounds. Here, an analysis of the statistical isotropy for the CMB in the case of the cubic 3-torus topology is presented. Since the statistical anisotropy in the CMB 3-torus sky maps is very tiny, the analysis requires very large ensembles consisting of 100000 CMB simulations in order to reveal deviations from the isotropy. Orbit sum rules, which also have applications in quantum chaos, are used to describe the anisotropy structures found in the torus ensembles. They relate the length of orbits, which connect a given point on the surface of last scattering with the topological mirror points of the CMB observer, to the anisotropy pattern. It is found that the positions on the surface of last scattering which are closest to mirror observers possess the largest deviations from the statistical isotropy. Furthermore, the anisotropy of the normalised modulus of the gradient of the CMB temperature field is discussed.

</details>

Abstract: Recent results from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) hint at dynamical dark energy, making the accurate modelling of such cosmologies increasingly important for future analyses. In this work, we show that because, when written in superconformal time $τ$, the $N$-body equations of motion depend on the background cosmology only through the expansion history $a(τ)$, matching $a(τ)$ allows us to map one cosmology onto another. As a first, observationally relevant, application of this new method, we match a Chevallier--Polarski--Linder (CPL) cosmology to a constant-$w$ cosmology with the closest $a(τ)$. This allows us to use a single fixed-$w$ simulation to reproduce the simulation predictions of the target CPL cosmology at the field level at redshifts $0\le z\le3$. We validate this matching with 100 pairs of CPL and fixed-$w$ \textsc{mg-glam} simulations, spanning $0.1\leΩ_{\rm m}\le0.5$, $-1.4\le w_0\le-0.5$ and $-1.8\le w_a\le-0.15$. For a DESI-like cosmology, the matter power spectra of the matched and target simulations agree to 0.24\% for $k\lesssim1\,h\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$ and 0.8\% up to $k\lesssim3\,h\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$ at $0\le z\le3$, and the density phases are reproduced with a stochasticity below 0.02\%. Displacements, velocities, momenta, redshift-space multipoles, bispectra and the halo and void abundances are similarly well predicted. For most models we consider, we achieve subpercent error in the power spectrum up to scales $k\lesssim1\,h\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$ at all redshifts, with density stochasticity below 0.03\% over the same scales. The method allows constant-$w$ simulations and field-level emulators to be used for CPL cosmologies, and generalises to any cosmology with no additional scale dependence.

Abstract: We show that clustering in general dynamical dark energy models $w(z)$ can be predicted with constant dark energy $w_0\text{CDM}$ models at the field level via spectral equivalence. The prescription of this method requires equating comoving distances to the last scattering surface between predictor and target models, which is equivalent to requiring equal recent background expansion history averages. Dark energy models with the same distance thus form approximate equivalence classes, with nearly identical matter fields. To test the precision of spectral equivalence beyond the CPL parametrization, we run $N$-body simulations with various dynamical dark energy models, including spline-based $w(z)$, all matched to a single $w_0\text{CDM}$ run. Our measurements show that the power spectra of all matched dark energies agree with the predictor within $0.2\%$ up to $k = 0.5\,h\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$ and within $0.6\%$ up to $k = 1\,h\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$. We also compare the non-Gaussian information contained in the simulated fields. Bispectra differ by at most $0.26\%$ across all runs up to $k = 0.5\,h\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$ and $1.75\%$ up to $k = 1\,h\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$, while wavelet scattering transform coefficients are all within $0.05\%$. The decorrelation, $1 - r^2$, between each matched field and the predictor stays below $0.006\%$ up to $k = 1\,h\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$. This matching prescription thus offers a new tool to constrain dynamical dark energy models with arbitrary equations of state at the field level, without running additional simulation suites.
