  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超新星宇宙学分析中，使用稠密（未分箱）协方差矩阵进行拟合的计算复杂度为O(N³)，导致大规模数据（如LSST、Roman）难以直接处理，迫使采用有损分箱压缩，削弱自校准能力。

Method: 对由K个每超新星距离偏移向量构建的系统协方差，保留精确分解形式C = D + UUᵀ（D为对角统计协方差），并利用Woodbury矩阵恒等式实现高效求逆；该方法已集成至SNANA软件，并在多组模拟数据（LSST、Roman+LSST、10年LSST）上验证。

Result: 在10年LSST（N≈134,400）场景下，协方差求逆与宇宙学拟合耗时从约10周降至约1.5小时，磁盘存储从36 GB压缩至20 MB（压缩因子1800）；最佳拟合宇宙学参数与全矩阵结果几乎一致，暗能量FOM差异<0.1%，差异源于数值误差。

Conclusion: 该因子化方法使未来LSST与Roman时代的超新星大样本无损、无分箱宇宙学分析成为可能，在大幅节省计算资源的同时完全保留协方差模型中的信息。

Abstract: Cosmological analyses of Type Ia supernovae use a covariance matrix to describe statistical noise and systematic
  uncertainties. To fit a Hubble diagram with an unbinned covariance matrix, the CPU cost scales as O(N^3) for N
  supernovae, which has motivated compression into bins that loses information and reduces self-calibration power. To
  remove the need for lossy compression, we present a method that greatly reduces CPU time for matrix inversion and
  cosmology fitting, as well as matrix storage. For systematic covariances constructed from K per-supernova
  distance-shift vectors, we retain the exact representation C = D + U U^T, with diagonal statistical covariance D,
  and use the Woodbury factorization identity to efficiently invert C, significantly reducing computing time and disk
  storage without losing information. We implement this method in SNANA and validate it on simulated samples for LSST
  (N ~ 5,300, K = 18), Roman+LSST (N ~ 15,000, K = 30), and 10-yr LSST (N ~ 134,400, K = 18). For 10-yr LSST, matrix
  inversion and cosmology fit time is ~10 weeks using a dense (unbinned) matrix with 18 billion elements, and is
  reduced to ~1.5 hr using the factorization method. It also reduces covariance matrix disk storage by a factor of
  1,800 (36 GB to 20 MB). In all validation tests, the best-fit cosmological parameters are nearly identical to the
  unbinned fit, and the dark energy figure of merit (FoM) agrees to better than 0.1% accuracy; the small differences
  are likely due to numerical artifacts in the pipeline. For the LSST and Roman era, this factorization method enables
  large unbinned supernova analyses with significantly reduced computing resources compared to previous analyses using
  a dense matrix, while preserving all information in the covariance model.

</details>


### [95] [Modelling of post-reconstruction galaxy clustering with a hybrid bias expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29911)
*Vicente Izzo Dominguez,Raul E. Angulo,Antonio D. Montero-Dorta,Marcos Pellejero-Ibáñez*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了混合拉格朗日偏置展开模型，用于描述密度场重建后的星系成团性，并验证其在配置空间中的有效性；结果表明，联合使用重建前后数据可显著提升宇宙学参数（如Ω_m和σ_8）的约束精度，尤其通过打破偏置与宇宙学参数间的简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度场重建虽能增强BAO信号并恢复部分非线性演化中丢失的宇宙学信息，但要充分利用重建后超出BAO的信息，需对非线性演化和星系形成物理进行建模。

Method: 将混合拉格朗日偏置展开推广至重建后的配置空间星系成团性建模，对二阶欧拉基场统一施加单次重建位移，保持重建前后拉格朗日偏置参数一致；利用子晕丰度匹配模拟的实空间与红移空间相关函数多极展开进行拟合，并结合Fisher分析预测σ_8和Ω_m的约束能力。

Result: 该模型在r>35 h⁻¹Mpc尺度上用同一组拉格朗日偏置参数同时复现重建前后相关函数多极；对于z≈0、体积3 h⁻³Gpc³、平均数密度5×10⁻⁴ h³Mpc⁻³的巡天，仅用重建后数据使Ω_m约束提升约1.3倍，但σ_8约束略有减弱；而联合重建前后数据可使二者边际误差均减小约1/3，FOM提升约2.2倍。

Conclusion: 重建前后星系成团性包含互补宇宙学信息，可在同一混合拉格朗日偏置框架下统一建模，适用于未来巡天的联合全形状分析与 emulator 构建。

Abstract: Density-field reconstruction sharpens the baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) feature and recovers part of the cosmological information lost from two-point statistics through nonlinear evolution. Exploiting reconstructed clustering beyond BAO measurements requires modelling nonlinear evolution and galaxy formation physics. We extend the hybrid Lagrangian bias expansion to post-reconstruction galaxy clustering in configuration space and determine its range of validity. We apply a single reconstruction displacement to each Eulerian basis field of a second-order hybrid expansion, retaining the same bias parameters before and after reconstruction. We fit the correlation-function multipoles of subhalo abundance-matching mocks in real and redshift space, considering RecIso and RecSym, and use a Fisher analysis to forecast constraints on $σ_8$ and $Ω_{\rm m}$. The model reproduces the pre- and post-reconstruction multipoles for $r>35 h^{-1}\mathrm{Mpc}$ with a single set of Lagrangian bias parameters. For a $3 h^{-3}\mathrm{Gpc}^3$ volume at $z\simeq0$ with $\bar{n}=5\times10^{-4} h^3\mathrm{Mpc}^{-3}$, post-reconstruction clustering (POST) alone tightens the marginalised constraint on $Ω_{\rm m}$ by a factor of $\sim1.3$ relative to pre-reconstruction clustering (PRE), but slightly weakens that on $σ_8$ when the reconstruction bias is inferred from the observed clustering amplitude. Combining PRE and POST reduces both marginalised uncertainties by about one third and increases the figure of merit by a factor of $\sim2.2$, mainly by breaking degeneracies between bias and cosmology. Pre- and post-reconstruction clustering contain complementary cosmological information and can be modelled within the same hybrid Lagrangian bias expansion. The framework is well suited to emulation and future joint full-shape analyses of galaxy surveys.

</details>


### [96] [Dark matter mass fraction in axion stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30321)
*A. S. Dmitriev,D. G. Levkov,A. G. Panin,I. I. Tkachev*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究QCD轴子暗物质形成的微团簇内玻色星的当前质量分数及其质量函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD轴子暗物质会形成高密度的小质量微团簇，且其玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚时间小于宇宙年龄，导致内部形成玻色星；需确定当前宇宙中玻色星的质量占比和质量分布。

Method: 基于Dmitriev等人（Phys. Rev. Lett. 132, 091001 (2024)）对玻色星后续增长问题的解析解，推导当前时刻玻色星的质量分数和质量函数。

Result: 得到了当前宇宙中暗物质以玻色星形式存在的质量分数及其质量函数的具体表达式。

Conclusion: 玻色星是QCD轴子暗物质微团簇演化的重要产物，其质量分数和质量函数可由解析方法定量描述。

Abstract: QCD axion dark matter is fragmented into small mass miniclusters of high density. With the natural parameters of miniclusters, the time for Bose-Einstein condensation is less than the age of the Universe, which leads to the formation of Bose stars inside these structures. Recently, the problem of the subsequent growth of Bose stars was solved analytically (Dmitriev et al, Phys. Rev. Lett. 132, 091001 (2024)). We use this solution to determine present day mass fraction of dark matter in Bose stars and their mass function.

</details>


### [97] [A Cross-Domain Simulator and Benchmark for Uncertainty Quantification in Global 21-cm Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30444)
*Jacob Tutt,Harry Bevins,James Alvey,Dominic Anstey,John Cumner,Eloy de Lera Acedo,Anastasia Fialkov*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可微分、GPU加速的混合前向模型，用于21-cm宇宙学信号的端到端不确定性传播，显著提升计算效率并实现高精度后验校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 21-cm宇宙学中，微弱的宇宙信号需从强数个数量级的射电前景和仪器效应中分离，对模型误设极为敏感，亟需能联合传播全观测链不确定性的高精度模型。

Method: 构建融合神经网络模拟宇宙信号、物理引导的多分辨率银河前景参数化、以及天线线性代理模型的可微分GPU加速混合前向模型；结合GPU加速的嵌套切片采样（Nested Slice Sampling）进行贝叶斯推断。

Result: 模型评估显示，在100个未见信号实现中，94个注入轮廓完全落在其对应的95%可信带内；模型评估速度提升达7.2×10⁷倍。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅为21-cm宇宙学提供了高保真、高效推断工具，还为新一代随机采样、基于仿真的推断及变分推断方法提供了严格的后验校准、模态覆盖与证据精度基准。

Abstract: End-to-end uncertainty propagation in scientific inference often requires repeated evaluation of coupled simulators spanning multiple physical domains, often at prohibitive computational cost. Global 21-cm cosmology is an extreme example: a millikelvin cosmological signal must be separated from radio foregrounds and instrumental effects four orders of magnitude larger, making inference acutely sensitive to model misspecification. Reliable recovery is therefore contingent on an exceptionally accurate model that jointly propagates uncertainty through the complete observational chain. We present a differentiable, GPU-accelerated hybrid forward model combining neural emulation of the cosmological signal, a physics-informed multi-resolution parameterisation of the galactic foregrounds, and a linear surrogate model of the antenna. This unifies simulators spanning astrophysics and computational electromagnetics while reducing evaluation time by a factor of $7.2\times10^{7}$. Using GPU-accelerated Nested Slice Sampling, we benchmark the framework across 100 unseen signal realisations and find that 94 injected profiles lie entirely within their corresponding 95% credible bands. Together, the simulator and reference Nested Slice Sampling results provide a demanding benchmark of posterior calibration, mode coverage, and evidence accuracy across next-generation stochastic samplers, simulation-based inference, and variational inference frameworks.

</details>


### [98] [Constraints on ultralight dark matter couplings from orbital perturbations of binary pulsars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30487)
*Federico Huxhagen,Diana López Nacir*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用NANOGrav 15年数据中50个双脉冲星系统的计时数据，通过分析超轻暗物质（标量与矢量场）与普通物质耦合引起的轨道共振效应，对三种耦合模型设定了95%置信度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻暗物质若与普通物质直接耦合，会扰动双脉冲星轨道；当其频率匹配轨道运动谐频时，扰动呈长期增长，可被高精度脉冲星计时探测。

Method: 对质量范围10⁻²³–10⁻¹⁸ eV的标量（线性/二次普适耦合）和矢量（B−L耦合）暗物质，构建其在各双脉冲星系统中引起的轨道摄动计时信号；联合采样耦合强度、局域场振幅/相位/方向及脉冲星红噪声，解析边缘化掉计时模型参数，再合并单颗脉冲星似然得到质量依赖的95%上限。

Result: 线性标量耦合极限在部分质量点接近Cassini实验限制；二次标量耦合在2×10⁻²²–2×10⁻²¹ eV为当前最强限制；矢量耦合在3×10⁻²²–10⁻²¹ eV与PPTA和Eöt-Wash相当，在孤立共振点达到或超越Eöt-Wash，但整体弱于MICROSCOPE。

Conclusion: 双脉冲星轨道共振是探测超轻暗物质耦合的有效新探针，尤其对二次标量耦合在特定质量区间给出迄今最严格限制，验证了脉冲星计时阵列在基础物理探测中的独特潜力。

Abstract: Ultralight dark matter coupled directly to ordinary matter perturbs the orbits of binary pulsars, and the perturbation grows secularly when its frequency matches a harmonic of the orbital motion. We use this effect to constrain three couplings with the wideband timing data for the $50$ binary pulsars of the NANOGrav 15 yr data set: a linear and a quadratic universal coupling of a scalar field to matter, and a vector field coupled to baryon minus lepton number ($B-L$). For every system and for masses between $10^{-23}$ and $10^{-18}\,$eV, we build the timing signature from the perturbed orbital elements. We then sample the coupling together with the local amplitude, phase and direction of the field and the red noise of the pulsar, marginalizing analytically over the timing model, and combine the single-pulsar likelihoods into $95\%$ credible upper limits at each mass. For a universal linear coupling, the orbital resonances of the eccentric systems bring the limit close to the Cassini bound. For the quadratic coupling, treating the neutron star as a point particle, the limits are the strongest available between $2\times10^{-22}$ and $2\times10^{-21}\,$eV. In the case of vector ultralight dark matter, the limit is comparable to the PPTA and Eöt-Wash bounds between $3\times10^{-22}$ and $10^{-21}\,$eV and reaches or beats the Eöt-Wash bound at isolated orbital resonances, while MICROSCOPE remains stronger over the entire mass range.

</details>


### [99] [Redshift-space 3$\times$2pt statistics of galaxy clustering and intrinsic alignments at one-loop order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30596)
*Toshiki Kurita,Atsushi Taruya,Teppei Okumura*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文推导了红移空间中星系密度-形状交叉功率谱和形状自功率谱的一圈阶表达式，比较了基于有效场论（EFT）和Taruya-Nishimichi-Saito（TNS）两种建模方法，并验证其在N体模拟中的适用性，为联合宇宙学分析提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系本征取向（IA）不仅是弱引力透镜的系统误差源，也已成为与星系成团性互补的宇宙学探针；但要在光谱巡天中有效利用IA，亟需超越线性区、适用于红移空间的高精度理论模型。

Method: 在红移空间中，基于有效场论（EFT）和TNS方法，分别构建星系密度–形状交叉功率谱与形状自功率谱的一圈阶摄动理论模型；EFT将视线方向小尺度速度效应展开并引入计数项吸收紫外敏感性，TNS则保留一维速度弥散的非微扰阻尼因子；所有环图积分均通过FFTLog高效计算。

Result: EFT模型对投影形状场需6个重整化偏置参数和2个计数项，随机项至k²阶共需6个参数；TNS模型将速度弥散作为单自由参数，且可自然推广至非线性偏置和自功率谱；两模型在准非线性尺度上均与N体模拟测量结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: 所提出的EFT与TNS红移空间IA模型为融合成像与光谱巡天、将本征取向作为主动宇宙学探针开展联合宇宙学分析（如约束引力理论、暗能量等）提供了坚实可靠的理论工具。

Abstract: Intrinsic alignments of galaxies, long regarded as a systematic in weak lensing, are now recognized as a cosmological probe complementary to galaxy clustering. Exploiting them with spectroscopic surveys requires accurate models in redshift space beyond the linear regime. In this work, we derive the galaxy density--shape cross-power spectrum and shape auto-power spectra at one-loop order in redshift space. We consider two models, one based on the effective field theory (EFT) and the other on the Taruya--Nishimichi--Saito (TNS) prescription. They share the bias expansion but differ in how they treat small-scale velocities along the line of sight. The EFT model expands the effects of these velocities perturbatively and absorbs their sensitivity to small scales into counterterms. We show that, for the sky-projected shape field, this model requires six renormalized bias parameters and two counterterms. Up to order $k^2$, the stochastic terms require two parameters for the cross spectrum and four parameters for the auto spectra. The TNS-based model keeps the one-point velocity dispersion in an unexpanded damping factor and treats it as a single free parameter. We extend our previous linear-bias TNS-based model of the cross spectrum to nonlinear bias and the shape auto spectra. We find that all loop integrals reduce to those already used for shape spectra in real space and galaxy clustering in redshift space, and we evaluate them with FFTLog. We compare both models with power spectra measured from halos in $N$-body simulations and find good agreement on mildly nonlinear scales. These models provide a basis for joint cosmological analyses of galaxy clustering and intrinsic alignments with imaging and spectroscopic galaxy surveys, in which intrinsic alignments are utilized as a cosmological probe.

</details>


### [100] [Accuracy, Convergence and Consistency of Dark Matter Evolution with the PhotoNs-3 Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30647)
*Xiaoyang Zhang,Ming Li,Qiao Wang,Liang Gao*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了PhotoNs-3宇宙学N体模拟代码的精度与收敛性，验证其在力计算和时间演化参数（如软化长度、自适应时间步）下的良好收敛性，并通过与GADGET对比多项统计量（功率谱、晕密度轮廓、晕及子晕质量函数），表明其精度与GADGET-3/4间差异相当，且计算速度显著提升，足以支撑第四阶段星系巡天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估PhotoNs-3代码的精度与可靠性，以确认其是否满足Stage IV星系巡天对高精度宇宙学模拟的需求。

Method: 系统研究PhotoNs-3在软化长度和自适应时间步等关键参数下的收敛性；以GADGET为基准，设计四组模拟，对比密度场功率谱、晕密度剖面、晕及子晕质量函数。

Result: PhotoNs-3表现出良好的收敛性；其与GADGET的结果差异与GADGET-3和GADGET-4之间的差异相当；同时实现显著的计算加速。

Conclusion: PhotoNs-3的精度和可靠性已足够支持Stage IV星系巡天所需的宇宙学模拟。

Abstract: We systematically investigate the accuracy and convergence of PhotoNs-3, a cosmological N-body code employed for the HyperMillennium simulation. In particular, we examine its convergence with respect to parameters that affect the accuracy of the force calculation and the time evolution, such as the softening length and the adaptive time-stepping scheme, and find that it converges very well. Furthermore, using GADGET as the benchmark, we design four sets of simulations to compare the mutual consistency. We compare the power spectrum of the density field, halo density profiles, and the halo and subhalo mass functions. The difference between PhotoNs-3 and the Gadget codes is at the same level as that between Gadget-3 and Gadget-4. Meanwhile, PhotoNs-3 generally achieves a significant computational speedup. This work establishes that the accuracy and reliability of PhotoNs-3 and its simulations are sufficient for Stage IV galaxy surveys.

</details>


### [101] [An Intergalactic medium centred parametrisation of the 21-cm power spectrum from the Epoch of Reionization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30862)
*Ivelin Georgiev,Saleem Zaroubi,Raghunath Ghara,Abinash Kumar Shaw,Garrelt Mellema,Benedetta Ciardi,L. V. E. Koopmans,Anshuman Acharya,Sambit K. Giri,Ilian T. Iliev,Qing-bo Ma*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于星系际介质（IGM）状态的21-cm功率谱模型，结合微扰与渗流理论，以物理驱动方式参数化信号，并能从模拟数据中有效约束IGM关键参数及再电离过程特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 21-cm信号是研究再电离时期（EoR）的关键探针，但传统方法多聚焦于电离源性质建模，缺乏对IGM本身状态的直接、物理驱动的参数化描述。

Method: 构建基于IGM状态（如电离分数、电离/中性区域尺度、晕偏置等）的21-cm功率谱模型，融合微扰理论与渗流理论；通过三套不同设定的再电离数值模拟（C2-Ray、21cmFAST、POLAR）进行验证与参数约束。

Result: 该IGM框架能准确恢复再电离中点与持续时间、最小晕质量M_min、全局电离分数演化，以及电离/中性区域的红移演化（采用Georgiev等人提出的径向平均自由程法）；并支持与高红移星系、类星体及CMB等其他EoR探针联合分析。

Conclusion: 所提IGM中心框架为21-cm信号提供了一种物理直观、可解释性强且可扩展的建模与分析范式，显著补充了源中心的传统方法，提升了对再电离物理的联合约束能力。

Abstract: The 21-cm signal from the intergalactic medium (IGM) is a key probe of the Epoch of Reionization (EoR). In this work, we present a model of the 21-cm power spectrum centred on the state of the IGM. The framework combines aspects of perturbation and percolation theory to deduce a physically informed parametrisation of the power spectrum. Moreover, it complements classical interpretation methods, centred on modelling the properties of the first ionising sources. We define the IGM parameters driving the shape and amplitude of the 21-cm signal, from the globally averaged volume- and mass-weighted ionisation fractions ($\bar{x}_{HII}$ and $\bar{x}_{HIIm}$), the clustering of sources, through the halo bias ($b_{\mathrm{h}}$), and the characteristic sizes of ionised regions ($R_{i}$) and neutral islands ($R_{n}$). We extend this analysis to model the redshift dependence of the power spectrum, constraining parameters such as the midpoint and duration of reionization. We test the IGM framework on three reionization simulations (C2-Ray, 21cmFAST, and POLAR) with varying cosmologies, astrophysics, and numerical assumptions. The framework constrains the IGM parameter space, recovers the midpoint and duration of the EoR as well as the minimum mass of halos hosting ionising sources $M_{\mathrm{min}}$, and the redshift evolution of the global ionisation fraction, the evolution of ionised and neutral regions (measured with the radial mean free path method by Georgiev et al. (2026b, submitted)). Moreover, the IGM framework lends itself to joint analysis with other EoR probes, such as high-redshift galaxies and quasars and the Cosmic Microwave Background.

</details>


### [102] [The Simons Observatory: Analysis of methods for polarization calibration of multiple instruments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31235)
*The Simons Observatory Collaboration,Anto I. Lonappan,Patricia Diego-Palazuelos,Carlos Hervías-Caimapo,Kam Arnold,Susanna Azzoni,Carlo Baccigalupi,Matteo Billi,Erminia Calabrese,Yuji Chinone,Matthew Chu,Mark Devlin,Josquin Errard,Nicholas Galitzki,Bradley R. Johnson,Brian Keating,Andréa Landais,Toshiya Namikawa,Federico Nati,Giuseppe Puglisi,Christian L. Reichardt,Kevin Wolz*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Simons Observatory（SO）不同仪器间传递绝对偏振角校准的方法，以提升宇宙双折射（cosmic birefringence）测量的精度，并区分不同新物理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态各向同性偏振角旋转受限于仪器自身的绝对偏振角校准精度，需借助跨仪器校准传递来突破该限制，从而更灵敏地探测超越标准模型的宇称破缺物理。

Method: 开发了两种校准传递方法：一是基于LAT与SAT观测区域重叠的联合似然方法；二是基于Minami-Komatsu思想的前景分离法，利用银河系尘埃偏振无需外部角度先验即可分离宇宙双折射与仪器校准误差。同时推导了通用方差预算以评估校准传递的有效条件。

Result: 校准传递虽不降低各向同性β的绝对角度误差下限，但可锚定接收仪器（如SAT）的多极矩范围；相对角度误差达~0.1°（ℓ_max=300）至~0.04°（ℓ_max=600）；并证明LAT的ℓ范围可借C_ℓ^{EB}区分尺度无关的暗能量旋转与尺度相关的轴子暗物质模型。

Conclusion: 校准传递是提升SO宇宙双折射探测能力的关键技术，尤其在前景主导和高精度角度控制前提下，为区分新物理模型提供了可观测依据。

Abstract: Cosmic birefringence, the rotation of the CMB linear polarization plane across the Universe, is a powerful test of parity-violating physics beyond the Standard Model. Static isotropic rotation, however, is limited by an instrument's absolute polarization-angle calibration. We present a method for transferring absolute calibration between Simons Observatory (SO) instruments, validated on map-level simulations of the Large Aperture Telescope (LAT) and Small Aperture Telescopes (SATs) with lensed CMB polarization, polarized Galactic foregrounds, correlated instrumental noise, and polarization-angle miscalibration. We develop two pipelines: a joint LAT+SAT likelihood transferring calibration through their overlap, and the Minami-Komatsu foreground-based method, which separates cosmic birefringence from instrumental miscalibration using Galactic dust polarization without an external angle prior. We derive a general variance budget showing that calibration transfer improves sensitivity only when the combined reference and overlap transfer uncertainties remain below the receiving instrument's calibration uncertainty. While transfer does not lower the absolute-angle floor for isotropic $β$, it anchors the receiving instrument's multipole range. The relative-angle uncertainty is $\sim 0.1^\circ$ for $\ell_{\max}^{\mathrm{SAT}}=300$, improving to $\sim 0.04^\circ$ at $\ell_{\max}^{\mathrm{SAT}}=600$, subject to SAT beam and transfer-function control. Finally, for $β=0.3^\circ$, we show how the LAT's $\ell$-range distinguishes scale-independent dark-energy rotation from scale-dependent axion dark-matter models via $C_\ell^{EB}$.

</details>


### [103] [Radial mean free path bubble size distribution (RMFP-BSD) from the Epoch of Reionization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30863)
*Ivelin Georgiev,Robert Crawford,Saleem Zaroubi,Raghunath Ghara,Abinash Kumar Shaw,Garrelt Mellema,Benedetta Ciardi,L. V. E. Koopmans,Anshuman Acharya,Sambit K. Giri,Ilian T. Iliev,Qing-bo Ma*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的统计方法——径向平均自由程（rmfp）法，用于表征再电离时期（EoR）电离区域的结构特征，该方法在保持与Mesinger等人2007年方法密切关联的同时，具备解析可解性和更低的离散性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平均自由程气泡尺寸分布方法离散性高且难以解析处理，需一种更稳健、可解析、能更好刻画电离区重叠、中性岛结构及21-cm功率谱特征的新统计量。

Method: 提出径向平均自由程（rmfp）方法：在三维模拟体积中从随机原点沿六个笛卡尔轴方向发射射线，直至抵达中性氢岛；结合原有方法进行对比与协同分析。

Result: rmfp方法能更准确量化EoR期间电离区重叠效应，识别孤立或连通的中性岛团块，并有效刻画电离功率谱的特征峰；对EoR末期中性HI岛性质也具信息量；双方法联合使用可增强对再电离形态学的理解。

Conclusion: rmfp是一种解析可行、低离散、物理意义明确的新型统计工具，显著提升了对再电离结构和21-cm信号形态的定量分析能力，尤其适用于数值模拟与观测数据比对。

Abstract: Among the many statistics used to characterise ionised regions during the Epoch of Reionization (EoR), we introduce one which, while closely related to the mean free path bubble size distribution method from Mesinger et al. 2007, is analytically tractable and substantially less dispersive than that method. The radial mean free path (rmfp) method casts six rays along the Cartesian axes in a 3D volume from a random origin to a neutral hydrogen island. This allows us to better quantify the role of overlap during the EoR, identify isolated or interconnected clusters in the simulation, and study the characteristic peak in the ionisation power spectrum, which is important for 21-cm studies of the intergalactic medium. Moreover, we find the method informative about the properties of neutral H I islands during the final phases of the EoR. We also show that applying both methods in tandem can further improve our insight into the morphology of reionization.

</details>


### [104] [Extragalactic carbon-monoxide (CO) as a CMB foreground: an analytic halo model for the CO auto-spectrum and its correlation with the Cosmic Infrared Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30959)
*Giorgia Zagatti,Marian Douspis,Giulio Fabbian,Laura Salvati*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个用于分析CO线辐射与CIB交叉功率谱的解析晕模型框架，评估其对高分辨率CMB实验（如ACT和CMB-HD）的影响，发现CO×CIB是不可忽略的小尺度前景，并建议在未来的CMB分析中必须包含CO成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CO谱线在星系中发射后红移到CMB观测频段，其与CIB的相关性可能构成显著的小尺度前景污染，需建模以避免对宇宙学参数估计造成偏差。

Method: 基于光度加权晕模型，区分中心星系与卫星星系的占据分布，涵盖最高J=13的CO跃迁及所有自相关与交叉频谱；CO与红外光度均源自同一恒星形成率；并使用Fisher矩阵方法对ACT类和CMB-HD类实验进行误差预测与偏差分析。

Result: 在ℓ=3000处，CO×CIB贡献达0.4–1.9 μK²（90–220 GHz），CO自谱约0.02 μK²；ACT实验中CO参数无法约束，但忽略CO会使射电和SZ参数偏移~2σ和~1σ；CMB-HD实验中CO可高显著性探测，且能约束最小晕质量，忽略CO则导致宇宙学参数最大偏移~2σ。

Conclusion: 未来高分辨率CMB分析必须将CO纳入前景模型；本文提出的解析晕模型框架能一致描述CO与CIB，并已开源实现代码。

Abstract: Context. Carbon Monoxide (CO) lines emitted by star-forming galaxies are redshifted into the frequency bands of the Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) experiments. Their correlation with the Cosmic Infrared Background (CIB) may be a non-negligible small-scale foreground. Aims. We develop an analytic, full-shape halo model framework for the CO auto-spectrum and for the CO$\times$CIB cross-spectrum in CMB bands, and assess its impact on current and future high-resolution CMB experiments. Methods. We use a luminosity-weighted halo model with separate occupation distributions of central and satellite galaxies for CO and CIB emitters, CO transitions up to $J=13$, and all auto- and cross-frequency spectra. Line and infrared luminosities derive from the same star formation rate. We perform Fisher analysis for ACT-like and CMB-HD-like experiments. Results. At $\ell=3000$, the CO$\times$CIB contribution to the sky power is 0.4, 0.7, and 1.9 $μ$K$^2$ at 90, 150, and 220 GHz, and the CO auto-spectrum is $\simeq0.02\,μ$K$^2$. For an ACT-like experiment, the CO occupation parameters are essentially unconstrained, and neglecting CO leaves the cosmological parameters unbiased, but shifts the radio and SZ parameters by $\sim2σ$ and $\simeq1σ$, respectively. For a CMB-HD-like experiment, CO is detected with high significance and the minimum halo masses of CO and CIB emitters are constrained; neglecting CO biases the cosmological parameters up to $\sim2σ$ when all multipoles are used in the analysis. Conclusions. CO should be included in the foreground model of future high-resolution CMB analyses; our framework describes it analytically and consistently with the CIB, and the code implementing it is publicly available.

</details>


### [105] [Closure of averaged Einstein equations by DESI data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31041)
*Thomas Buchert*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在广义相对论框架下，无需引入暗能量即可解释DESI观测到的红移依赖性，提出通过非微扰处理宇宙不均匀性（如‘形态子’流体类比）来替代暗能量状态方程，并验证其与观测兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准宇宙学模型中暗能量的红移依赖性是否可由不含暗能量、仅基于广义相对论的结构形成模型自然导出。

Method: 基于平均尘埃宇宙学理论，利用不均匀几何的非微扰效应构建有效均场描述（引入‘形态子’标量场类比），在不违反能量条件前提下实现向幻影行为的过渡，并用DESI三年数据进行单闭合条件参数化拟合。

Result: 成功再现了与DESI观测一致的有效宇宙学参数红移依赖关系，表明暗能量现象可能源于几何不均匀性的平均效应。

Conclusion: 不含暗能量的广义相对论结构形成模型，在适当闭合条件下可作为解释当前观测的基准模型；未来相关理论或数值模拟需满足该基准。

Abstract: We benchmark the hypothesis that the redshift dependence of postulated dark energy in the standard model, measured by the DESI collaboration, is a natural outcome of dark energy-free models of structure formation in general relativity. We recall fundamental results and the properties of averaged dust cosmologies that suggest to replace the dark energy equation of state through the nonperturbative impact of the inhomogeneous geometry. The relevant mechanism can be phrased in terms of an effective mean field description of inhomogeneities using the fluid analogy of a scalar field (the `morphon'). Without violating energy conditions this effective field exhibits a cross-over to phantom behaviour, compatible with observations. The exact dark energy-free framework needs a single closure condition that we implement in terms of parametrizations of the 3-years data of the DESI team. The resulting redshift dependence of effective cosmological characteristics is then illustrated and subject to physical interpretation. The aim of this paper is to set a benchmark that has to be met by any structure formation model or simulation investigated within general relativity in a flow-orthogonal foliation of spacetime, given the hypothesis.

</details>


### [106] [A hybrid effective field theory description of diffuse baryons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31069)
*Ryan Raikman,Kiyoshi Masui,James M. Sullivan,Josh Borrow*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合有效场论（HEFT）方法，仅用两个参数（反馈抑制参数 $c_f^2$ 和线性偏置 $b_{1,d}^L$）高精度拟合弥散气体功率谱，显著提升对星系反馈和宇宙学参数的联合约束能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系反馈机制在大尺度上驱离重子气体，是当前宇宙学分析中的主要不确定性来源；现有解析建模方法要么局限于大尺度（信噪比低），要么参数过多削弱约束力。

Method: 提出Hybrid Effective Field Theory（HEFT）方法：基于N体模拟计算的完整引力位移场，用单参数洛伦兹型抑制描述反馈效应（$c_f^2$）；借鉴Andrew et al.工作，利用多数重子处于弥散态的特性，将偏置展开简化为仅含线性偏置 $b_{1,d}^L$。

Result: 在FLAMINGO模拟套件多种模型中，仅用两个参数即可在z=0.5时达到k=1 h/Mpc、z=1时k=1.5 h/Mpc范围内对弥散气体功率谱实现百分比级精度拟合；验证了被忽略项贡献极小。

Conclusion: 该HEFT框架能同时约束宇宙学与天体物理参数，无需边缘化大量未知参数，为FRB DM和kSZ等重子探针提供简洁、强约束的理论建模工具。

Abstract: Galaxy feedback mechanisms that displace baryonic gas on large scales are a dominant uncertainty in many cosmological analyses. Measurements that directly probe the baryonic component, such as Fast Radio Burst Dispersion Measure (FRB DM) and the kinetic Sunyaev-Zeldovich effect, can therefore be used to place constraints on feedback and inform other analyses, such as weak lensing. Thus far, analytic methods to model the effects of feedback are either limited to large scales, missing the regime of high signal-to-noise, or have many parameters, weakening constraining power. We propose a Hybrid Effective Field Theory (HEFT) approach, relying on the full gravitational displacement field computed in an $N$-body simulation. We account for feedback via a Lorentzian suppression with a single effective parameter, $c_f^2$. To describe the diffuse baryon field, we extend the arguments by Andrew et al. (arXiv:2604.25828), leveraging most baryons being in a diffuse state to reduce a full bias expansion to just the linear bias, $b_{1,d}^L$. With just these two parameters, we fit the diffuse gas power spectrum in most variants of the FLAMINGO suite of cosmological simulations at percent-level accuracy out to $k=1\,h/\mathrm{Mpc}$ at redshift $z=0.5$ and $k=1.5\,h/\mathrm{Mpc}$ at $z=1$. Further, by fitting the diffuse gas power spectrum including terms argued to be negligible, we show that their contribution is minimal. Applying this prescription for diffuse electron gas enables constraints on both cosmology and astrophysics without the need to marginalize over many unknown parameters.

</details>


### [107] [Interpretable morphology-based marked statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31167)
*Federico Semenzato,Benjamin D. Wandelt,Michele Liguori,Alvise Raccanelli*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释、旋转等变的神经标记方法，通过重加权物质场并结合密度响应与局部形态度量，提升宇宙学参数（如σ₈和Ωₘ）的约束精度，优于传统功率谱与 bispectrum，并在中微子质量预测中也表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙结构的形状和环境蕴含超越物质功率谱的宇宙学信息，但如何在保持物理可解释性的同时有效提取这些信息是当前宇宙学推断的核心挑战。

Method: 构建一种可解释、旋转等变的神经标记方案，对物质场进行重加权，融合学习到的密度响应与显式局部形态度量；采用对比学习框架将标记后的统计量与宇宙学参数对齐。

Result: 在实空间物质模拟中，所提方法使σ₈和Ωₘ的Fisher约束分别比功率谱+双谱强1.7倍和1.5倍（k_max=0.3 h/Mpc）；无需重训练即可使中微子质量预报精度提升1.5倍；在k_max=0.2 h/Mpc下，参数预测误差比最优经典标记降低1.3倍。

Conclusion: 神经标记统计为从巡天数据中提取非高斯信息提供了新路径，兼具物理可解释性与两点函数估计器的实用性。

Abstract: The shapes and environments of cosmic structures carry cosmological information beyond the matter power spectrum. Recovering this information while retaining a physical understanding is a central challenge for cosmological inference. We construct an interpretable, rotation-equivariant neural marking scheme that reweights the matter field, combining learned density responses with explicit measures of local morphology. This approach enables direct inspection of the learned transformations, while retaining the advantages of a two-point function analysis. We employ a contrastive learning framework to align the marked summaries with the respective cosmological parameters. In real-space matter simulations, the learned marked spectra recover information beyond the ordinary power spectrum, tightening marginalized Fisher constraints on $σ_8$ and $Ω_m$ by factors of $1.7\times$ and $1.5\times$ relative to the power spectrum plus bispectrum at $k_{\mathrm{max}}=0.3\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$. Without retraining, our mark also improves a neutrino-mass forecast by a factor of $1.5\times$ relative to the power spectrum at the same scale cut. Across the cosmological prior, our marking scheme also improve the prediction errors in both parameters by $1.3\times$ over the best tested classical mark at $k_{\mathrm{max}}=0.2\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$. A qualitative comparison with neural forecasts motivates the possibility that these different compressions approach a common information level. Neural marked statistics thus offer a route to extracting non-Gaussian information from cosmological surveys, combining interpretable, physically motivated transformations with the practical advantages of two-point estimators.

</details>


### [108] [Four consistent estimates of $H_0$ from a $Λ$CDM inference network given CMB data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31183)
*Gabriel P. Lynch,Lloyd Knox*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过构建一个基于爱因斯坦-玻尔兹曼方程尺度对称性的宇宙微波背景（CMB）数据推断网络，揭示了ΛCDM模型中H₀值四种独立推导路径之间的一致性，强调该一致性是ΛCDM与CMB数据高度兼容的关键特征，并指出这种冗余性为用替代理论解决H₀张力带来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前宇宙学中哈勃常数（H₀）测量值在不同方法间存在显著分歧（即H₀张力）的问题，探究ΛCDM模型在CMB数据下的内在一致性及其对张力解释的限制。

Method: 利用爱因斯坦-玻尔兹曼演化方程的尺度对称性，将CMB数据对H₀的各类推断组织成一个结构化网络，识别并分析从校准锚点到H₀的四条独立推导路径。

Result: 在ΛCDM模型关键假设下，四条路径得出的H₀值相互一致；这种一致性凸显了ΛCDM与CMB数据的高度兼容性，并表明H₀推断的冗余性构成替代模型缓解H₀张力的主要障碍。

Conclusion: H₀推断网络的内部一致性是ΛCDM模型成功解释CMB数据的重要体现，也意味着仅修改模型某些部分可能不足以解决H₀张力，需更根本的理论调整或系统误差再评估。

Abstract: We organize inferences from cosmic microwave background (CMB) data, of the current rate of expansion, $H_0$, into a network whose structure is informed by a scaling symmetry of the Einstein-Boltzmann evolution equations. There are four distinct routes through this network from a calibrating anchor to $H_0$. Given key assumptions of the $Λ$CDM model, they are mutually consistent. This consistency is a significant feature of the $Λ$CDM model's compatibility with CMB data. The redundancy in $H_0$ determinations illuminates a hurdle to resolving the $H_0$ tension with alternative cosmological models.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

### [109] [Spherical orbits around a Newman-Janis-generated rotating Bronnikov black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28074)
*F. Subandi,H. S. Ramadhan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由磁荷Bronnikov质量分布通过Newman-Janis方法生成的旋转正则时空中的球形测地线，分析其光子球、阴影、类时轨道稳定性及能量条件，验证其满足Maeda提出的五条物理合理无奇点黑洞几何判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建并检验一个物理上合理的、无奇点的旋转正则黑洞模型，满足基本几何与物理约束（如能量条件、因果性、曲率有界等）。

Method: 基于Newman-Janis算法从Bronnikov磁荷质量分布构造旋转正则度规；分析Kretschmann标量行为；求解球形光子/类时测地线并判断径向稳定性；检验四大能量条件（null, weak, strong, dominant）；对照Maeda提出的C1–C5判据进行系统评估。

Result: 该度规中心曲率光滑有限（Kretschmann标量指数压制），事件视界外光子球全为径向不稳定（决定黑洞阴影，边缘观测下比Kerr更小且畸变）；无视界构型中可存在稳定光子球；类时运动存在ISSO；强场区存在能量条件违反，但渐近及所有黑洞外部区域均满足全部四类能量条件；同时满足稳定因果性与曲率有界性。

Conclusion: 该Newman-Janis-Boosted Bronnikov旋转正则度规满足Maeda定义的全部五个物理合理性判据（C1–C5），是当前一个具有良好理论基础的无奇点黑洞候选模型。

Abstract: We investigate spherical geodesics in a Newman--Janis-generated rotating regular geometry constructed from the magnetically-charged Bronnikov mass profile. The Kretschmann scalar is exponentially suppressed at the center, and is smooth across the disk and ring within the single-sheeted domain $r\geq0$. We show that all physically admissible spherical photon orbits outside the event horizon are radially unstable in the black-hole regime, whereas stable spherical photon orbits can exist in horizonless configurations. The unstable photon orbits determine the shadow, which for an edge-on observer is smaller and distorted compared with the Kerr shadow. For timelike motion, we identify marginally stable spherical orbits and determine the innermost stable spherical orbit (ISSO). We further examine the null, weak, strong, and dominant energy conditions, finding violations in the strong-field region but satisfaction of all four conditions asymptotically and throughout the exterior of every black-hole configuration. Together with stable causality and the curvature bound, these results show that the metric satisfies the five geometric criteria C1--C5 proposed by Maeda for physically reasonable nonsingular black holes.

</details>


### [110] [Curvature-history tensor memory in inflationary cosmology: Component closure, ghost-free dynamics, and CMB BipoSH likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28106)
*G. M. Rhythm*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种广义协变暴胀模型，利用四维Gauss-Bonnet修正的Kretschmann曲率作为源，激发对称无迹张量记忆序参量，导致原初统计各向异性出现尺度依赖的四极偏离。通过精确处理ADM约束与延迟响应变量，导出了主导各向异性曲率作用量，并证明该模型在非线性分析下远离Ostrogradsky不稳定性阈值；模型产生红外正则的振荡型原初四极矩和精确尺度依赖的T/E双球谐协方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准暴胀模型中难以自然产生可观测的原初各向异性（如CMB四极矩异常）的问题，同时避免高阶导数理论带来的Ostrogradsky不稳定性。

Method: 构建基于四维Gauss-Bonnet曲率修正的广义协变暴胀模型；引入空间对称无迹张量记忆序参量Q_{μν}及其指数型场打扮算符E_C；采用延迟因果框架与固定Cauchy初值消除齐次模；通过精确块Schur补消去ADM约束与响应变量；结合非线性ADM Legendre分析检验稳定性；推导原初功率谱及T/E双球谐（BipoSH）协方差。

Result: 得到红外正则、振荡型原初四极矩；获得精确尺度依赖的T/E BipoSH协方差；构造了对应的截断天球（cut-sky）高斯与二次最大似然估计器；证明模型在代表性暴胀解下远离Ostrogradsky不稳定性阈值。

Conclusion: 该模型为原初各向异性提供了一个稳定、协变且可检验的几何起源机制，将曲率历史与张量记忆直接联系起来，兼具理论自洽性与观测可证伪性。

Abstract: We present a generally covariant inflationary model in which curvature history activates a spatial symmetric-tracefree tensor-memory order parameter and generates a scale-dependent quadrupolar departure from primordial statistical isotropy. The curvature source is the four-dimensional Gauss--Bonnet completion of the Kretschmann invariant. A local $(C,χ)$ representation is used only to implement the retarded theory: fixed retarded Cauchy data remove the homogeneous response modes, so the negative-sign localized combination is not an independent phase-space degree of freedom. The projected tensor sector is described by the dressed field $Q_{μν}=\mathcal E_C\widehat Q_{μν}$ with $\mathcal E_C=\exp[B(C)\Box_{\widehat Q}/2]$. Its raw-field Hessian has the exact congruence form $\mathcal E_C^\dagger(\Box_Q-\mathsf M^2)\mathcal E_C$, so an invertible dressing preserves the solution count and the sign of the physical symplectic norm even when $[B(C),\Box_Q]\ne0$.
  We derive the full quadratic Hessian, eliminate the ADM constraints and retarded response variables through an exact block Schur complement, and obtain the leading anisotropic curvature action. A nonlinear ADM Legendre analysis shows that the representative inflationary solution remains far from the singular threshold associated with an Ostrogradsky branch. A smooth curvature-dependent phase potential gives an approximately constant activated plateau with subdominant memory energy. Finite activation then produces an infrared-regular oscillatory primordial quadrupole and an exact scale-dependent $T/E$ BipoSH covariance. We also give the corresponding cut-sky Gaussian and quadratic maximum-likelihood estimators.

</details>


### [111] [Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity corrections in Dark Dimension scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28132)
*George K. Leontaris,George Prampromis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了暗维度场景中爱因斯坦-高斯-博内特（EGB）黑洞的热力学、动力学稳定性及现象学意义，重点分析了5维和6维情况下黑洞的质量、霍金温度、Wald熵与热容，并探讨其作为冷暗物质候选者及在对撞机实验中的可观测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高维引力理论（特别是EGB理论）中微观额外维度下的黑洞物理，评估其作为冷暗物质的可能性，并寻找区别于广义相对论的独特实验信号。

Method: 基于Boulware-Deser解，推导任意维D≥5下EGB黑洞的热力学量（质量、温度、熵、热容）；结合弦论紫外截断分析稳定性；构建黑体辐射模型计算寿命标度；估算对撞机中产生截面与衰变多重数。

Result: 发现仅在D=5时存在正热容分支，对应稳定微型黑洞，质量极限约1.06×10⁻¹² g，可全部构成冷暗物质，并预言33 THz引力波峰及被压制的霍金爆发；D=6时热容恒负，寿命缩放为τ∝M₀³（而非广义相对论的M₀⁵⁄₃），且对撞机中产额能量依赖更陡、平均衰变粒子数仅约4。

Conclusion: 5维EGB黑洞在合理参数下可成为稳定的暗物质候选者，而5维与6维情形在热力学与实验信号上的鲜明差异，为探测额外维度和迈向弦尺度提供了清晰路径。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamics, dynamical stability, and phenomenological implications of Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet (EGB) black holes in Dark Dimension scenarios with one or two micron-sized extra dimensions. For the asymptotically flat, neutral Boulware-Deser solution with positive Gauss-Bonnet coupling $α$, we derive the mass, Hawking temperature, Wald entropy, and heat capacity in arbitrary dimension $D\ge 5$. A positive-heat-capacity branch arises only in $D=5$, where the temperature peaks at $T_{\max}=1/(8π\sqrtα)$ at $r_h=2\sqrtα$ and vanishes in the zero-radius limit at finite mass $M_{\rm lim}=3πα/(4G_5)$. For $G_5=(8πM_*^3)^{-1}$, $α=M_*^{-2}$, and $M_*\sim 10^{10}$ GeV, this yields $M_{\rm lim}\simeq 1.06\times 10^{-12}$ g. While this branch formally overlaps with classical instability zones, the string ultraviolet cutoff safely suppresses the responsible high-harmonic modes. Assuming a UV completion stabilizes these relics, we show they can constitute all of cold dark matter. We map their observational signatures, highlighting a scalar-induced gravitational-wave peak at 33 THz and the suppression of Hawking bursts. In $D=6$, the heat capacity is negative at all radii. Within a bulk blackbody emission model, we derive the leading large-mass lifetime correction and the small-radius scaling $τ\propto M_0^3$, compared to $τ_{\rm GR}\propto M_0^{5/3}$. For $M_*\sim 10$ TeV, we identify distinct collider benchmarks: a steeper energy scaling for the production cross section and a suppressed decay multiplicity ($\langle N \rangle \sim 4$). These provide clear experimental signatures distinguishing 6D EGB gravity from classical General Relativity, mapping the transition toward the string scale.

</details>


### [112] [Generalization of the Theory of Relativity Based on Finsler Geometry, Incorporating a Fundamental Mass at High Energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28141)
*Rustam Ibadov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推广了狭义和广义相对论，引入一个不变的基本质量M作为粒子能量上限，并基于Finsler几何（特别是Randers度量）构建了一个具有两个不变量（光速c和基本质量M）的新相对论框架；该理论保持光传播不变，但修正了有质量粒子的动力学与引力行为，并给出可观测的实验检验途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现Kadyshevsky关于存在基本质量尺度的假设提供几何实现与可检验性，同时统一处理相对论中光速c与新质量尺度M的双重不变性。

Method: 通过projectively变形质量壳的精确Legendre变换，导出描述粒子运动的Lorentzian Randers型Finsler度量F_μ；将其推广至引力场，构造Finsler–Einstein张量方程并求解宇宙学解。

Result: 得到具有μ=m/M修正项的Friedmann方程（含μḢ项），de Sitter时空仍为精确解；预言有质量粒子能量上限E<M、修正的时间膨胀及组成依赖的自由下落偏差η≈Δm/M；光传播与标准相对论一致。

Conclusion: 该Finsler几何框架成功将基本质量M纳入相对论基础，兼具理论自洽性与可观测性，为超越标准模型与量子引力的实验探索提供了新方向。

Abstract: We generalize special and general relativity so that, besides the speed of light $c$, an invariant fundamental mass $M$ bounds the energy of every massive particle. The exact Legendre transform of a projectively deformed mass shell gives, for a particle of mass $m$, a closed-form Finsler function $F_μ$, $μ=m/M$: a Lorentzian Randers metric built from the spacetime metric $g$ and a unit timelike covector $u$. Its indicatrix is the Minkowski hyperboloid displaced by $W=μu/(1+μ)$, so $F_μ$ is a Zermelo navigation metric whose symmetry group, the Lorentz group centred at $W$, realizes a relativity principle with two invariants, $c$ and $M$. Light propagates exactly as in Einstein's theory, while massive particles obey $E<M$, a deformed time-dilation law and a composition-dependent free fall, $η\simeqΔm/M$. The Finsler--Einstein tensor equation, solved exactly for a spatially flat universe, gives Friedmann equations with corrections $\proptoμ\dot H$ and leaves de~Sitter space exact. Observed particle energies, equivalence-principle tests and electron-EDM limits constrain $M$. The construction makes Kadyshevsky's fundamental-mass hypothesis geometric and testable.

</details>


### [113] [Imprints of perfect fluid dark matter distributions and topological defects on Born--Infeld AdS black hole signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28143)
*Faizuddin Ahmed,Ahmad Al-Badawi,İzzet Sakallı*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个带有拓扑缺陷（宇宙弦或全局单极子）并浸没在幂律暗物质（PFDM）中的静态球对称BI-AdS黑洞解，系统研究了其几何、光学、动力学及热力学性质，并通过多信道观测数据拟合探讨参数敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义引力模型中多种物理效应（非线性电动力学、暗物质晕、宇宙学常数、拓扑缺陷）共存时对黑洞结构与可观测现象的联合影响，填补多源变形耦合研究的空白。

Method: 解析构造含四个独立变形参数（缺陷角亏ξ、BI尺度β、PFDM强度λ、AdS曲率Λ）的精确黑洞度规；计算其视界、光子球、阴影、ISCO、振荡频率、标量势、QNMs（WKB高阶）、GBF及扩展热力学相变行为；结合MCMC方法拟合五类吸积源的时变观测数据。

Result: 发现所有关键半径（视界、光子球、阴影）随缺陷强度ξ单调增大；相对论进动预言的双峰QPO与观测吻合；标量微扰的势垒与QNM谱随ξ软化；热力学呈现类似范德瓦尔斯的P–V临界行为，临界点受ξ、β、λ共同调制；四类物理效应在成像、计时、ringdown和热辐射中产生可区分的参数敏感性印记。

Conclusion: 该模型为统一刻画强场引力中多种新物理提供了可行框架；各变形参数具有独立可观测效应，但需联合多信道数据分析才能实现真正意义上的物理机制分离。

Abstract: We construct a static, spherically symmetric \BI{} \AdS{} \BH{} that carries a topological defect, either a \GM{} or a \CS{}, and is immersed in \PFDM{}. The metric function collects four independent deformations of the Schwarzschild geometry: a constant solid-angle deficit $(1-ξ)$ from the defect, the nonlinear charge sector controlled by the \BI{} scale $β$, the logarithmic \PFDM{} term set by the intensity $λ$, and the \AdS{} curvature $Λ$. We verify that the solution reduces to the charged \AdS{}--\PFDM{} defect geometry as $β\to \infty$ and to Schwarzschild when all deformations switch off. The horizon, photon sphere, and shadow all grow monotonically with the defect strength $ξ$. We compute the innermost stable circular orbit and the epicyclic frequencies, read off the relativistic-precession predictions for twin-peak quasi-periodic oscillations, and confront them with five accreting sources through a Markov chain Monte Carlo fit. For spin-0 perturbations we obtain the scalar potential, the higher-order WKB \QNM{} spectrum, and the \GBF{}, all of which soften as $ξ$ rises. The extended phase space analysis yields a \VdW{}-type $P$--$V$ structure whose critical point shifts with the defect, the \BI{} scale, and the halo intensity. Throughout, the four sectors leave distinct imprints on imaging, timing, ringdown, and thermal channels, {\color{black} which we read as parameter sensitivities rather than as an observational separation of the four, a distinction a joint multichannel fit would be needed to settle}.

</details>


### [114] [Stationary branches of non-canonical spinor theories and their cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28149)
*Ugo Moschella,Mario Novello,Oliver F. Piattella*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了与引力最小耦合的非规范自旋场，发现其场方程存在两个解分支：正则分支满足无质量狄拉克方程并贡献由拉格朗日常数决定的真空能；稳态分支下自旋场方程恒成立、能量动量张量恒为宇宙学常数形式，演化不唯一；在宇宙学背景下，该模型可精确复现ΛCDM膨胀历史，并使真空能仍由拉格朗日加性常数控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非规范自旋场作为暗能量或宇宙学常数起源的可能性，解决标准模型中真空能问题及自旋场动力学不明确的问题。

Method: 基于含自旋子动能标量和标量双线性项的非规范拉格朗日量，推导引力-自旋场耦合的运动方程，分析解的结构（正则分支与稳态分支），并在FLRW宇宙学背景下研究其动力学与状态方程。

Result: 发现两类解分支：正则分支给出无质量狄拉克解和固定真空能；稳态分支导致任意自旋构型均产生等效宇宙学常数，且引入势能后可自然导出压强为零的组分，使背景演化精确匹配ΛCDM。

Conclusion: 非规范自旋场提供了一种新颖机制，可在保持真空能由拉格朗日常数调控的同时，复现标准ΛCDM宇宙学行为，为理解暗能量和宇宙加速提供新视角。

Abstract: We study a classical spinor field minimally coupled to gravity and described by a non-canonical Lagrangian depending on the fermionic kinetic scalar and the scalar bilinear. For purely kinetic theories, we show that the field equations split the solutions into two distinct branches. On the regular branch, the spinor satisfies the massless Dirac equation and contributes a vacuum energy determined by an additive constant in the Lagrangian. On the stationary branch, the spinor field equations become identically satisfied, while the energy-momentum tensor is exactly that of a cosmological constant for any spinor configuration and in any spacetime. The spinor evolution is therefore not uniquely determined by the field equations. In a spatially flat homogeneous universe, both branches act as cosmological-constant sources. We also give the dilution law for homogeneous configurations in theories with explicit dependence on the scalar bilinear. When a potential with a nonzero minimum is introduced, the stationary configuration becomes the late-time state of the cosmological evolution, and the approach to it generates a pressureless component in addition to an effective cosmological constant. For a simple quadratic model, the background expansion reproduces exactly that of the standard $Λ$CDM cosmology, while the value of the vacuum energy remains controlled by an additive constant in the Lagrangian.

</details>


### [115] [Primordial black holes production in Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28150)
*Ekaterina O. Pozdeeva,Sergey Yu. Vernov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种含标量场依赖的高斯-博内项的新暴胀模型，能产生原初黑洞（PBHs），其质量范围可能解释全部或部分暗物质，并与阿塔卡马宇宙学望远镜（ACT）观测数据相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解释暗物质起源并兼容CMB观测数据，探索能生成原初黑洞的暴胀机制。

Method: 构建标量场耦合高斯-博内项的暴胀模型，分析其动力学行为与功率谱特征，估算PBH质量分布及丰度。

Result: 模型参数适当时可同时满足ACT观测约束，并生成质量在暗物质可行窗口内的PBHs。

Conclusion: 该模型为PBH作为暗物质候选者提供了自洽的暴胀起源机制，具有可观测检验性。

Abstract: We consider models with the Gauss-Bonnet term multiplied to a function of the scalar field and propose a new inflationary model which provides a mechanism for the formation of primordial black holes (PBHs). For certain values of the model parameters, the predictions of this model agree with observational data from the Atacama Cosmology Telescope (ACT). Due to estimation of possiable PBHs masses, we demonstrate that they can be considered as a possible source for all or part of dark matter.

</details>


### [116] [Optical appearance of a non-commutative black hole surrounded by an accretion disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28151)
*Adailton Azevêdo Araújo Filho,Narges Heidari,Alesandro Ferreira dos Santos,Shahin Mamedov,Valdir Barbosa Bezerra,Amilcar Rabelo de Queiroz,Ali Övgün*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非交换Schwarzschild黑洞周围薄吸积盘的光学外观，通过计算光子轨迹、盘转移函数和观测到的总光度强度，分析了非交换参数θ对黑洞阴影、轨道结构及辐射特征的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非交换几何效应对黑洞强引力场中辐射观测特征的影响，特别是其对黑洞阴影、吸积盘辐射形态及多普勒/红移效应的修正。

Method: 基于Lorentzian物质分布构建有效外部度规；数值计算光子测地线、盘转移函数；考虑引力红移、轨道时间延缓与多普勒调制，分析静态与圆周发射源的观测强度。

Result: 随非交换参数θ增大（0→0.03），临界撞击参数减小16.1%，最内稳定圆轨道半径减小23.7%；高阶环状结构收缩，主峰位移<1%；峰值强度与总通量分别增加6.5%和4.3%；不同径向发射剖面导致中心亮度凹陷形态不同；轨道运动引发倾斜图像两侧的不对称性。

Conclusion: 非交换几何显著改变黑洞强场光学特征，尤其影响高阶引力透镜结构与辐射分布不对称性，为未来EHT等观测提供可检验的理论预言。

Abstract: We investigate the optical appearance of a non--commutative Schwarzschild black hole surrounded by a transparent, geometrically thin emitting disk. A Lorentzian matter distribution supplies the effective exterior metric. We compute photon trajectories, disk transfer functions, and the observed bolometric intensity for static and circular emitters, taking into account gravitational redshift, orbital time dilation, and Doppler modulation. At fixed asymptotic mass, increasing $θ=Θ/M^2$ from zero to $0.03$ reduces the critical impact parameter by approximately $16.1\%$ and the innermost stable circular orbit radius by $23.7\%$. The narrow higher-order features follow this contraction, while the broad direct peak shifts by less than $1\%$ for a fixed emission profile. With the same source normalization, the peak direct intensity and the signal integrated over the screen aperture increase by approximately $6.5\%$ and $4.3\%$, respectively. Moreover, different radial emission profiles produce distinct central brightness depressions at the same critical curve, and orbital motion generates an asymmetry between the approaching and receding sides of inclined images.

</details>


### [117] [Spin-Torsion Coupling: Vector and Scalar Torsional Potentials of Chiral Spinor Currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28182)
*Elisa Varani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在弱场、Ricci流启发的几何框架下，研究由手性旋量流激发的挠率场的积分表示，构建了屏蔽格林核解，分析了其与反对称四维标架扰动及挠率张量的关系；重点考察了静止环形构型下的挠率场特性，发现其磁偶极矩为零但存在非零环形矩和有效环流，并探讨了Yukawa屏蔽效应及其类Aharonov-Bohm几何结构含义；进一步引入混合手性源以描述相反手性区域间的有效耦合，计算相应翻转扇区积分；最后研究轴向背景中大质量马约拉纳中微子的动力学，区分手性混合与螺旋度振荡，并指出横向挠率分量的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索挠率引力中手性物质源（如中微子）与几何挠率场之间的深层联系，建立有限程挠率势、环形几何与中微子自旋动力学的统一数学框架，以期揭示可观测的挠率效应与新型拓扑几何结构。

Method: 基于旋量流与轴向挠率的耦合，推导并求解屏蔽格林核形式的挠率场积分表示；采用反对称四维标架扰动与contorsion张量描述挠率几何；聚焦静止环形对称构型进行解析分析；引入混合手性源模型并计算对应翻转扇区积分；结合轴向挠率背景，建立大质量Majorana中微子的运动方程并分析其手性/螺旋度演化。

Result: 获得手性旋量源激发的 screened 挠率场积分解；揭示环形构型下挠率场具有非零环形矩与有效环流但无磁偶极矩；明确Yukawa屏蔽长度对环形矩和几何相位的影响；提出混合手性源模型及对应翻转扇区积分表达式；阐明横向挠率分量在Majorana中微子手性混合中的关键作用，并区分其与螺旋度振荡的物理起源。

Conclusion: 本文构建了一个连接手性旋量源、有限程挠率势、环形空间几何与中微子自旋动力学的自洽理论框架，不仅为挠率引力中的物质-几何耦合提供了新视角，也为未来探索挠率输运、类Aharonov-Bohm效应及中微子相关可观测现象奠定了数学基础。

Abstract: We investigate integral representations of torsional fields sourced by chiral spinor currents within a weak-field, Ricci-flow-inspired geometric framework. Starting from the coupling between spinorial currents and axial torsion, we construct screened Green-kernel solutions and examine their relation to antisymmetric tetradic perturbations and contorsion.
  Particular attention is devoted to stationary toroidal configurations, for which the magnetic-type dipole moment vanishes while a nonzero toroidal moment and effective circulation remain. The dependence of these quantities on Yukawa screening is analyzed, together with their implications for an Aharonov-Bohm-like geometric structure.
  We further introduce a mixed-chirality source describing an effective connection between oppositely polarized domains and investigate the corresponding flip-sector integral. The dynamics of massive Majorana neutrinos in an axial background is then examined, distinguishing chiral mixing from helicity oscillations and identifying the role of transverse torsional components.
  The resulting framework connects spinorial sources, finite-range torsional potentials, toroidal geometry, and neutrino spin dynamics, while providing a mathematical basis for further investigations of torsional transport and observable effects.

</details>


### [118] [Kinetic moment estimates and a one-sided Lyapunov barrier for the relativistic fermionic Michie-King equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28278)
*Dorota Bors,Robert Stańczy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有固定Tolman-Klein不变量的平衡族中的截断Ruffini-Argüelles-Rueda（RAR）方程，利用Cauchy-Schwarz不等式导出压强与密度比及其导数之间的全局关系，并构造Lyapunov型壁垒，得到关于质量-半径比的显式Lambert上界（0.6879及更优的0.6734），且无需额外有限半径存在性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为严格约束相对论费米子星体模型中质量-半径比（2Gm/(rc²)）的上限，避免依赖传统存在性定理，需建立基于动力系统和不等式分析的直接、显式、可验证的界限。

Method: 采用截断RAR（即相对论费米子Michie-King）状态方程；利用三个动能矩导出压强-密度比及其导数的精确表达式；应用Cauchy-Schwarz不等式建立二者全局关系；构造改进的一侧填充式Bors-Stańczy Lyapunov壁垒并检验所有边界；结合Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程进行相平面分析；修正先前线性比较公式中的对数系数。

Result: 获得正则TOV构型下2Gm(r)/(rc²)的显式Lambert上界：通用情形为0.6879212521，沿其极值点（对角线）进一步优化至0.6734385929；该界限不依赖独立的有限半径存在性证明；同时修正了既有线性比较公式的对数系数错误。

Conclusion: 通过严格的不等式分析与Lyapunov壁垒构造，本文为相对论费米子星体提供了首个无需存在性前提的、显式且可计算的质量-半径比紧致上界，显著提升了理论约束的可靠性与实用性。

Abstract: We study the cutoff Ruffini--Argüelles--Rueda (RAR), or relativistic fermionic Michie--King, equation of state along an equilibrium family with fixed Tolman--Klein invariants. Three kinetic moments give exact formulas for both the pressure-to-density ratio and the derivative of pressure. Cauchy--Schwarz yields a global relation between these two quantities. It implies vertical vector-field domination of the associated Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff system by the linear equation of state $P=ρc^2/\sqrt3$ in the physical wedge $0<y\leq3x$. We construct a filled, one-sided version of the Bors--Stańczy Lyapunov barrier and check every boundary. This controls all outward excursions, including those after a lower exit from a Lyapunov sublevel. For regular configurations the resulting explicit Lambert bound is $2Gm(r)/(rc^2)<0.6879212521$. Using the diagonal where this ratio has a stationary value sharpens the bound to $0.6734385929$. The argument is conditional on a regular TOV configuration and does not require a separate finite-radius existence theorem. We also identify and correct a logarithmic coefficient in the previously used explicit linear-comparator formula.

</details>


### [119] [Radial stability of nonlinearly scalarized black holes in Einstein-phantom scalar-Gauss-Bonnet theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28523)
*De-Cheng Zou,Yun Soo Myung,Meng-Yun Lai,Hyat Huang,Wen-Cong Gan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-幻影标量-Gauss-Bonnet理论中非线性标量化黑洞的径向稳定性，发现两类解（低阶与主支），并利用S-形变法分析其稳定性，同时探讨了稳定性与热力学性质的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非线性标量化黑洞在修正引力理论中的稳定性，特别是其径向模式行为及与热力学性质的联系。

Method: 将标量-度规扰动约化为薛定谔型方程，结合S-形变法分析零能解，并对比多项式与指数耦合下的稳定性；同时关联径向稳定性与热力学分析。

Result: 低阶支存在一个增长模，不稳定；主支在指数耦合下可找到稳定的解；多项式耦合下的规范标量化黑洞也呈现类似分支稳定性特征。

Conclusion: 主支标量化黑洞在特定耦合（如指数型）下可稳定，而低阶支普遍不稳定；径向稳定性与热力学行为存在内在联系。

Abstract: We study the radial stability of nonlinearly scalarized black holes found in Einstein--phantom scalar--Gauss--Bonnet theory with polynomial and exponential couplings. Two branches of lower and primary are found for phantom scalarized black holes. The coupled scalar--metric perturbations reduce to a Schrödinger-type equation, after eliminating redundant metric perturbations. The lower branch including the pure quartic solutions has one growing mode, while positive zero-energy solutions for the primary branch could establish stability through the $S$-deformation method. Stable primary solutions are found for the exponential coupling. Also, we explore the connection between radial stability and thermodynamic analysis for two branches. Furthermore, we show that canonical scalarized black holes obtained from Einstein--scalar--Gauss--Bonnet theory with polynomial coupling reveals unstable lower and stable primary branches.

</details>


### [120] [The Science Chapter of the Einstein Telescope Geometry Comparison Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30570)
*Alessandra Buonanno,Federico De Lillo,Arnab Dhani,Tim Dietrich,Ulyana Dupletsa,Archisman Ghosh,Ish Gupta,Jan Harms,Francesco Iacovelli,Vuk Mandic,Harsh Narola,B. S. Sathyaprakash,Chris Van den Broeck,Haowen Zhong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文比较了爱因斯坦望远镜（ET）三种几何构型（单站三角形Δ、双站近对齐L形2L、双站错位L形2L）在多项科学目标上的性能表现，发现各构型各有优势：2L构型总体灵敏度更高，错位2L更利于源定位与多信使天文学，近对齐2L更优于随机背景探测，而Δ构型在连续/爆发源及千赫兹频段表现良好；FIM预测经贝叶斯验证基本可靠，加入Cosmic Explorer后整体性能提升但构型相对排序不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为优化爱因斯坦望远镜（ET）的几何构型设计，满足其多元科学目标（如致密天体、宇宙学、随机背景、多信使天文等），需系统评估不同布局的科学附加值。

Method: 采用代表ET科学目标的多种指标，对比三种构型（单站10-km三角形Δ、双站15-km L形近对齐与错位2L）；使用Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）进行参数估计预测，并以约550个双黑洞和中子星-黑洞信号的贝叶斯推断结果进行验证；进一步分析加入Cosmic Explorer后网络性能变化。

Result: 所有构型均能实现ET核心科学目标，但优势领域不同：2L构型在多数灵敏度驱动任务中优于Δ；错位2L最利于源定位、多信使天文与亮标准烛光宇宙学；近对齐2L最适于随机背景探测；Δ在连续/爆发源及千赫兹频段与2L相当，且源定位优于近对齐2L；30°取向2L是随机背景与定位能力的良好折中；FIM在内在参数上无系统偏差，但在距离与天空定位上因无法处理多模态与长程关联而存在偏差，但不影响构型排序；加入Cosmic Explorer提升所有构型性能，尤其高红移源表征与体积定位，但构型相对排名基本不变。

Conclusion: ET三种候选几何构型各具科学优势，应依据优先科学目标选择；错位2L为综合性能最优推荐，30°取向2L为实用折中方案；FIM可用于快速构型筛选，但关键定位任务需辅以贝叶斯验证；未来网络中ET与Cosmic Explorer协同可进一步拓展科学能力。

Abstract: This is the Scientific Added Value Chapter of the Einstein Telescope (ET) Geometry Comparison Report, commissioned from the ET Organization by the ET Board of Governmental Representatives. We compare three configurations: a single-site 10-km triangle ($Δ$) and two 15-km L-shaped detectors (2L) at widely separated sites, either nearly aligned or misaligned. We use metrics representative of the ET science case: compact-binary populations, dense matter, cosmography, stochastic backgrounds, multimessenger astronomy, fundamental physics, and continuous and burst sources. All three configurations deliver the ET science case, but they excel in different areas. For most sensitivity-driven science, the 2L configurations outperform the $Δ$. The misaligned 2L is best for localizing and characterizing individual sources, multimessenger astronomy, and bright-siren cosmology. The nearly aligned 2L is best for stochastic backgrounds. The $Δ$ localizes sources better than the aligned 2L and performs comparably to both 2L configurations for continuous and burst sources and at kilohertz frequencies. A 2L orientation of $30^\circ$ is a good compromise between stochastic-background sensitivity and localization. We validate Fisher information matrix (FIM) forecasts against Bayesian inference for $\sim550$ binary black hole and neutron star-black hole signals. Despite substantial differences, the FIM shows no systematic preference for either configuration for intrinsic parameters. For distance and sky localization, the FIM cannot capture multimodality and long-range correlations, leading to larger discrepancies, which however do not affect the ranking. Adding Cosmic Explorer to the network improves the reach of all three ET configurations, especially in volume localization and characterization of high-redshift sources. Differences between geometries shrink, but the ranking is largely unchanged.

</details>


### [121] [Quantum coherence and entanglement of mixed states in relativistic settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29322)
*Xing-Le Zhao,Zhen Yang,Shu-Min Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Unruh-DeWitt探测器模型中，初始为X型两量子比特混合态的纠缠与相干性随匀加速变化的行为；发现纠缠会发生突变死亡，而相干性则更持久，直至无限加速度极限才消失，揭示了混合初态在相对论量子信息中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室制备的量子态通常是混合态，而纯态仅为理想极限；本文由此出发，探究真实混合初态下相对论效应（如Unruh效应）对量子资源（纠缠与相干性）的影响。

Method: 采用微扰理论计算一对Unruh-DeWitt探测器（一个惯性、一个匀加速）与闵可夫斯基真空标量场相互作用后的约化态，并据此计算其纠缠度（concurrence）、l1范数相干性与相对熵相干性。

Result: 对于所考虑的初始X态族，纠缠在有限加速度下发生突然死亡，且初始纠缠越大，死亡阈值越高；两种相干性测度在纠缠死亡后仍非零，并在无穷加速度极限下趋于零；结果还揭示了这些量子资源对探测器能隙和相互作用时长的依赖性。

Conclusion: 相干性比纠缠更具鲁棒性，在加速环境下持续更久；初始态的混合性是影响相对论量子信息处理中资源演化的重要因素。

Abstract: States prepared in laboratory experiments are generally mixed because of imperfect preparation and residual coupling to the environment, whereas pure initial states represent an idealized limit. Motivated by this distinction, we investigate quantum entanglement and coherence in a pair of Unruh-DeWitt detectors initially prepared in a general two-qubit X state. One detector remains inertial and uncoupled, while the other undergoes uniform acceleration and interacts locally with a massless scalar field in the Minkowski vacuum. We obtain the reduced detector state within a perturbative treatment and evaluate its concurrence, $l_1$ norm of coherence, and relative entropy of coherence. For the initial-state families considered, the resulting expressions exhibit entanglement sudden death at finite acceleration, with larger initial entanglement associated with a higher threshold. Both coherence measures remain nonzero beyond this threshold and approach zero in the formal infinite-acceleration limit of these expressions. We also examine the dependence of these resources on the detector energy gap and interaction duration. The results distinguish the persistence of coherence from that of entanglement in the mixed-state detector model and identify initial mixedness as a relevant ingredient in relativistic quantum information.

</details>


### [122] [Regge calculus for $f(R)$ gravity with torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29447)
*Xiao-Kan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Regge微积分框架下，不依赖人为定义的几何挠率，构建了含时空挠率的f(R)引力理论；发现挠率的轴向部分由无质量Kalb-Ramond二形式携带，在四维中对偶于顶点上的标量场（即轴子），从而将离散理论简化为纯Regge引力耦合标量场（标量子与轴子）；推导了格点作用量与完整运动方程，并验证其连续极限，且在S³正则三角剖分下重现了闭宇宙的弗里德曼-雷乔杜里系统的一阶演化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免在Regge离散框架中人为引入几何挠率定义，寻求一种自然、不依赖额外离散微分结构的含挠率f(R)引力构造方式。

Method: 基于D'Adda的带坐标单纯形引力表述，在Regge微积分中引入Kalb-Ramond二形式来承载挠率的轴向部分，并利用四维Hodge对偶将其映射为对偶顶点上的标量场（轴子），结合标量子，形成两个标量场耦合的纯Regge引力模型；推导格点作用量、离散运动方程，并分析齐次各向同性格点下的Friedmann–Raychaudhuri系统。

Result: 得到完整的离散作用量与运动方程；验证其连续极限正确；在S³正则三角剖分下精确计算出动能与曲率系数；证明离散方程在一阶时间步长下复现连续闭宇宙解。

Conclusion: 挠率效应在此特定模型中可完全由两个标量场（标量子和轴子）刻画，无需引入完整的离散微分几何或显式定义格点上的挠率张量，为离散引力中处理挠率提供了一种简洁而自洽的新路径。

Abstract: We formulate $f(R)$ gravity with spacetime torsion in Regge calculus, but without an \emph{ad hoc} geometric definition of torsion. Working in D'Adda's formulation of simplicial gravity with coordinates, we show that the axial sector of the torsion is carried by a massless Kalb--Ramond two-form, which in four dimensions is dual to a single scalar on the dual vertices. The discretized theory is therefore pure Regge gravity coupled to two scalar fields, the scalaron and the axion, without a geometric definition of torsion on the Regge lattice. We derive the lattice action and the complete set of discrete equations of motion, and we verify the continuum limit. For a homogeneous and isotropic lattice we obtain the discrete Friedmann--Raychaudhuri system, compute the exact kinetic and curvature coefficients for a regular triangulation of $\mathbb S^3$, and show that the discrete equations reproduce the continuum closed solution at first order in the evolution time step. The results therefore present a very special case in which the torsional effects are completely described by scalar fields on the Regge lattice and there is no need of a full discrete differential calculus.

</details>


### [123] [Birefringent Imaging and Interferometry with Periodic Orbits and Accretion in Einstein-nonlinear-electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29472)
*Shi-Hai Dong,Farokhnaz Hosseinifar,Orlando Luongo,Hassan Hassanabadi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-非线性电动力学框架下磁荷黑洞周围的双折射成像、干涉可见度、中性周期测地线及薄盘辐射，发现两种有效光子度规导致不同的光学结构和辐射特征，磁荷主导参数变化，非线性效应较弱但可分辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非线性电动力学对黑洞周边光学与辐射现象的影响，特别是磁荷黑洞区别于常规黑洞的独特可观测效应。

Method: 采用de Paula等人提出的Euler-Heisenberg型非线性模型，将非线性耦合视为自由参数；分析两类有效光子度规下的光子环、临界曲线、傅里叶可见度、中性束缚轨道（利用有理轨道频率分类）及薄盘辐射（含偏振分辨热谱）。

Result: 发现两个独立的光子度规产生分离的临界曲线振荡与窄环调制尺度；识别出无需双势垒间光子运动即可成立的双模对应关系；获得EMRI相关保守引力波快照；计算了薄盘通量、温度、辐射效率及偏振热谱；磁荷主导所有参数依赖性，非线性修正较小但可通过与Maxwell情形对比分辨。

Conclusion: 磁荷在非线性电动力学黑洞中起主导作用，其光学与辐射特征显著区别于标准黑洞；双光子度规结构为观测区分此类新物理提供了理论基础，且偏振观测可辅助判别发射源构型（静态薄发射体 vs. 运动厚热盘）。

Abstract: We investigate birefringent imaging, interferometric visibility, neutral periodic geodesics, and thin-disk radiation around a magnetically charged black hole in Einstein-nonlinearelectrodynamics, using the Euler-Heisenberg-type model of de Paula et al. [14]. The nonlinear coupling is treated as a free model parameter. Two effective photon metrics give rise to distinct unstable light rings, capture thresholds, and images. Transforming their image-order intensities into signed Fourier visibilities reveals separate critical-curve oscillations and a narrow-ring modulation scale in the unresolved sum. This two-mode correspondence does not require photon motion between two potential barriers. Rational orbital frequencies are then used to classify neutral bound trajectories and provide conservative gravitationalwave snapshots relevant to extreme-mass-ratio inspirals. A stationary thin-disk calculation supplies flux, temperature, emitted efficiency, and polarization-resolved thermal spectra. Across the sampled parameters, magnetic charge dominates the changes, while matched Maxwell comparisons resolve smaller nonlinear corrections. The static, optically thin emitters and the orbiting, optically thick thermal disk are distinct source configurations. Polarization comparisons use the same material source within each configuration; their brightness and spectral results are conditional on that configuration.

</details>


### [124] [Viscous damping of axisymmetric oscillation modes of uniformly rotating relativistic stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29617)
*Óscar H. Petit,José A. Font*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了体粘度和剪切粘度对均匀旋转相对论星体轴对称振荡阻尼的影响，采用BDNK一阶粘性流体力学框架，在Cowling近似下进行二维数值模拟；结果表明，所有模式的振荡均在几毫秒内快速衰减，其中F模主要由体粘度主导，l=2、3模主要由剪切粘度主导，l=1模则对粘度不敏感；旋转使F模也对剪切粘度敏感；结果揭示了阻尼机制随自转速率和致密程度变化的稳健趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解粘性效应对旋转中子星振荡模式的阻尼作用，特别是区分体粘度与剪切粘度在不同模式中的主导贡献，并探究其随星体旋转速率和致密程度的变化规律。

Method: 采用BDNK一阶粘性相对论流体力学理论，在Cowling近似下构建二维数值代码，对两类多极子星模型序列（覆盖从非旋转到质量剥离极限的自转速率及不同致密程度）进行轴对称小扰动计算，分析l=0（F模）及l=1,2,3基本模的阻尼行为。

Result: 所有粘性情形下振荡均在几毫秒内快速衰减；F模阻尼主要由体粘度主导，l=2和l=3模主要由剪切粘度主导，l=1模对粘度不敏感且衰减由数值耗散主导；旋转使F模亦对剪切粘度敏感；阻尼率斜率不受数值耗散影响，因而粘性敏感性分析能可靠识别各模式主导阻尼机制。

Conclusion: BDNK框架下，不同振荡模式对体粘度和剪切粘度具有显著差异的敏感性，该敏感性随自转速率和星体致密程度呈现稳健变化趋势，为利用引力波信号反演中子星物态方程和输运系数提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study the effects of bulk and shear viscosity on the damping of axisymmetric oscillations of uniformly rotating relativistic stars, using the first-order viscous hydrodynamics formulation of Bemfica, Disconzi, Noronha and Kovtun (BDNK). The equations of motion are implemented in a two-dimensional numerical code under the Cowling approximation, assuming the fluid evolves on the fixed spacetime of a rotating star. We build two sequences of polytropic stellar models and investigate how damping depends on rotation rate (from the non-rotating case to the mass-shedding limit) and compactness. Our study focuses on the quasi-radial $l=0$ $F$ mode and the $l=1,2,3$ fundamental modes, covering a dataset of 288 perturbed stellar models. For all modes and chosen viscosities, the BDNK runs show that oscillations decay within a few milliseconds, much faster than in the perfect fluid case. The $F$ mode damping is dominated by bulk viscosity, consistent with its global compression of the star, while the $l=2$ and $l=3$ modes are mainly damped by shear viscosity. The $l=1$ mode is largely insensitive to both viscosity coefficients, and its decay rate is dominated by numerical dissipation. Rotation renders the $F$ mode sensitive to shear viscosity. Since the inherent numerical damping does not affect the slopes of the decay rates, our viscous sensitivity estimates faithfully identify the dominant damping mechanism for each mode, shear or bulk viscosity, and reveal robust trends with rotation rate and compactness.

</details>


### [125] [Gravitational waves in hierarchical triples with lensing and relativistic orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29638)
*Brian C. Seymour,Fabiano Feleppa,Luka Vujeva,Johan Samsing*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的建模方法，用于精确计算银河核附近三级系统（含双黑洞及第三天体）产生的引力波信号，通过光线追踪和偏振平行传输，在几何光学极限下实现无弱场展开的精确计算，并利用椭圆积分高效处理环境调制效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双黑洞系统可能存在于星系核附近，其引力波形受时间延迟、引力透镜效应和角动量进动等调制，可借此探测第三天体；但现有后牛顿近似方法精度不足。

Method: 在几何光学极限下，通过追踪类光测地线并平行传输其偏振态来精确计算引力波；将内双星演化按后牛顿信号处理，环境调制则用椭圆积分高效计算；引入新的相对论性透镜、像差与偏振修正项。

Result: 该方法显著提升了三级系统引力波波形的精度，且计算高效，为未来LISA数据中参数估计与可探测性分析提供了新波形代码基础。

Conclusion: 所提方法突破了传统后牛顿近似的局限，实现了高精度、高效率的三级系统引力波建模，有望推动LISA对致密天体多体系统的观测与研究。

Abstract: Binary black holes (BBH) may be formed near galactic nuclei. If such BBH systems exist, modulations to the gravitational waveform through time delays, lensing, and angular momentum precession could allow the presence of the third body to be measured in this system. In this letter, we advance the modeling for hierarchical triples by carefully including new relativistic lensing, aberration, and polarization terms. We show that the gravitational waves from this system can be computed exactly in the geometric optics limit by ray tracing the null geodesics and parallel transporting their polarization without a weak-field expansion. Our construction evolves the inner binary as if it were a post-Newtonian signal, and computes the environmental modulations efficiently with elliptic integrals. We find that this method improves the accuracy of such a triple waveform significantly compared to previous post-Newtonian approximations, and allows numerically efficient computation of the modulations. This lays the groundwork for future hierarchical triple waveform codebases, which can be used in LISA parameter estimation and detectability.

</details>


### [126] [Ionization of gravitational atoms by a companion-sourced Yukawa potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29939)
*Yi Wang,Jiayi Yao*

Main category: gr-qc

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超辐射云在双星环境中的演化及其对引力波信号的影响，特别是引入新物理（如Yukawa型第五力）后对云耗散机制的改变。

Method: 理论分析结合微扰计算，分别处理弱/强Yukawa耦合情形，比较连续谱电离与束缚态混合两种耗散通道，并计算其对引力波相位和啁啾率的修正。

Result: Yukawa相互作用在弱耦合下通过干涉增强或削弱电离功率，在强耦合下可主导云耗散；连续谱电离比束缚态混合更早终止超辐射增长；引力波啁啾率在各轨道谐波开启电离通道处呈现特征性变化。

Conclusion: Yukawa型新相互作用显著影响超辐射云动力学，连续谱电离是关键耗散机制，其效应可在引力波观测中留下可观测印记，为探测超越标准模型的新物理提供新探针。

Abstract: Superradiant instabilities around rotating black holes can produce clouds of ultralight bosons bound by the black hole's gravitational field. A binary companion can ionize these clouds by driving transitions into continuum states in the black hole's 1/r gravitational potential, extracting energy and angular momentum from the orbit. We investigate how a companion-sourced Yukawa interaction modifies this process. At weak coupling, the interference term between the gravitational and Yukawa transition amplitudes provides the leading correction to the ionization power. For the orbital frequencies here, an attractive interaction enhances continuum ionization and accelerates the inspiral, whereas a repulsive interaction has the opposite effect. At larger coupling, the pure Yukawa contribution to the ionization power becomes increasingly important and can compete with the gravitational contribution, particularly for lighter mediators. Comparing continuum ionization with off-resonant bound-state mixing, we find that continuum ionization can halt superradiant growth at a substantially smaller Yukawa coupling. At their respective critical couplings, the Yukawa interaction provides the dominant contribution to cloud depletion in both channels. Finally, we calculate the phase correction to gravitational waves due to continuum ionization and characterize how the chirp-rate response varies with frequency near the thresholds at which successive orbital harmonics open continuum-ionization channels.

</details>


### [127] [Void inside the horizon: connecting the third-quantized vacuum to a classical spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29963)
*Dong-han Yeom*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在第三量子化框架下，如何从量子引力真空态中涌现出经典时空，特别聚焦于黑洞内部的Kantowski-Sachs时空。通过波包构造与超空间的欧几里得解析延拓，连接第三量子化真空与经典几何，并指出需引入非线性自相互作用和婴儿宇宙虫洞耦合以驱动该相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免经典奇点问题，解释黑洞内部如何从量子真空演化出经典时空结构。

Method: 基于Kantowski-Sachs时空的正则量子化构建波包；引入超空间而非时空本身的欧几里得解析延拓；采用第三量子化框架描述真空到经典几何的跃迁；纳入非线性自相互作用与婴儿宇宙虫洞耦合项。

Result: 提出超空间欧氏解析延拓可定义连接第三量子化真空与经典几何的壳内轨迹，并指出完整动力学需包含非线性及虫洞耦合项。

Conclusion: 第三量子化为理解黑洞内部经典时空的量子起源提供了可行框架，其动力学必须超越自由场近似，包含特定的非线性与拓扑效应。

Abstract: We investigate the emergence of a classical spacetime from a quantum-gravitational vacuum within the framework of third quantization applied to the black hole interior. Following the canonical quantization of the Kantowski--Sachs spacetime, one can construct a wave packet that explains the classical interior of a Schwarzschild black hole. This wave packet should be extended to the classical singularity. To avoid this, one can introduce an analytic continuation and the Euclidean wave function. This is not an analytic continuation of the spacetime, but of the superspace. To give this a suitable meaning, it is reasonable to introduce the concept of the third quantization. In this aspect, the Euclidean analytic continuation of the superspace explains the on-shell trajectory connecting the third-quantized vacuum state to a coherent classical geometry. Finally, we discuss how nonlinear self-interactions and baby-universe wormhole couplings must be incorporated into the third-quantized action to dynamically drive this transition.

</details>


### [128] [The type-II double copy for relativistic Rindler fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30242)
*Yu-Cheng Wang,Jing-Rui Zhang,Yun-Long Zhang*
