<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Weyl双拷贝在更一般时空几何（如Petrov II型Rindler流体）中的适用性，拓展双拷贝对非平凡、非真空、动力学背景的描述能力。

Method: 基于固定平坦Rindler背景，采用一致旋量约定和实场强表示，将Weyl旋量代数分解为两个对称二阶Maxwell旋量；在相对论梯度展开至一阶精度下检验Maxwell方程与零拷贝标量方程；分离退化参数与梯度参数，分析向Petrov D/N型的连续极限；并与非相对论情形对比。

Result: 成功实现Petrov II型Rindler流体的混合单拷贝构造，Maxwell与标量方程在一阶梯度展开下成立；证实当复剪切与涡度趋于零时，可连续退化至Petrov D型和N型，且各路径下方程精度得以保持；相对论构造比非相对论情形更具普适性与一致性。

Conclusion: Weyl双拷贝框架可有效推广至含流体动力学的一阶相对论性弯曲背景，支持Petrov II型几何的物理单拷贝解释，并揭示了不同Petrov类型的内在统一性与连续关联。

Abstract: Within the Weyl double copy framework, we study a mixed single-copy construction for the Petrov type-II geometry of first-order relativistic Rindler fluids. Working on a common fixed flat Rindler background with consistent spinor conventions and a real field strength representation, we algebraically factorize the Weyl spinor into two symmetric rank-two Maxwell spinors and test the Maxwell equations and the zeroth copy scalar equation up to the first order in the relativistic gradient expansion. We separate the degeneration parameter from the gradient expansion parameter and analyze the continuous degeneration to the Petrov type-D and type-N branches as the complex shear and vorticity tend to zero, together with the preservation of the accuracy of the Maxwell and scalar field equations along each path. We also compare the relativistic construction with the nonrelativistic case.

</details>


### [129] [Segre Classification as a Framework for Generating Exact Solutions in Static Cylindrical Spacetimes with Levi-Civita Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30258)
*Wardah Aroosh Afzal,Tooba Feroze*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于Segre分类分析了柱对称静态时空，确定了七种可能的迹自由里奇张量Segre类型，并为其中几种类型给出了满足物理与稳定性条件的精确解，包括各向同性、各向异性及带电情形，并讨论了其能量条件、C-能量与曲率不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解柱对称静态时空中物质与能量分布的几何与物理特性，特别是通过Segre分类系统刻画里奇张量结构并构建符合物理约束的精确解。

Method: 采用Newman-Penrose形式下的Ricci旋量Segre分类方法，结合爱因斯坦场方程求解柱对称静态度规；对不同Segre类型分别构造解（如引入新度规函数、使用Krori-Barua函数、耦合非零电磁场等），并验证Zeldovich条件、因果律、能量条件和绝热指数等物理与稳定性准则。

Result: 得到了对应于Segre类型[1,(111)]、[1,1(11)]、[1,111]、[(1,1)11]、[(1,11)1]、[(1,1)(11)]的若干新精确解；识别出两类暗能量类构型（ρ = -P_r）；获得一个匹配Levi-Civita外区的带电内解，并发现径向压强在边界为零等价于Darmois匹配条件；计算并比较了各解的C-能量与曲率不变量。

Conclusion: Segre分类为系统研究柱对称引力源提供了有效框架；所得解不仅满足基本物理要求，还揭示了不同Segre类型与物态（如各向同性流体、各向异性流体、暗能量、快子、带电流体）之间的深刻对应关系。

Abstract: The main objective of this article is to analyze cylindrically symmetric static spacetime based on the Segre classification, where each Segre type corresponds to a set of canonical forms of Ricci spinors represented by Newman-Penrose scalars. The surviving Segre types of trace-free Ricci tensor in the cylindrical geometry are $[1,(111)]$, $[1,1(11)]$, $[(1,1)11]$, $[1,111]$, $[(1,11)1]$, $[(1,1)(11)]$ and $[(1,111)]$. Different combinations of eigenvalues and eigenvectors formed via Segre classification aid in understanding the distribution of matter and energy within that particular kind. Solutions are presented for the isotropic type $[1,(111)]$, for the anisotropic type $[1,1(11)]$, where a new metric function is introduced, and for type $[1,111]$, where all three pressures differ and the Krori-Barua functions are used. They satisfy the physical and stability criteria, namely the Zeldovich condition, causality condition, energy conditions and adiabatic index. The types $[(1,1)11]$ and $[(1,11)1]$ have $ρ=-P_r$ and give dark energy like configurations in which the strong energy condition is violated in the core, the latter having the tachyonic fluid form of the energy momentum tensor. For type $[(1,1)(11)]$, a charged solution in the presence of a non-null electromagnetic field is obtained and joined to an electrovac exterior. Matching with the Levi-Civita exterior shows that the vanishing of the radial pressure at the boundary is equivalent to the Darmois conditions. The C-energy and the curvature invariants are evaluated for each solution, and the results are compared with earlier works.

</details>


### [130] [Imprints of quantum space in the ringdown phase of Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30391)
*Nikola Herceg,Tajron Jurić,A. Naveena Kumara,Andjelo Samsarov,Ivica Smolić,Kumar S. Gupta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在非对易几何框架下，利用伪半Killing Drinfeld扭变研究了Kerr黑洞的引力准正规模（QNMs）的量子-空间修正，首次系统分析了非对易Kerr时空中的引力度规扰动，并估计了LIGO观测中可能的量子修正信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子引力效应在黑洞引力波环down阶段的可观测表现，特别是非对易几何对Kerr黑洞准正规模的影响，弥补此前仅限于Schwarzschild情形的研究空白。

Method: 采用保持经典Kerr解为精确背景的伪半Killing Drinfeld扭变，在自旋与量子变形参数一阶近似下推导轴向引力扰动的Schrödinger型主方程；结合WKB近似与Leaver连分式法计算基本QNM频率。

Result: 发现自旋、纯量子效应及其耦合均对QNM振荡频率和衰减率产生可观测修正；估算GWTC-3与GWTC-5.0中部分并合残余黑洞的频移与阻尼时间变化量。

Conclusion: 非对易几何引起的量子形变显著改变Kerr黑洞QNM谱，自旋–量子耦合引入新的自旋依赖修正，为未来高精度引力波观测提供潜在的新物理探针。

Abstract: We investigate quantum-space corrections to the gravitational quasinormal modes (QNMs) of Kerr black holes within the framework of noncommutative geometry. The analysis employs a pseudo-semi-Killing Drinfeld twist, which preserves the classical Kerr geometry as an exact background solution while introducing nontrivial quantum corrections to linearized gravitational perturbations. Working to first order in the black hole spin and the quantum deformation parameter, including their mixed contribution, we derive a Schrödinger-like master equation for axial gravitational perturbations with a frequency- and spin-dependent effective potential. We compute the fundamental QNM frequencies using the WKB approximation and Leaver's continued-fraction method.Our results reveal corrections to the QNM oscillation frequencies and damping rates arising from rotation, pure quantum effects, and their interplay.To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic analysis of gravitational metric perturbations in a noncommutative Kerr black hole spacetime using a Drinfeld twist, extending prior work beyond the Schwarzschild case. Our findings demonstrate that quantum deformation effects alter both the oscillation frequencies and damping rates of quasinormal modes, with spin-quantum coupling introducing additional spin-dependent corrections to the Kerr QNM spectrum. Using a Kerr-anchored phenomenological prescription, we estimate the fractional frequency and damping-time shifts for selected merger remnants from the GWTC-3 and GWTC-5.0 catalogs. These estimates illustrate the potential signatures of quantum corrections in gravitational-wave ringdown.

</details>


### [131] [Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals: accessing generic orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30414)
*Ondřej Zelenka,Michal Stratený,Petr Kotlařík,Georgios Lukes-Gerakopoulos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种机器学习方法，用于预测极端质量比旋进（EMRI）中主导的径向模式数，以减少引力波建模中不必要的模式计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在EMRI波形建模中，需大量计算Teukolsky方程的频率域模式振幅，但其中许多模式贡献极小；而事先无法判断哪些模式可忽略，导致计算资源浪费。

Method: 采用机器学习模型，基于其他已知模式数来预测主导径向模式数。

Result: 该方法能有效识别并跳过对通量贡献可忽略的模式，显著降低EMRI能量和角动量通量计算的计算开销。

Conclusion: 机器学习可用于加速EMRI波形建模中的关键数值计算，提升LISA数据处理效率。

Abstract: Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals (EMRI) are a significant gravitational wave source and a target for the upcoming LISA mission. Computations of the fluxes of conserved quantities, such as energy and angular momentum, are a critical part of modeling EMRI waveforms and can be performed using a frequency domain formalism to solve the Teukolsky equation. This requires computations of large numbers of mode amplitudes, many of which are negligible. However, it is unknown ex ante which can be safely skipped. We present a machine learning approach to estimate the dominant radial mode number as a function of the remaining mode numbers and apply it to reduce the number of unnecessary computations in EMRI flux calculation.

</details>


### [132] [Post-Newtonian expansion of the energy and angular momentum flux radiated to infinity: Eccentric-equatorial orbits about a Kerr black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30437)
*Jezreel C. Castillo,Christopher Munna,Zachary Nasipak,J. David Brown,Charles R. Evans*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了非自转质点在克尔黑洞周围偏心赤道轨道上辐射到无穷远的能量和角动量通量的解析表达式，采用Teukolsky形式和Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi方法，展开至10PN阶和e^20阶，并验证了其高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构建高精度引力波模板，需精确计算极端质量比旋进（EMRI）系统中轨道衰减的辐射反作用，尤其在克尔黑洞背景下缺乏高阶解析通量结果。

Method: 基于一阶黑洞微扰理论，采用Teukolsky方程框架；利用Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi（MST）方法获得无穷级数解析解；通过Mathematica符号计算实现对1/p（p为无量纲半通径）和偏心率e的双重展开，达10PN和e^20精度；对自旋依赖项进行解析重求和。

Result: 得到了能量与角动量辐射通量的高精度双参数（1/p, e）解析表达式，首次达10PN + e^20；识别出可对e作闭式重求和的自旋相关项；在a=0.9M、p≈20–75、e=0.1–0.7范围内，解析通量与数值Teukolsky代码结果吻合达~10^{-7}。

Conclusion: 该解析通量显著提升EMRI建模效率与精度，可在大p、中等e区域替代耗时的数值Teukolsky计算，为未来空间引力波探测（如LISA）提供关键理论工具。

Abstract: We present analytical expressions for the energy and angular momentum fluxes radiated to infinity by a (nonspinning) point mass in an eccentric-equatorial orbit about a Kerr black hole. These results build upon our previous work involving point masses on eccentric orbits about a Schwarzschild black hole. We now adopt the Teukolsky formalism in first-order black hole perturbation theory to extend our methods to Kerr. We obtain the expressions using the method of Mano, Suzuki, and Takasugi, which provides infinite-series analytical solutions to the Teukolsky equation that we generate using Mathematica. The expressions presented are obtained as a double expansion in the post-Newtonian (PN) compactness parameter $1/p$, where $p$ is the dimensionless semi-latus rectum, and in the eccentricity $e$, up to 10PN and $e^{20}$, extending beyond prior 6PN and $e^{16}$ results available for generic orbits. We characterize how the PN flux coefficients depend on the black hole spin $a$ and $e$, and find a set of spin-dependent components that can be resummed as closed-form expressions in $e$. The analytical expressions are then validated with numerical comparisons against high-precision sample results from an independent Teukolsky code and with a broader set of numerically interpolated values derived from a flux data set produced with the same code. For $a=0.9M$, the numerically evaluated 10PN fluxes match current interpolated flux grids ($\sim10^{-7}$) for $p\gtrsim20$ to $75$ as $e$ ranges from $0.1$ to $0.7$, where they could replace Teukolsky-code results.

</details>


### [133] [Dissipative Tidal Love Numbers of Neutron Stars from Viscous Heating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30466)
*Nikola Savić,Filippo Vernizzi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了非旋转相对论性中子星在低频下的主导耗散潮汐响应，采用世界线有效场论方法，并通过两种互补方式确定线性耗散Love数；结果表明，在所考虑的粘滞模型下，冷中子星的剪切粘滞潮汐耗散对引力波相位的影响可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解中子星潮汐耗散对双星系统引力波信号的影响，特别是其在低频段的相位修正，以判断是否可观测。

Method: 采用世界线有效场论框架，分别通过星体内部红移粘滞熵产和外部度规虚部（频率奇函数部分）两种方式计算线性耗散Love数，并结合公开物态方程与简单热模型进行评估。

Result: 对于1.4+1.4太阳质量、红移温度10^5 K的双中子星系统，在20 Hz至1 kHz频段内，潮汐耗散引起的引力波相位偏移小于10^{-3}周期；引入热反馈后进一步降至10^{-6}周期以下。

Conclusion: 在当前剪切粘滞模型下，冷中子星的潮汐耗散对引力波相位影响极小，因此可观测的耗散潮汐信号可能源于其他物理机制。

Abstract: We compute the leading low-frequency dissipative tidal response of nonrotating relativistic neutron stars. We formulate the response in a worldline effective field theory. We determine the linear dissipative Love number in two complementary ways: from the redshifted viscous entropy production inside the star and from the imaginary, frequency-odd part of the exterior metric. The first method reduces the calculation to an integral over the static equilibrium tide; the second provides an independent check. We apply our approach to publicly available equations of state and assess the corresponding correction to the gravitational-wave phase of neutron-star binaries. For an equal-mass $1.4+1.4M_\odot$ binary with both stars at a redshifted temperature $T_\infty=10^5\,\mathrm K$ and a standard electron shear viscosity, tidal dissipation shifts the gravitational-wave phase by less than $10^{-3}$ cycles between $20\,{\rm Hz}$ and $1\,{\rm kHz}$ for the four equations of state considered. Including the resulting rise in the stars' temperature in a simple thermal model reduces the shift below $10^{-6}$ cycles. For the viscosity model considered here, shear-viscous tidal dissipation in cold neutron stars is negligible for gravitational-wave phasing. This suggests that a measurable dissipative tidal signature would have to come from a different mechanism.

</details>


### [134] [Geometric distinguishability of two-source gravitational-wave signals from the extrinsic geometry of the single-source waveform manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30560)
*Cezar Valentin Ionescu,Paul Adrian Gogîţă,Laurentiu Ioan Caramete,Ana Caramete*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于波形流形几何结构的引力波源混淆分析方法，定义了‘到流形距离’统计量，并揭示其与多源/单源似然比的关系；通过微扰分析和数值验证，表明波形流形的外在曲率是表征局部源混淆的关键坐标无关诊断量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重叠引力波信号可能导致强简并性，即多源波形可被单个有效源精确拟合，从而难以分辨真实源数，亟需一种几何上清晰、坐标无关的混淆判据。

Method: 将单源波形族视为噪声加权探测器希尔伯特空间中的嵌入流形，定义‘到流形距离’统计量（即单源模型拟合的最小残差），在无噪极限下将其平方与双源/单源似然比关联；对邻近参数双源情形进行切空间微扰展开，分析残差在法空间的主导项，由第二基本形式决定；最后用LISA型时延干涉响应下的六参数频域旋进模型进行数值验证。

Result: 理论推导表明有效单源拟合吸收所有主导切向变形，首阶不可约残差位于流形法空间并由第二基本形式控制；数值优化重现预测的四次方标度律；二维混淆区图显示可分辨边界具有强各向异性。

Conclusion: 波形流形的外在曲率是描述局部多源混淆的坐标无关核心诊断量，该几何框架为识别参数空间中最难分辨的重叠源方向提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Overlapping gravitational-wave signals can produce strong degeneracies in which a multi-source waveform is accurately represented by a single effective source. We formulate this source-confusion problem geometrically by treating the family of single-source waveforms as an embedded manifold in the noise-weighted detector Hilbert space. We introduce the distance-to-manifold statistic, defined as the minimum residual obtained by fitting a waveform with the single-source model, and show that its squared value is directly related to the maximized likelihood difference between two-source and single-source hypotheses in the noiseless limit. For two sources with nearby parameters, perturbative minimization demonstrates that the effective single-source fit absorbs all leading tangent-space deformations. The first irreducible residual lies in the normal space of the waveform manifold and is governed by its second fundamental form. We validate our approach using a six-parameter frequency-domain inspiral model with a LISA-like time-delay-interferometry response. Direct optimization reproduces the predicted quartic scaling over the numerically resolved regime, while two-dimensional confusion-zone maps reveal strongly anisotropic distinguishability boundaries. These results establish extrinsic waveform-manifold curvature as a coordinate-independent diagnostic of local multi-source confusion and provide a practical framework for identifying parameter-space directions in which overlapping gravitational-wave sources are most difficult to resolve.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建无需慢滚假设动态施加即可满足当前暴胀观测约束的完全解析k-本质模型，解决慢滚近似局限性及奇点问题。

Method: 采用动能函数K(X)=X^n/X_s^{n-1}与标量场跟踪条件φ̇²=γH^{-m}，推导哈勃参量、尺度因子与标量场关于宇宙时的解析表达式，并结合辐射与冷暗物质流进行数值分析以检验奇点行为。

Result: 获得完全解析暴胀解，声速恒定、无梯度不稳定；观测指数仅由n和m决定；暴胀后数e倍时间出现压强奇点，但引入标准物质组分后该奇点被经典规避。

Conclusion: 该k-本质模型在保持解析性与观测兼容性的同时，揭示了暴胀后奇点的经典可规避性，为暴胀后时期演化提供了新视角。

Abstract: In this work we develop a fully analytic $k$-essence inflation solution which is compatible with the observational constraints on inflation, without the need for the slow-roll assumption being imposed dynamically in the field equations. The analytic solution is obtained for a kinetic scalar sector of the form $K(X)= \frac{X^n}{X_s^{n-1}}$ and for the scalar field satisfying a tracking condition $\dotφ^2=γH^{-m}$. For this class of theories, the sound speed of the primordial curvature perturbations is constant, so no gradient instabilities occur in the theory. The Hubble rate, the scale factor and the scalar field can be analytically expressed as functions of the cosmic time, and the observational indices of inflation depend solely on the parameters $n$ and $m$. The resulting evolution is inflationary and it develops a pressure singularity a few $e$-foldings after the end of inflation. However, using qualitative and quantitative numerical arguments, we show that the pressure singularity is classically avoided when the matter fluids of radiation and cold dark matter are taken into account.

</details>


### [136] [Constraining Regular Black Hole Models with the Effective Field Theory treatment of General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30616)
*Sami Viollet*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用广义相对论作为有效场论的微扰预测来约束黑洞背景下非微扰量子引力方法的新方案，并以Hayward度规为例验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用微扰理论结果对非微扰量子黑洞模型（如正则黑洞）进行约束和检验，以提升量子引力理论的可检验性。

Method: 基于广义相对论有效场论框架下的微扰计算，推导静态粒子在黑洞附近相互作用能的一阶量子修正；将该结果与以修正Schwarzschild度规形式表示的非微扰黑洞模型进行比较，检验其一致性。

Result: Hayward度规无法复现微扰预测的量子修正；同时给出了能精确复现该结果的最一般度规形式。

Conclusion: 微扰有效场论可作为判别非微扰量子黑洞模型合理性的有力工具，为筛选可行的量子引力模型提供了新标准。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a method for using perturbative predictions from treating general relativity as an effective field theory to constrain non-perturbative approaches to quantum gravity in black hole contexts. We start by reviewing a known result from this framework, which estimates the first quantum corrections to the interaction energy of a static particle near a black hole. We then propose a way to test non-perturbative quantum black hole models - specifically those formulated as modified Schwarzschild metrics, also known as regular black holes - against this perturbative result. To illustrate, we apply our method to the Hayward metric, showing that it does not align with the perturbative prediction, and we identify the most general metric that can reproduce the expected result.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 1) 中心荷相等是Ricci-flat黑洞支持常数反常解的充要条件；2) 中心荷不等时产生普适标量毛发，其电荷由κ、χ_h和(c_W−c_E)决定；3) 电荷符号在临界Q/M处翻转，极值极限仅保留Weyl荷；4) 所有幺正物质对应的毛发在柯西视界发散；5) 降阶后理论的动力学完全由中心荷差控制，并推广了无鬼与拉普拉斯稳定性条件。

Conclusion: 引力迹反常通过中心荷差引入可观测的黑洞标量毛发，该毛发具有严格的热力学-几何-共形起源，为检验量子引力紫外完成提供了新窗口；同时揭示了反常结构对黑洞内部（柯西视界）不稳定性的重要贡献。

Abstract: We study black holes in Gabadadze's weakly coupled effective theory of the gravitational trace anomaly, keeping both the Euler and the Weyl central charges. We prove that a constant anomalyon on any Ricci-flat geometry, the Kerr family included, solves the full theory if and only if the two central charges coincide. Otherwise, the black holes continuously connected to this branch carry scalar hair whose charge is fixed by the surface gravity, the horizon topology and the difference of central charges alone: maximally supersymmetric matter leaves black holes bald, the Standard Model and photon-dominated sectors produce hair of definite sign, and conformal collider bounds confine it to a universal window. Electric charge separates the two anomaly invariants: the hair reverses sign at a critical charge-to-mass ratio set by the ratio of central charges, its extremal value retains only the Weyl charge, and the Euler charge governs the near-horizon growth of the anomalyon. For every unitary matter sector, the hair regular at the event horizon diverges at the Cauchy horizon. Finally, once the higher-derivative Weyl operator is order-reduced, the background, the anomalyon-graviton mixing and all propagation speeds depend only on the difference of central charges, which extends the no-ghost and Laplacian stability conditions to the full anomaly.

</details>


### [138] [Detecting Frame Dragging Effect with Next-Generation Spaceborne Clocks in a LEO-DRO Satellite Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30726)
*Li-Fang Li,Hanlin Song,Xing Bian,Tong Liu,Shao-Jiang Wang,Song He,Yang Li,Jiaxing Leng,Ziren Luo,Peng Xu,Rong-Gen Cai*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用低地球轨道（LEO）与远距逆行轨道（DRO）卫星星座进行高精度频率比对的方法，旨在直接探测地球自转引起的参考系拖曳（frame-dragging）效应，其信号量级约为10^{-20}；借助下一代光学钟（如锶光晶格钟）的稳定性进展，该测量在近期具备可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实验对广义相对论中地球引力磁场（即参考系拖曳）的直接测量仍受限于精度；需更高灵敏度的时空基准以验证爱因斯坦钟同步中的引力磁效应。

Method: 构建LEO-DRO双星卫星星座，建立包含后牛顿度规、地球高阶多极矩、日月潮汐势及引力磁矢势的完整相对论性频率比对模型。

Result: 理论预测帧拖曳信号达10^{-20}量级，而最新锶光晶格钟已实现2.5×10^{-19}（一日）的分数频率稳定度，逼近该探测门槛。

Conclusion: 该方案有望在短期内实现对地球参考系拖曳效应的直接空间测量，从而约束地球引力磁场，并为研究引力磁效应对相对论时钟同步的影响奠定基础。

Abstract: Our approach employs a satellite constellation comprising one satellite in low Earth orbit (LEO) and one satellite in distant retrograde orbit (DRO). This configuration is designed to enhance the sensitivity to the Earth's rotation-induced frame-dragging signal while providing a stable and gravitationally isolated frequency reference. We derive a complete relativistic formulation for the frequency comparison between LEO and DRO, incorporating the post-Newtonian metric, higher-order Earth multipoles, lunar and solar tidal potentials, and the gravitomagnetic vector potential. The frame-dragging signal is of order $10^{-20}$, a level that is becoming increasingly accessible with next-generation optical clocks. Recent advances in zero-dead-time strontium optical lattice clocks have demonstrated a fractional frequency instability of $2.9\times10^{-19}$ at an averaging time of 20,000~s, with an estimated one-day stability of $2.5\times10^{-19}$. With continued advances in clock stability, achieving fractional-frequency sensitivity at the $10^{-20}$ level appears feasible in the near term, potentially enabling the direct measurement of a frame-dragging signal of the same order. Such a measurement would constrain Earth's gravitomagnetic field, enabling future investigations of gravitomagnetic effects on Einstein clock synchronization.

</details>


### [139] [Schwarzschild-de Sitter space is not rigid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31019)
*Philippe G. LeFloch,Luc Rozoy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从紧致的亚极端Schwarzschild-de Sitter区域出发，构造了一族具有两个额外黑洞视界的静态轴对称真空解，每个视界面积可预先指定；证明了所有视界与对称轴均正则，度规与lapse函数在边界上实解析；通过多尺度拼接与一致椭圆估计等技术完成构造，并分析了lapse函数最大值集的几何性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有可控几何结构（如指定视界面积）的新静态黑洞解，以拓展广义相对论中真空时空解的存在性与多样性理论，并研究lapse函数极值集的形态及其物理含义。

Method: 采用多尺度拼接方法（gluing at separated scales），结合一致椭圆估计；引入局部张量源恢复线性化方程的可解性；利用全局Green张量匹配Schwarzschild局部修正；通过反射对称性和中心径向位置调节使源系数消失；借助球面上的热方程和径向Green函数分析lapse函数的角向变化及极大值集几何。

Result: 构造出一族满足所有正则性（视界、轴、边界解析性）和面积约束的静态轴对称真空解；当质量参数趋于零时，解光滑收敛到参考解（除球面上两个对跖点外）；在小质量情形下，lapse函数在唯一赤道圆上取最大值，其Hessian在横截平面负定，且较小特征值模渐近于与质量成正比的正常数；邻近正则水平集为环面。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅实现了对静态黑洞时空几何的精细调控，还揭示了lapse函数极值结构（如环形最大值集）与黑洞参数间的定量关系，为理解静态时空的稳定性、相变及热力学类比提供了新工具与范例。

Abstract: Starting from any compact sub-extremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter region with positive cosmological constant, we construct a family of static axisymmetric vacuum solutions with two additional black-hole horizons, each of prescribed area. All horizons and the symmetry axis are regular, and the metric and lapse are real analytic up to the boundary. As the mass parameter tends to zero, the metric and lapse converge smoothly to the reference solution away from two antipodal points on its sphere of maximal lapse. Rescaling near either added horizon yields the Schwarzschild spatial metric in the limit. For small prescribed mass, the lapse attains its maximum precisely on one equatorial circle. Its Hessian is negative definite in the transverse plane, with the smaller eigenvalue magnitude asymptotic to a positive constant times the added mass. Nearby regular level sets are tori. The proof combines gluing at separated scales with uniform elliptic estimates. An integral condition removes infinitesimal translations of each Schwarzschild model, while a localized tensor source restores solvability of the linearized equation. Matching the local Schwarzschild corrections to a global Green tensor identifies the leading source coefficients. Reflection makes these coefficients equal, and varying the common radial position of the centers makes them vanish. A radial Green function and the heat equation on the sphere determine the angular variation of the lapse and the geometry of its maximum set.

</details>


### [140] [Lyapunov Stability of a Schwarzschild Black Hole Surrounded by the Kiselev Anisotropic Fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31035)
*Saswati Roy,Paramita Debnath,Shubham Kala,Hemwati Nandan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了被Kiselev各向异性流体包围的Schwarzschild黑洞周围的测地线结构和圆轨道稳定性，分析了不同状态方程参数ω和归一化参数N对光球、ISCO和OSCO的影响，并通过坐标时间Lyapunov指数检验其稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Kiselev各向异性流体对黑洞周围时空几何及粒子轨道动力学的影响，特别是光球、稳定圆轨道边界及其稳定性判据。

Method: 利用类时与类光测地线的有效势能，确定光球、最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）和最外稳定圆轨道（OSCO），并结合坐标时间Lyapunov指数分析其稳定性；推导适用于任意ω的(λ_t^T)^2闭式表达式。

Result: 光球对所有考虑的ω和N均不稳定，其不稳定性随N变化趋势依赖于ω；对于ω=-1和-2/3，存在ISC0与OSCO共存区间，二者在临界N值处合并；对于ω≥-1/3，稳定圆轨道存在于ISCO以外所有半径；(λ_t^T)^2符号变化精确对应ISCO和OSCO位置。

Conclusion: Kiselev流体显著改变黑洞周围稳定轨道结构，Lyapunov指数可精确刻画稳定性的边界，结果涵盖Schwarzschild–de Sitter等特例，对黑洞阴影、吸积盘及准周期振荡研究具潜在意义。

Abstract: We investigate the geodesic structure and the stability of circular orbits around a Schwarzschild black hole surrounded by a Kiselev anisotropic fluid, characterised by the equation-of-state parameter $ω$ and the normalisation parameter $N$. We consider six representative values, $ω=-1,-2/3,-1/3,0,1/3$ and $1$, over the admissible range of $N$ determined by the horizon structure, including the corresponding extremal limits. Using the effective potentials for null and timelike geodesics, we determine the photon sphere, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) and the outermost stable circular orbit (OSCO), and examine their stability through coordinate-time Lyapunov exponents. The photon sphere is unstable for all values of $ω$ and $N$ considered. Its instability weakens with increasing $N$ for $ω\leq0$ and vanishes at extremality for $ω=-1$ and $-2/3$, whereas it varies non-monotonically with $N$ for $ω=1/3$ and strengthens for $ω=1$. For $ω=-1$ and $-2/3$, stable circular orbits are confined between the ISCO and the OSCO, which merge at a critical value of $N$ beyond which no stable circular orbits exist. In contrast, for $ω\geq-1/3$ stable circular orbits exist at all radii beyond the ISCO, whose location varies with $N$. We also derive a closed-form expression for the squared coordinate-time Lyapunov exponent $(λ_t^T)^2$, valid for arbitrary $ω$, whose sign changes exactly at the ISCO and OSCO, so that the Lyapunov criterion reproduces the boundaries of stable circular motion. The Schwarzschild--de Sitter, mass-shifted Schwarzschild and Reissner--Nordström geometries are recovered as special cases, and the results may have implications for black-hole shadows, thin accretion discs and quasi-periodic oscillations.

</details>


### [141] [Relativistic dynamical friction and accretion for Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31061)
*Nicholas Speeney,Soumodeep Mitra,Emanuele Berti,Andrea Maselli,Sumanta Chakraborty*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了极端质量比旋进（EMRIs）在LISA观测中受暗物质环境影响的动力学摩擦与吸积效应，特别关注克尔黑洞周围暗物质晕的相空间结构对摩擦力方向和大小的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用LISA观测EMRIs探测大质量黑洞周围的暗物质分布，需精确建模暗物质引起的相对论性动力学摩擦与吸积，而以往工作多基于施瓦西度规或局部密度近似，忽略相空间各向异性和黑洞自旋效应。

Method: 将非自转致密天体置于嵌入碰撞less暗物质分布的克尔黑洞的赤道圆轨道上，采用绝热形成的暗物质尖峰模型（源自Hernquist初态），通过积分相对论散射与捕获截面，在完整（通常各向异性）相空间中计算动量转移。

Result: 发现：1）远离黑洞时摩擦力正比于局域暗物质密度，且随黑洞自旋增大而增强；2）在高速自转黑洞（a≳0.37）内稳定圆轨道附近，参考系拖曳迫使束缚暗物质共转，导致次级天体被介质‘追上’，摩擦减弱甚至反向（尤其在暗物质密度峰值处）；3）非自转黑洞下若初始晕具强净角动量，也可模拟类似反向，但需超出宇宙学模拟的各向异性；4）常用仅依赖局域密度的Chandrasekhar型公式会高估摩擦力。

Conclusion: 建模EMRIs中暗物质效应时，必须纳入环境完整的相空间结构，不能仅依赖局域密度近似；黑洞自旋与暗物质相空间各向异性对动力学摩擦有定性影响，可能显著改变EMRI波形与参数估计。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) observed by LISA will probe the environments of massive black holes (BHs), including the dark matter (DM) overdensities expected to form around them. We compute the relativistic dynamical friction and accretion experienced by a non-spinning compact object on circular equatorial orbits around a Kerr BH embedded in a collisionless DM distribution, extending previous results in Schwarzschild. The DM is modeled as a spike grown adiabatically from an initial Hernquist profile, and the momentum transfer is obtained by integrating relativistic scattering and capture cross sections over the full, generically anisotropic phase space seen by the secondary. Far from the primary the friction tracks the local DM density, which increases with spin. Near the innermost stable circular orbit of rapidly spinning BHs $(a\gtrsim 0.37)$, however, frame dragging forces bound DM particles to co-rotate, so that the secondary is overtaken by the surrounding medium: the friction weakens and eventually reverses sign where the DM density peaks. We show that a similar reversal can be mimicked around a non-rotating BH if the initial halo carries large net angular momentum, although this requires anisotropies beyond those found in cosmological simulations. We also show that commonly used Chandrasekhar-like prescriptions, which depend only on the local density, can overestimate the friction force. Our results demonstrate that the full phase-space structure of the environment must be included when modeling DM effects on EMRIs.

</details>


### [142] [Enhancing eccentricity through tidal resonance in binary neutron star systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31172)
*Ivan Markin,Natalie Williams,Tim Dietrich*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过数值相对论模拟发现，即使初始为近圆轨道的双中子星系统，在并合晚期也可能因其中一颗中子星的f模共振而产生可观测的轨道偏心率，该效应与恒星自转相关，独立于系统形成历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道偏心率是揭示双中子星形成历史的关键参数；通常认为可观测偏心率意味着动力学形成，但本文旨在探究是否存在其他非形成历史相关的偏心率起源。

Method: 采用数值相对论模拟方法，研究两颗高速自转（一颗顺行、一颗逆行）中子星的并合过程，重点关注f模潮汐共振及其对轨道和自转演化的影响，并构建自旋依赖的共振锁定模型进行解释。

Result: 观测到逆行自转中子星发生持续f模共振，伴随其自转角动量因轨道角动量转移而逐渐变化；该自转演化可被自旋修正的共振锁定模型很好描述；共振导致轨道偏心率在晚期显著增长。

Conclusion: 双中子星系统在晚期并合阶段可通过f模共振自发产生偏心率，这一机制不依赖于其初始形成环境，对含快速自转中子星的波形建模和引力波数据分析具有重要启示。

Abstract: Orbital eccentricity of binary neutron star systems is a key parameter to reveal their formation history. Since binaries formed in isolation are expected to circularize long before merger, measurable eccentricity suggests dynamical formation in dense stellar environments. However, using numerical-relativity simulations, we show that eccentricity can be introduced in the late inspiral of an initially quasicircular binary, due to the $f$-mode resonance of one of the stars. We simulate the merger of two highly spinning neutron stars, one star spinning prograde and the other retrograde. Persistent tidal resonance with the $f$ mode of the retrograde-spinning star is observed, accompanied by a gradual change of its spin due to angular momentum transfer from the orbit. This spin change can be well explained by a simple resonance locking model that accounts for the spin-dependent shift of the $f$-mode frequency. These findings reveal an origin of eccentricity in binary neutron stars that is independent of their formation history and provide important information for modeling binary neutron star dynamics with rapidly spinning stars.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天体测量已成为探测引力波的一种可行手段，可补充脉冲星计时方法，提供重叠且更高频段的引力波覆盖。

Method: 类比脉冲星计时方法，构建恒星位置横向偏转间的自相关统计量，并进一步建立恒星偏转与脉冲星计时残差之间的互相关统计量；统一采用相同形式框架，并估算其信噪比及弱/中信号区间的标度律。

Result: 提出了适用于天体测量探测随机引力波背景的相关统计方法，推导了自相关与互相关的信噪比估计及相应标度律。

Conclusion: 天体测量与脉冲星计时在引力波探测中具有形式统一性，二者可协同提升对随机引力波背景的探测能力。

Abstract: Astrometric measurements have emerged as a viable probe of gravitational waves (GWs), complementing pulsar timing searches by providing overlapping and higher GW frequency coverage. At its core, the search for GWs using astrometry is very similar to the search with pulsar timing data: GWs induce a change in pulse arrival times along the line of sight to a pulsar and a transverse angular deflection in the apparent position of a star. In analogy to pulsar timing, we develop a correlation statistic between pairs of stellar deflections for astrometry to search for a stochastic GW background. With a common formalism for both astrometry and pulsar timing, we also develop the cross-correlation statistic between stellar deflections and pulsar timing residuals. We estimate the signal-to-noise ratios from the correlation statistics and provide scaling laws in the weak- and intermediate-signal regimes.

</details>

*Paul Ramond*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于广义相对论中延展体的多极展开框架，构建包含任意阶自旋与潮汐多极矩的协变标量势U，导出哈密顿量H；在Kerr度规下显式构造高阶运动常数，并通过解析判据结合数值验证其存在性与唯一性。

Result: 1) 任意阶多极矩延展体的保守运动由H=-½μ²+μU描述，且μ与自旋范数严格守恒；2) Kerr黑洞的多极矩是唯一能在Kerr背景中产生直至四次自旋守恒量（扩展Carter/Rüdiger常数）的配置；3) 五次自旋阶上不存在此类守恒量，揭示隐藏对称性在该阶破裂。

Conclusion: Kerr黑洞双星在保守测试体极限下展现出一种仅在有限自旋阶（≤4）成立的动力学隐藏对称性，这种对称性是其区别于其他致密天体的本质特征，但并非无限阶对称。

Abstract: We show that the conservative equations of motion of an extended test body, with force and torque sourced by spin-induced and adiabatic tidal multipoles of arbitrary order, are the flow of the Hamiltonian $H=-\tfrac12μ^2+μU$, where $μ$ is the body's kinematic mass and all multipoles enter through a covariant scalar potential $U$. For such bodies, a certain notion of mass and a spin norm are shown to be exactly conserved, at all multipolar orders and in any background. We then apply our framework to the Kerr spacetime, and construct extensions of the Carter and Rüdiger constants through hexadecapolar order (quartic in spin). They are found to exist if, and only if, the body's multipoles are those of a Kerr black hole. Together with the energy and angular momentum, they make the dynamics of a spinning test black hole in Kerr perturbatively integrable through that order. Lastly, at the next, quintic-in-spin order, an analytic criterion implemented numerically reveals that neither constant admits a continuation, for any conservative multipoles. In the conservative, test-body limit, black-hole binaries thus carry a dynamical hidden symmetry shared by no other compact objects, which persists to high multipolar order but ultimately breaks.

</details>

Abstract: We construct conserved charges of asymptotic symmetries for negative-cosmological-constant vacuum spacetimes from the Neumann data for the Hamiltonian and momentum constraints on maximal conformally-compact Cauchy hypersurfaces. This gives a general and canonical initial value treatment of charges for vacuum spacetimes with timelike conformal boundary.

</details>

*Siwei Han,Zeinab Imani,Jun Feng,Robert B. Mann*

Result: 单探测器跃迁概率与有效温度随加速度增大而增大，但对挠率-加速度比\bar{b}的依赖呈能量间隙依赖的非单调性；双探测器关联提取能力（并发度与互信息）随加速度或间距增大而被抑制，且抑制程度强烈依赖轨迹类型；四类轨迹均存在仅靠真空关联即可实现真实纠缠提取的参数区域。

Conclusion: 探测器运动轨迹的微分几何属性（尤其是挠率）显著调制其感知的量子真空效应；真空关联提取不仅取决于加速度大小，更依赖整体运动形态；该结果为基于运动操控的量子信息协议提供了新自由度。

Abstract: We study Gaussian-switched Unruh-DeWitt detectors coupled to a massless scalar field in the (2+1)-dimensional Minkowski vacuum and following four classes of uniformly accelerated trajectories: linear, catenary, cusped, and circular motion. We first determine how the single-detector transition probability and effective temperature depend on acceleration and the torsion-to-acceleration ratio $\bar{b}$, whose continuous variation connects these four trajectory classes. We find that both quantities generally increase with acceleration, while their dependence on $\bar{b}$ can become nonmonotonic depending on the energy gap. We also examine a correlation-harvesting protocol by two detectors following horizontally displaced copies of the same trajectory. Increasing acceleration or separation generally suppresses the harvested concurrence and quantum mutual information, but the extent of suppression at fixed acceleration depends strongly on the trajectory class. For each of the four trajectory classes, we identify regimes of genuine entanglement harvesting in which vacuum correlations alone suffice to entangle the detectors, while the communication contribution alone cannot overcome local noise.

### [147] [Horizon mergers and initial test-charge acceleration in charged black-hole geometries with anisotropic sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28095)
*Saeed Noori Gashti,Mohammad Ali S. Afshar,Mohammad Reza Alipour*

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有弦云、Kiselev幂律成分或对数轮廓的静态球对称黑洞几何中视界合并及带电测试粒子的初始径向加速度，推导了视界合并条件并分类Kiselev分支，分析了不同模型下测试粒子向外加速所需的电荷-质量比阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同暗能量/暗物质类环境（如弦云、Kiselev型、对数轮廓）对黑洞视界结构和带电粒子运动的影响，特别是视界合并条件与初始动力学行为。

Method: 采用统一的质量函数形式，分离麦克斯韦贡献后分析有效环境应力张量的各向异性；推导视界合并条件与电荷-质量比阈值；对不同背景轮廓（弦云、对数、Kiselev）分别求解并比较其阈值特性与稳定性。

Result: 发现内事件视界合并电荷仅给出电荷上限；三正视界共存需额外正下限；弦云模型阈值下确界为1/z（仅在无穷远趋近）；对数轮廓阈值全局最小值可达约1.251125；Kiselev中性平衡半径唯一但径向不稳定；密度匹配仅提供条件归一化，非观测拟合。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了几何视界界限与测试粒子动力学特征，但未建立双黑洞作用力定律、宇宙加速机制，亦不构成弱引力猜想的检验。

Abstract: We study horizon mergers and the initial radial acceleration of charged test particles in static, spherically symmetric black-hole geometries with a string cloud, a Kiselev power-law component, or a logarithmic profile conventionally. After separating the Maxwell contribution, the effective environmental stress tensors are anisotropic and are not identified with homogeneous quintessence or pressureless cold dark matter. Using a common mass-function formulation, we derive the horizon-merger conditions and classify the Kiselev branches. In this family, the inner-event merger charge provides only an upper bound: three distinct positive horizons can additionally require a positive lower charge bound associated with the event-cosmological merger. For a test particle released from rest relative to a static observer, we derive the local charge-to-mass threshold for outward initial acceleration. Writing $λ=q/μ$ and $z=Q/Q_{\rm ext}$, where $Q_{\rm ext}$ is the inner-event merger charge, the subextremal string-cloud threshold has infimum $1/z$, approached only at infinity. For the subextremal logarithmic profile, the threshold exceeds unity throughout the finite exterior and attains a finite global minimum. For $α/M=0.4$ and $Q/M=1.2$, this minimum is $λ_*\simeq1.251125$. In a nondegenerate Kiselev static patch, the neutral balance radius is unique and radially unstable. Density matching supplies conditional profile normalizations, not observational fits. For the logarithmic profile, the integration parameter is distinguished from the mass combination inferred from circular motion. These results characterize geometric horizon bounds and test-particle dynamics; they do not establish a two-black-hole force law or a mechanism for cosmic acceleration, nor do they constitute a test of the Weak Gravity Conjecture.

</details>


### [148] [Level repulsion and the quantum viscosity of near-extremal black branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28203)
*Panos Betzios,Olga Papadoulaki,Maria Myrto Pegioudi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了近极端黑洞膜在JT引力框架下的剪切粘度行为，发现量子涨落导致粘度发散，通过引入非微扰完成（矩阵积分）来解决该问题，并揭示了能级关联、普适性粘度及与Airy核的精确对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决近极端黑洞膜在AdS2喉区下剪切粘度在盘近似中发散的问题，探索非微扰效应如何影响输运性质和能级统计。

Method: 结合JT引力的盘近似、一环计算、非微扰矩阵模型完成、Kubo公式、双标度极限分析、Airy核精确求解，以及与弦方程定义的显式完成模型对比。

Result: 1) DC粘度因能级排斥在有限极熵S0下消失；2) 在双标度极限下获得有限普适粘度η_max ≈ 0.174 (ω/ω_sp) η_sc；3) 极低温下（CT ∼ e^{-2S0/3}）退火平均量失效，而淬火平均的η/s比值始终为正有限；4) 不同非微扰完成对体部结果一致，边缘淬火结果偏差仅百分之几，退火量则依赖完成方式达O(1)。

Conclusion: 非微扰完成对理解黑洞低能输运至关重要；粘度行为由能级统计主导，其普适性在双标度极限和Airy边缘下得以精确刻画；淬火平均更可靠地反映普适物理。

Abstract: Below the gap scale of the AdS$_2$ throat, quantum fluctuations make the shear viscosity of near-extremal black branes grow as $(CT)^{-1/2}$ in the disk approximation of JT gravity, and the one-loop $η/s$ diverges. To cure this divergence, we include the non-perturbative completion of JT gravity as a matrix integral. The Kubo formula then probes universal features of level correlations. At finite extremal entropy $S_0$, level repulsion makes the DC viscosity vanish. A finite, universal viscosity survives in a double-scaling limit in which the probe frequency $ω$ is held in a fixed ratio to the level-spacing scale $ω_{\rm sp}\equiv e^{-S_0}/C$. At fixed $ω$, with $ω_{\rm sp}\llω\ll e^{S_0/3}ω_{\rm sp}$ the Schwarzian growth then saturates at $η_{\max}\simeq0.174\,(ω/ω_{\rm sp})\,η_{sc}$, where $η_{sc}$ is the semiclassical viscosity, and the viscosity decreases at lower temperatures. At the extremely low temperature scale $CT\sim e^{-2S_0/3}$ the genus expansion and the one-loop entropy computations completely break down. There we start probing the edge of the spectrum and the problem reduces exactly to the Airy kernel. We find that the ratio of the quenched averages of $η$ and $s$ is finite and positive at every non-zero temperature. We compare our results with explicit completions defined by the string equation, in which the spectrum is bounded from below. The bulk results are the same in these completions and the quenched edge results change only by a few per cent, while the annealed quantities depend on the non-perturbative completion at order one.

</details>


### [149] [The index from a super-point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28204)
*Dhanvarsh Annamalai,Hyunook Hur,Thomas Hussel,Monica Jinwoo Kang,Craig Lawrie,Jaewon Song*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文扩展了编码4维N=2超共形场论Higgs分支和Macdonald指标的滤过分次环的代数几何构造，使其适用于Schur扇区包含费米子生成元的理论；这些环是Z2-分次交换环，其指标由广义弧空间的Hilbert级数再现；研究了自由N=2矢量多重态、N=4超杨-米尔斯（su(2)和su(3)规范代数）、秩一N=3 SCFT及E6帽(SU(2)) SCFT等具体例子；对E6帽(SU(2))理论，通过在Zhu C2-代数的一阶射流环中补充三个关系，广义弧空间成功复现其Macdonald指标至q^51阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已有的代数几何方法推广到包含费米子Schur算子的N=2 SCFT，以统一描述Higgs分支与Macdonald指标，并检验VOA的经典自由性。

Method: 构建Z2-分次交换的滤过分次环，利用广义弧空间及其Hilbert级数计算Macdonald指标；对具体理论（如N=4 SYM、N=3 SCFT、E6帽(SU(2))）显式计算对应理想（如Jacobi理想之和）及VOA结构（如AFFT A(6)代数）。

Result: 成功为含费米Schur算子的理论建立广义弧空间框架；明确给出多个理论的理想结构；发现A(6)代数非经典自由，但通过在一阶射流环添加三个关系可使广义弧空间精确复现E6帽(SU(2))的Macdonald指标至q^51阶。

Conclusion: 广义弧空间方法可有效处理含费米子生成元的N=2 SCFT；Macdonald指标的几何实现依赖于超越Zhu C2-代数的更高阶导数关系；该框架深化了VOA、几何表示论与SCFT指标间的联系。

Abstract: We extend the algebro-geometric construction of filtered graded rings that encode both the Higgs branch and the Macdonald index of 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal field theories to theories whose Schur sector contains fermionic generators. The rings are then $\mathbb{Z}_2$-graded-commutative, and the index is reproduced by the Hilbert series of a generalized arc space, whose defining ideal may contain relations among the generators and their derivatives. We study the free $\mathcal{N}=2$ vector multiplet, $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills with gauge algebras $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ and $\mathfrak{su}(3)$, the rank-one $\mathcal{N}=3$ SCFTs, and the $\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ SCFT. For the rank-one $\mathcal{N}=3$ SCFTs, the ideal is the sum of the Jacobian ideals of a single superpotential and of its two supersymmetry variations. For $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills, the ideal is similarly the sum of the Jacobian ideals of the components of a single $\mathcal{N}=4$ superfield, which is a singlet of the $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ flavor symmetry for gauge algebra $\mathfrak{su}(2)$, and a triplet for $\mathfrak{su}(3)$. The associated VOA for the $\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ theory is given by the Feigin--Feigin--Tipunin $\mathcal{A}(6)$ algebra. The arc space of Zhu's $C_2$-algebra of $\mathcal{A}(6)$ does not reproduce the vacuum character, and therefore $\mathcal{A}(6)$ is not classically free. Supplementing Zhu's $C_2$-algebra by three relations in the first jet ring, the generalized arc space reproduces the Macdonald index of $\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ up to, at least, order $q^{51}$.

</details>


### [150] [The Gradient Structure of Perturbative RG Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28205)
*Lorenzo Benfatto,William H. Pannell,Andreas Stergiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过球面上的共形Ward恒等式，证明了在任意维度d中，微扰重整化群流可以被构造为关于对称度规的势函数A的梯度流，从而解决了长期存在的反对称度规障碍问题；在偶数维（2,4,6）中，A在固定点处等于	ilde{F}，由此导出适用于全阶微扰的	ilde{F}-定理。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自Jack和Osborn三十多年前以来未能消除的、阻碍RG流成为纯梯度流的反对称度规障碍。

Method: 利用球面上的共形Ward恒等式选择特定重整化方案，消去反对称度规项；结合微扰理论、Weyl一致性条件与球面路径积分定义的	ilde{F}量。

Result: 证明在任意维度d下，beta函数可表为势函数A关于正定对称度规的梯度（全阶微扰成立）；A在固定点等于\tilde{F} = \sin(\pi d/2) \ln Z_{S^d}，从而确立	ilde{F}-定理至所有微扰阶；指出除d=6,10,...附近自由标量场外，相关算符源不影响结论。

Conclusion: Weyl一致性条件下的RG流本质上是梯度流，其势函数A具有普适物理意义（如	ilde{F}），为量子场论中的单调性定理提供了坚实微扰基础；唯一未解决情形是d≈6时φ³理论中自由标量源的处理。

Abstract: Weyl consistency conditions show that perturbative renormalisation group flows in two, four and six dimensions are almost gradient flows. The obstruction is an antisymmetric contribution to the metric on the space of couplings, which has resisted removal since the work of Jack and Osborn more than 35 years ago. We show that a conformal Ward identity on the sphere allows it to be removed by a choice of scheme. The beta function is then the gradient of a potential $A$ with respect to a symmetric metric, to all orders in perturbation theory. The Ward identity gives this result in any dimension $d$, in theories with and without a Weyl anomaly. At leading order the metric is fixed by the two-point functions of the perturbing operators, so unitarity makes it positive definite at weak coupling. At fixed points $A$ equals $\widetilde{F}=\sin\tfrac{πd}{2}\,\ln Z_{S^{d}}$, so the $\widetilde{F}$-theorem follows for perturbative flows to all loop orders. We show that sources of relevant operators, which on the sphere mix with the curvature, do not affect these results. In unitary theories the only exception is the source of a free scalar field near $d=6,10,\ldots\,$, so that for $φ^3$ theory near six dimensions the gradient property remains open.

</details>


### [151] [An all-orders proof of gradient flow for multiscalar $φ^4$ theory in four dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28206)
*Marten Reehorst*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了四维多标量φ⁴理论的重整化群流在所有微扰阶上都是梯度流，利用Osborn-Jack的Weyl一致性条件，并通过重写反对称项为对称项与势能项，严格构造出正定度规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子场论中重整化群流是否具有梯度结构（即是否存在单调递减的C函数），对理解物理系统的可重整性、普适性和临界行为至关重要；此前仅在低阶被验证，本文旨在推广至全阶微扰理论。

Method: 基于Osborn-Jack的Weyl一致性条件，结合局部耦合生成泛函的有限性假设及大动量下Feynman积分展开的余项有界性；推导关于场旋转流和质量算符源项的二点函数Ward恒等式，将Weyl反常导致的反对称张量部分重新表达为两点函数与三点函数一阶矩，并进一步分解为对称度规与势能贡献。

Result: 严格证明了四维φ⁴理论RG流是全阶微扰下的梯度流；导出的正定度规显式给出；复现并验证了Pannell-Stergiou提出的五阶、六阶场旋转系数间四个约束关系。

Conclusion: 四维多标量φ⁴理论的重整化群流在全阶微扰下具有梯度结构，且对应度规正定；该结果支持C定理在该模型中的成立，并为更广义场论的梯度性质研究提供范式。

Abstract: We prove that the renormalisation group flow of multiscalar $φ^4$ theory in four dimensions is a gradient flow to all orders in perturbation theory. The proof uses the Weyl consistency conditions of Osborn and Jack, and it assumes two properties of perturbation theory: that the generating functional with local couplings is finite (also assumed by Osborn and Jack), and that the known expansion of massive Feynman integrals at large momenta holds with a bound on its remainder at each momentum. The consistency conditions give a gradient formula in which the tensor that multiplies the beta function is allowed to have an antisymmetric part, determined by coefficients of the Weyl anomaly. We derive a Ward identity for the two-point function of the marginal operators at constant couplings away from fixed points, with sources for the currents of field rotations and for the mass operator. It re-expresses one of the anti-symmetric contributions in terms of two-point functions and the first moment of the three-point function. We show that, after this rewrite, each of the resulting terms is equivalent to a symmetric contribution to the metric and a contribution to the potential, and we give both explicitly. The metric is positive definite to all orders in perturbation theory. The proof implies in particular the four relations among five- and six-loop coefficients of the field rotation, which Pannell and Stergiou derived as the conditions for a gradient flow at six loops. We check by explicit computation that these relations indeed hold. A companion paper treats $d=4-\varepsilon$ and other critical dimensions with a Ward identity on the sphere.

</details>


### [152] [Bring Your Own Instanton: Monopole Catalysis from Sourced SU(2) Instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28208)
*Csaba Csáki,Larissa Kiriliuk,Zhihan Liu,Rotem Ovadia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Rubakov-Callan质子衰变催化过程通过新型‘有源SU(2)瞬子’实现，该瞬子由费米子激发、具单位瞬子数且无指数压低效应；作者提出其绕数与费米子零模数相关，并推导出单极子内外费米子关联函数的闭式解，表明该催化是一种红外效应，散射费米子无需进入单极子核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解't Hooft-Polyakov单极子场中Rubakov-Callan催化质子衰变的微观机制，特别是为何该过程不呈现真空BPST瞬子所导致的指数压低速率。

Method: 引入并分析‘有源SU(2)瞬子’这一新型拓扑规范构型，建立其绕数与费米子零模数的关系，推导费米子插入算符在单极子内外的领头阶关联函数闭式表达式。

Result: 证实催化过程由有源瞬子主导，速率无指数压低；发现绕数等于费米子零模数，暗示存在相应指标定理；给出内外费米子关联函数的解析结果；指出催化是红外效应，散射费米子不需进入单极子核心。

Conclusion: Rubakov-Callan催化本质上源于有源瞬子这一新拓扑结构，属红外物理现象，挑战了需高能短距相互作用的传统图像，为单极子唯象学和拓扑场论提供新视角。

Abstract: We show that Rubakov-Callan catalysis of proton decay in the field of a 't Hooft-Polyakov monopole proceeds through "sourced SU(2) instantons", which are novel topological gauge configurations sourced by fermions and carrying unit instanton number. Unlike vacuum BPST instantons, these sourced instantons do not give rise to exponentially suppressed rates. We relate the winding number to the number of fermionic zero modes, suggesting an underlying index theorem for sourced instantons. We also calculate the closed-form expression for the leading-order correlator for fermion insertions both outside and inside the monopole core. Our results indicate that Rubakov-Callan catalysis is an IR effect where the scattering fermions do not reach the monopole core.

</details>


### [153] [Wormholes do not contribute to supersymmetric partition functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28210)
*Jan Boruch,Douglas Stanford*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了连接多个渐近边界的虫洞总会自发破缺至少部分由边界条件保持的对称性；对于费米子对称性，每个对称性轨道的贡献为零，因此虫洞不贡献于超对称配分函数的矩；但超对称配分函数仍可具有非零系综方差，源于保持所有连续对称性的‘非几何虫洞’构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解虫洞在超对称引力与全息对偶中的作用，特别是其对超对称配分函数及其矩的贡献问题。

Method: 基于对称性自发破缺分析、轨道贡献计算及SYK类模型中的非几何虫洞构型研究。

Result: 虫洞不贡献于超对称配分函数的矩；但系综方差可非零，源自非几何虫洞构型。

Conclusion: 虫洞在超对称配分函数矩中的贡献为零，但系综统计性质（如方差）仍可由其他构型主导。

Abstract: We show that wormholes connecting multiple asymptotic boundaries always spontaneously break at least some of the symmetries preserved by their boundary conditions. Their configurations then form nontrivial orbits of the broken symmetry, and for the case of a fermionic symmetry, the contribution of each orbit vanishes. We conclude that wormholes do not contribute to moments of supersymmetric partition functions. This extends results on supersymmetric indices by Iliesiu, Kologlu, and Turiaci. We emphasize that supersymmetric partition functions can still have a nonzero ensemble variance. In SYK-like examples that we analyze, this arises due to the contribution of ``non-geometric wormhole'' configurations that preserve all continuous symmetries.

</details>


### [154] [The chiral $M^{3,2}$ theory: partition functions beyond large-$N$ and quantum holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28594)
*Xuao Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了手性M^{3,2}理论中Bethe真空族的自由能行为，发现其随N^{3/2}增长，并在特定混合Chern-Simons耦合下由重子磁通区分；同时提出了球面配分函数的全阶Airy公式，并通过四导数和单圈修正对全息原理进行了高精度检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究手性M^{3,2}理论中Bethe真空的自由能标度行为及其与全息对偶的关联，验证AdS/CFT在强耦合极限下的精确性。

Method: 结合Bethe ansatz方法、高精度数值计算（三球面配分函数）、't Hooft极限与Cardy极限分析，以及解析推导all-order 1/N展开与Airy公式。

Result: 发现一族Bethe真空自由能按N^{3/2}增长；获得其全阶微扰1/N展开闭式解；确定重子磁通在特定CS耦合下区分真空；提出三球面配分函数的全阶Airy公式；四导数与单圈修正与全息预言高度一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为M^{3,2}理论的全息对偶提供了高精度支持，揭示了重子磁通在真空选择中的关键作用，并确立了Airy结构在强耦合三维超共形场论中的普适性。

Abstract: We identify families of Bethe vacua in the chiral $M^{3,2}$ theory whose individual free energies grow as $N^{3/2}$. High-precision data support closed forms for their all-order perturbative $1/N$ expansions. At a distinguished mixed Chern--Simons coupling, the baryonic magnetic flux distinguishes members of this family of vacua. We also study the three-sphere partition function numerically in the 't Hooft limit and Cardy limit, and propose an all-order Airy formula. The four-derivative and one-loop corrections are precision tests of holography.

</details>


### [155] [Relativistic Hydrodynamics as an Intersection Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28735)
*Nikita Nekrasov,Paul Wiegmann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将理想相对论流体力学表述为辛空间中的交集问题，利用微分形式和辛几何方法重新推导Lichnerowicz–Carter系统，并揭示其守恒律与几何结构的内在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解理想相对论流体力学的基本几何结构，统一描述其动力学方程与守恒律，超越传统张量方法，寻求更本质的辛几何刻画。

Method: 采用辛几何框架，将流体场表示为时空上的0、1、3、4次微分形式；利用时空微分同胚与动量1-形式平移的哈密顿作用定义余迷向子簇，再结合由度规与状态方程确定的拉格朗日子流形，通过二者交集重构流体方程组。

Result: 成功将Lichnerowicz–Carter系统实现为辛空间中余迷向子簇与拉格朗日子流形的交集；自然导出环量与Ertel不变量；阐明能量-动量守恒即本构拉格朗日量的协变性；给出规范流参数的几何意义。

Conclusion: 理想相对论流体力学具有深刻的辛几何本质，其全部动力学与守恒律可统一源于辛结构、对称性及其约束的几何交集，为后续研究非相对论极限、熵层面螺旋度与Calabi–Yau几何关联提供新视角。

Abstract: Ideal relativistic hydrodynamics admits a covariant description as an intersection problem in a symplectic space associated with spacetime. The independent fields are differential forms of degrees 0,1,3,4. Spacetime diffeomorphisms and shifts of the momentum one-form act Hamiltonianly; the zero locus of their moment map is a coisotropic subvariety encoding the transport equations. The spacetime metric and equation of state determine a Lagrangian submanifold, whose intersection with the coisotropic subvariety is precisely the Lichnerowicz--Carter system. We explain the geometric meaning of the canonical flow parameter, derive circulation and Ertel invariants directly from the constraints, and identify energy--momentum conservation with covariance of the constitutive Lagrangian. The nonrelativistic limit and a relation of entropy-level helicity to split Calabi--Yau geometry are briefly indicated.

</details>


### [156] [Massive Particles in Carrollian Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28780)
*Bin Chen,Zezhou Hu,Reiko Liu,Wen-Jie Ma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个三维Carrollian描述来编码四维平直时空中的质量标量场，并给出了明确的体-边界积分变换；该构造揭示了非零质量需要无限多个线性独立的主态，而无体自旋则在这些主态的后代间施加关系，这些关系等价于零模解耦并给出边界场的局域约束；变换保持完整的庞加莱对称性，并将体标量数据映射为满足该约束的边界场；还得到了二点、三点函数及相应的形式算符乘积展开系数；该构造为关联质量散射振幅与Carrollian关联函数以及从边界数据重建体场提供了具体起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大质量粒子如何在边界算符中编码是Carrollian全息理论中的核心问题。

Method: 构建三维Carrollian描述以表征四维平直时空中的质量标量场，并给出显式的体-边界积分变换；分析非零质量对主态数量的要求及无体自旋对后代态的约束关系；证明该约束等价于零模解耦并导出边界场的局域条件；验证积分变换对庞加莱对称性的保持性；计算二点和三点函数及相应OPE系数；强调复动量处的积分及洛伦兹符号下解析延拓的必要性。

Result: 非零质量需无限多个线性独立主态；无体自旋导致后代态间的关系，等价于零模解耦并给出边界场局域约束；积分变换保持庞加莱对称性，将体标量数据映射为满足该约束的边界场；获得二点、三点函数及形式OPE系数；积分在复动量处进行，解析延拓不可或缺。

Conclusion: 该构造为连接质量散射振幅与Carrollian关联函数、以及从边界数据重建体场提供了具体可行的起点。

Abstract: Understanding how massive particles are encoded in boundary operators is a central problem in Carrollian holography. We construct a three-dimensional Carrollian description of a massive scalar in four-dimensional flat spacetime, together with an explicit bulk-to-boundary integral transform. In this construction, nonzero mass requires infinitely many linearly independent primary states, while the absence of bulk spin imposes relations among their descendants. We show that these relations are equivalent to null-state decoupling and give a local constraint on the boundary fields. The integral transform preserves the full Poincaré symmetry and maps bulk scalar data to fields satisfying this constraint. We also obtain two- and three-point functions and the associated formal operator product expansion coefficients. The integral transform evaluates bulk data at complex momenta, making analytic continuation essential in Lorentzian signature. This construction provides a concrete starting point for relating massive scattering amplitudes to Carrollian correlators and for reconstructing bulk fields from boundary data.

</details>


### [157] [Thermodynamics of hard wall model of AdS/QCD at finite chemical potential with Gauss-Bonnet corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28884)
*Dharm Veer Singh,Shobhit Sachan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在五维爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-高斯-博内引力框架下，带反作用的硬壁AdS/QCD模型在有限夸克密度下的禁闭/去禁闭相变，得到了相变线的解析表达式，并分析了高斯-博内耦合对相变温度及禁闭相中ρ介子性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用物质在高温高密条件下的相结构，特别是QCD相变行为，是核物理与粒子物理的重要课题；AdS/QCD作为全息对偶方法，可为非微扰QCD提供有效描述，但需考虑更高曲率修正（如高斯-博内项）及其对相变和谱性质的影响。

Method: 基于五维爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-高斯-博内引力理论构建硬壁AdS/QCD模型，引入夸克物质的背反应；将禁闭相建模为红外截断的热荷AdS时空，去禁闭相为带电高斯-博内黑洞；通过热力学平衡与边界条件确定相变线；在禁闭相中求解矢量场波动方程并结合WKB近似分析ρ介子质量随密度的变化。

Result: 获得了固定夸克化学势和固定夸克数密度下的解析相变线；发现负高斯-博内耦合提升相变温度、扩展禁闭相至更高密度；在零密度下满足因果性限制的耦合值可使相变温度接近格点QCD结果；禁闭相中ρ介子及其径向激发态质量均随密度单调增加，在核密度下ρ质量上升约1%。

Conclusion: 高斯-博内曲率修正显著影响AdS/QCD模型的相结构与介子谱，为连接全息模型与真实QCD热力学及谱性质提供了更灵活且物理上更合理的参数空间；该框架下禁闭相在高密区域仍保持稳定性，且其动力学可通过有效约束尺度变化予以理解。

Abstract: We study the confinement/deconfinement transition of the hard wall model of AdS/QCD at finite quark density in five-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell--Gauss--Bonnet gravity, with the back-reaction of the quark matter on the geometry included. The deconfined phase is a charged Gauss--Bonnet black hole, and the confined phase is thermally charged AdS cut off in the infrared. The infrared value of the gauge field, which Lee, Park and Sin fixed by requiring a consistent quark number, becomes a function of the Gauss--Bonnet coupling. We obtain the transition line in closed form at fixed quark chemical potential and at fixed quark number density. At fixed density the transition passes through a region where the two phases coexist. A negative Gauss--Bonnet coupling raises the transition temperature and extends the confined phase to higher density. A value inside the causality bound gives a transition temperature at zero density close to the lattice value. In the dense confined phase we compute the masses and decay constants of the $ρ$ meson and its radial excitations. All masses increase with density. A WKB analysis relates the increase to a shorter effective size of the confining region, and at nuclear density the $ρ$ mass rises by about one percent.

</details>


### [158] [Finite-Temperature Stabilization of a KKLT Modulus in Three-Dimensional Lattice Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29000)
*Saya Mahmoudi,Furkan Utku Biber,Hyukjung Kim,Heeseung Zoe*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过三维Langevin格点模拟研究KKLT模的宇宙学稳定化，发现空间平均模虽遵循均匀演化，但临界附近长波涨落可导致局域区域越过势垒，表明平均捕获不保证所有区域稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究KKLT模在宇宙学演化中是否能被全局稳定化，特别是考虑空间涨落、热浴演化、耗散和随机效应等非均匀动力学因素的影响。

Method: 采用三维Langevin格点模拟，纳入随时间演化的热浴、耗散项、空间梯度及随机涨落；辅以线性Langevin理论分析与收敛性检验。

Result: 发现临界附近轨迹在不稳定势垒附近滞留约1 e-fold，引发长波涨落的快子放大，驱动部分局域区域越过势垒； Barrier-crossing 分数在窄随机层内与离散化无关。

Conclusion: 空间平均模的捕获不能保证所有空间区域都保留在稳定势阱内，局域涨落可能导致部分区域逃逸，这对KKLT型真空稳定性的宇宙学实现构成挑战。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological stabilization of a KKLT modulus through three-dimensional Langevin lattice simulations incorporating an evolving thermal bath, dissipation, spatial gradients, and stochastic fluctuations. While the spatially averaged modulus closely follows homogeneous evolution away from the capture threshold, near-critical trajectories linger near the unstable potential barrier for approximately one e-fold, allowing long-wavelength fluctuations to undergo tachyonic amplification and drive localized regions across the barrier even as the mean field turns back toward the stabilized vacuum. Linear Langevin theory reproduces the fluctuation growth, while convergence tests identify a narrow stochastic layer in which the barrier-crossing fraction shows no systematic dependence on the discretization at a fixed effective distance from the threshold. These results demonstrate that capture of the spatially averaged modulus does not guarantee that every spatial region remains within the stabilizing barrier.

</details>


### [159] [Exact stress-tensor fluctuations from conformal symmetry and black hole radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29060)
*José Navarro-Salas,Roberto Balbinot,Alessandro Fabbri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用二维共形对称性精确计算了霍金辐射中能动量张量分量的局部涨落，给出了其方差的闭式表达，并指出相对涨落按1/c标度（c为中心荷），从而支持大c下半经典引力近似有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半经典引力理论依赖于能动量张量期望值作为源，其可靠性要求围绕该期望值的涨落足够小；而二维共形对称性可精确确定霍金辐射s波部分的平均能动量，但其涨落行为尚不明确，需系统研究。

Method: 基于二维共形对称性，利用算符乘积展开（OPE）定义能动量张量平方，引入参数α标记点分离正规化方案；通过共形映射到局域惯性坐标系，导出T_{±±}涨落（即方差）的解析表达式。

Result: 得到T_{±±}局部涨落的闭式解：在史瓦西黑洞处，无穷远霍金流相对涨落为22/5c，视界处出射流为-44α/3c、入射流为(238+720α)/5c；当α=-3/10（Möbius协变方案）时，三者统一为22/5c；所有涨落均正比于1/c。

Conclusion: 二维共形场论中能动量涨落严格按1/c压制，因此在中心荷c较大时，半经典引力近似成立且可靠；该结果为黑洞辐射与时空反作用的半经典描述提供了关键涨落判据。

Abstract: Semiclassical gravity is sourced by the expectation value of the stress tensor, and it is reliable only if the fluctuations around this value are small. In two dimensions, conformal symmetry captures the essential physics of Hawking radiation in its s-wave sector and determines the mean value exactly, for any background and any quantum state. We show that it also determines the local fluctuations of the null components $T_{\pm\pm}$. The square of the stress tensor is defined by the operator product expansion, up to a multiple $α$ of $\partial_\pm^2T_{\pm\pm}$ that labels point-splitting prescriptions. Its transformation law under the conformal map to locally inertial coordinates gives the variance in closed form. The relative fluctuation scales as $1/c$, with $c$ the central charge. For the Schwarzschild black hole it is $22/5c$ for the Hawking flux at infinity, that of flat-space thermal radiation. At the horizon it is $-44α/3c$ for the outgoing flux and $(238+720α)/5c$ for the ingoing flux that drives the backreaction. Both reduce to $22/5c$ for $α=-3/10$, which corresponds to the Möbius-covariant prescription. For large $c$ these results support the semiclassical approximation.

</details>


### [160] [The Generalized Second Law from Fluctuation Theorems in a Quantum Reference Frame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29415)
*Feng-Li Lin,Sayid Mondal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了在固定半有限代数上经历幺正驱动和弛豫的黑洞外部的广义第二定律（GSL），通过引入引力荷作为量子钟、构建模交叉积，并结合量子信道涨落定理给出三种互补表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破Wall基于半经典几何和截面代数限制的GSL证明框架，建立更普适、不依赖特定几何假设且适用于固定‘ dressed exterior algebra’的量子化GSL理论。

Method: 利用引力荷作为量子钟构造模交叉积，使密度算符的迹熵复现广义熵；将GSL表述为开放外部系统的量子信道涨落定理，并发展三种动力学假设下的互补形式：分离幺正驱动与弛豫、时间局域描述、两时刻惊奇涨落定理。

Result: 证明了在控制慢钟极限下GSL成立，并量化了有限宽度时钟修正；明确了无幺正性校正项的‘dressed’演化满足GSL的充分条件（即弛豫保持全 dressed 参考态且不混杂不同 dressed-荷 本征子空间），并指出幺正性非GSL成立的必要条件。

Conclusion: GSL可在更基础的量子信息与代数量子场论框架下建立，无需预设半经典时空结构；其本质可归结为相对熵收缩、物理过程第一定律与模功协同作用的量子热力学规律。

Abstract: We prove the generalized second law (GSL) for black-hole exteriors undergoing unitary driving and relaxation on a fixed semifinite algebra. Wall's influential proof assumes semiclassical geometry and restricts fixed parent states to algebras of successive horizon cuts. We instead evolve the state on a fixed dressed exterior algebra. With complete characteristic data, the same semiclassical exterior supports both descriptions, analogous to the Heisenberg and Schrödinger pictures through state--operator duality. Using a gravitational charge as a quantum clock, we construct a modular crossed product with density operators whose trace entropy reproduces generalized entropy up to clock contributions. Our approach parallels thermodynamics in regulated field theory and formulates the GSL through a quantum channel fluctuation theorem (FT) for the open exterior. We develop three complementary formulations under specified dynamical assumptions. Separated unitary driving and relaxation combine modular work, the physical-process first law relating horizon absorption to area gain, and relative-entropy contraction through data processing. A time-local description accommodates simultaneous unitary driving and dissipation. A two-time surprisal FT treats the complete channel, identifies the nonunital correction, and yields a GSL bound for unital dressed evolution without resolving intermediate dynamics. Relaxation that does not mix distinct dressed-charge sectors and preserves the full dressed reference state is unital. Unitality is sufficient for this FT proof but is not necessary for the GSL along a particular evolution. The entropy bounds establish the GSL in a controlled slow-clock limit and quantify finite-width clock corrections.

</details>


### [161] [Symmetry resolved spread complexity for BMN matrix model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29425)
*Dibakar Roychowdhury*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了BMN矩阵模型在SO(6)标量扇区U(1)全局对称性下的对称性分解传播复杂度，分析了带电热场双重态（cTFD）在低温禁闭相和高温去禁闭相中的动力学行为，并揭示其与全息对偶中Lin-Maldacena几何与带电D0-膜黑洞的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子混沌、复杂度演化与全息对偶之间在对称性分解层面的联系，特别是在具有全局U(1)对称性的强耦合规范理论中，如何通过传播复杂度刻画相变与几何结构。

Method: 将时间演化的带电热场双重态按电荷本征值分解为正交子空间；计算各固定电荷扇区的返回振幅、谱矩、Lanczos系数及早期传播复杂度；结合低能有效模型（位移谐振子）与大N连续谱近似（热高斯分布），对比分析两个热力学相；建立与全息背景（LM droplet与带电D0-膜黑洞）的物理对应。

Result: 低温禁闭相：Lanczos对角系数线性漂移、非对角系数呈√n增长且初始跃迁受热抑制；复杂度不等分，存在早时正值性界ΔC(t)≥0；高温去禁闭相：Lanczos系数呈现普适性（常数a_n^{(q)}与√n标度b_n^{(q)}），传播复杂度以二次项主导；全息上分别对应相干弦涨落与黑洞视界附近的耗散型信息扰乱。

Conclusion: 对称性分解的传播复杂度可作为区分不同全息几何相的有效探针，揭示了守恒荷在量子复杂度演化与时空几何之间的关键桥梁作用。

Abstract: We investigate the symmetry-resolved spread complexity of the Berenstein-Maldacena-Nastase (BMN) matrix model under a global $U(1)$ symmetry acting within the transverse $SO(6)$ scalar sector. Decomposing the time-evolved charged thermofield double (cTFD) state into orthogonal fixed-charge sectors, we examine the return amplitude, spectral moments, tridiagonal Lanczos coefficients, and early-time spread complexity across the low-temperature confined ($βμ\gg 1$) and finite-temperature deconfined ($T \gg μ$) phases. In the low-temperature gapped regime, effective sector dynamics map onto decoupled, displaced harmonic oscillators with a Casimir-like zero-point offset, yielding factorized moments, linear drift in diagonal Lanczos coefficients $a_n^{(q)}$, and sub-linear $\sqrt{n}$ off-diagonal growth with thermally suppressed initial hopping. Charge-dependent Boltzmann weights, vacuum shifts, and degeneracies break complexity equipartition, while inter-sector coherence and charge dispersion enforce an early-time positivity bound $ΔC(t) = C_{\text{total}}(t) - \bar{C}(t) \ge 0$. In the \emph{finite}-temperature continuum phase, modeling the planar large-$N$ spectral density as a thermal Gaussian gives constant diagonal coefficients $a_n^{(q)} = \bar{E}_q$, universal square-root scaling $b_n^{(q)} = σ_E \sqrt{n}$, and a quadratic spread launch $\mathcal{C}^{(q)}(t) = σ_E^2(q,T)\,t^2 + \mathcal{O}(t^4)$ governed by the backreacted sector energy variance. Holographically, these regimes distinguish coherent string fluctuations in horizonless Lin-Maldacena (LM) droplet geometries from dissipative scrambling near charged ten-dimensional black D0-brane horizons.

</details>


### [162] [Quadratic-in-spin scattering of compact bodies at fourth post-Minkowskian order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29670)
*Taushif Ahmed,Syed Mehedi Hasan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了四阶后闵可夫斯基近似下，带有自旋的致密天体在任意速度和质量比下的散射角，并包含了自旋诱导的四极矩、辐射反作用以及曲率平方的自旋-潮汐项；通过三圈世界线积分得到了以多对数函数和椭圆积分为闭式表达的结果；红外极点精确抵消，确定了尾部对数项；得到了一个新的后牛顿系数和一般四极矩下的三阶角动量损失；新结果修正了黑洞散射的重求和预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确描述高速、强场下自旋致密双星系统的动力学行为，特别是为引力波建模提供高阶后闵可夫斯基（PM）精度的散射角解析结果，弥补现有理论中自旋-四极矩耦合、辐射反应及高阶尾项的缺失。

Method: 采用世界线有效场论方法，计算三圈世界线积分；系统纳入自旋至二次方、自旋诱导四极矩、辐射反作用及曲率平方型自旋-潮汐相互作用；利用解析积分技术将结果表示为多对数函数与椭圆积分的闭式表达；通过红外发散分析固定尾部对数项。

Result: 获得四阶PM下含自旋平方的散射角闭式解；首次导出对应的一般四极矩情形下的三阶角动量辐射损失；确定一个全新的后牛顿展开系数；红外极点完全抵消，尾对数项被唯一固定；该结果导致黑洞散射角的重求和预测发生可观测偏移。

Conclusion: 本工作显著推进了高精度散射振幅与引力波模板的理论基础，证实了高阶自旋效应与辐射修正在强场动力学中的关键作用，为未来LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA及LISA数据分析提供更可靠的理论输入。

Abstract: We present the fourth-post-Minkowskian scattering angle of aligned spinning compact bodies through quadratic spin at arbitrary velocity and mass ratio. We include spin-induced quadrupoles and radiation reaction, supplemented by curvature-squared spin-tidal terms. Three-loop worldline integrals yield closed forms in polylogarithms and elliptic integrals. Infrared poles cancel exactly, fixing the tail logarithm. We obtain a new post-Newtonian coefficient and third-order angular-momentum loss for general quadrupoles. The new input shifts resummed predictions for black-hole scattering.

</details>


### [163] [A note on Holographic Renormalization for Polarized IKKT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29677)
*Matteo Guglielmetti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了与极化IKKT矩阵模型真空对偶的IIB超引力解的壳上作用量的边界抵消项，通过变分原理和基于渐近运动方程的涨落边界条件实现；在固定D1-膜电荷系综下，超对称鞍点的重正化壳上作用量重现了矩阵模型结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为使IIB超引力解的壳上作用量在AdS/CFT对应中具有良好定义并能正确复现对偶矩阵模型的结果，需构造合适的边界抵消项以处理紫外发散，并适配极化IKKT模型的真空结构。

Method: 基于变分原理构造边界抵消项；采用由渐近运动方程导出的离壳涨落衰减条件；通过将RR二形式势离壳对偶化以实现固定D1-膜电荷的系综。

Result: 成功构造出适配极化IKKT模型真空的边界抵消项；重正化后的壳上作用量在超对称鞍点处精确复现了矩阵模型在固定D1-膜电荷系综下的结果。

Conclusion: 该边界抵消项的构造验证了IIB超引力与极化IKKT矩阵模型之间的全息对应在作用量层面的一致性，尤其在处理电荷约束与超对称真空时具有关键意义。

Abstract: We construct a boundary counterterm for the on-shell action of type IIB supergravity solutions dual to vacua of the polarized IKKT matrix model. The construction is based on the variational principle, with off-shell fluctuations whose fall-off conditions are based on the asymptotic equations of motion. The renormalized on-shell action of the supersymmetric saddles reproduces the matrix- model result once we pass to the ensemble of fixed D1-brane charges, which we implement off-shell by dualizing the RR two-form potential.

</details>


### [164] [Infrared finite scattering theory: Non-relativistic Coulomb scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29691)
*Kartik Prabhu,Shivam Upadhyay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了库仑势下的非相对论量子散射问题，通过Dollard框架发展了一种全阶消除库仑发散的微扰理论，并将其推广到海森堡绘景和LSZ约化形式，为处理长程相互作用和无质量场的红外发散提供了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统S矩阵方法在库仑势下因长程相互作用导致积分发散；Dollard框架虽能构造良定义的散射算符，但缺乏显式的微扰实现；且需推广至量子场论以处理红外发散问题。

Method: 1）在Dollard框架下构建全阶抵消库仑发散的微扰散射算符；2）显式计算至二阶Born近似验证发散相消；3）在海森堡绘景中引入含时正则变换，消除渐近相空间中的对数库仑修正；4）基于新正则变量重构Heisenberg S矩阵及适配的LSZ约化形式。

Result: 1）证明库仑发散在所有微扰阶上严格抵消；2）二阶结果复现已知的精确分布意义下的库仑散射振幅（含前向散射）；3）建立与Dollard散射算符等价的、适配修改后渐近动力学的LSZ形式；4）为QFT中长程/红外问题提供可推广的框架。

Conclusion: 本文不仅完善了非相对论库仑散射的微扰理论基础，而且通过海森堡绘景与正则变换方法，架起了Dollard理论与量子场论LSZ约化之间的桥梁，为系统处理如QED或引力中的红外问题奠定了新范式。

Abstract: We revisit non-relativistic quantum-mechanical scattering in a Coulomb potential. The long-range nature of the interaction leads to divergent integrals in conventional S-matrix calculations using the Born series. Dollard showed that a well-defined scattering operator can be constructed by replacing the free Hamiltonian $ H_0 $ with an appropriate asymptotic Hamiltonian $ H_{\rm D} $. However, explicit perturbative calculations within Dollard's framework have remained scarce. We develop a perturbative formulation of the Dollard scattering operator in which the Coulomb divergences cancel to all orders. Perturbatively, we demonstrate this cancellation explicitly through second Born order and show that the resulting amplitude reproduces the known exact distributional Coulomb amplitude, including its forward-scattering contribution. With the aim of generalizing our analysis to quantum field theory, we reformulate the problem in the Heisenberg picture. We construct a new set of canonical variables through a time-dependent canonical transformation that removes the logarithmic Coulomb correction from the asymptotic phase-space coordinates. Using these variables, we construct the corresponding Heisenberg S-matrix through several complementary approaches. This leads to a formulation of the Lehmann--Symanzik--Zimmermann (LSZ) reduction formalism adapted to the modified asymptotic dynamics, which we relate to the Dollard scattering operator. This construction provides a framework for extending the LSZ formalism to infrared divergences in relativistic quantum field theories with long-range interactions and massless fields.

</details>


### [165] [Bounds on entanglement fluctuations at holographic interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29699)
*Pallab Basu,Vishnu Jejjala*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究全息共形界面中纠缠涨落与熵的关联，证明了有效中心荷、容量和能量传输之间的不等式关系，并指出长Janus喉结构可在有限熵系数下实现无界容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在固定熵条件下纠缠涨落的最小可能值，特别是在全息共形界面背景下。
